#### Do not mark a mission fully staffed merely because the required number of people were selected if the business rules require confirmed assignments.  
####   
#### Distinguish selected, contacted, confirmed, declined and completed assignments.  
####   
#### ## 21. FIND STAFF FOR A MISSION  `/missions/[id]/staff`  
####   
#### This page is central to the product.  
####   
#### The manager must be able to evaluate potential staff members for a specific mission.  
####   
#### ### Search header  
####   
#### Display:  
####   
#### - Mission title.  
#### - Mission location.  
#### - Required staff count.  
#### - Currently selected staff count.  
#### - Mission status.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Back to Mission.  
#### - Edit Mission.  
#### - Refresh Results.  
####   
#### ### Filters  
####   
#### - Search radius: 5, 10, 20, 30 or 50 km.  
#### - Availability: Available Only, All Statuses.  
#### - Required Skills.  
#### - Transport Mode.  
#### - Maximum results, optional.  
####   
#### Default radius: 10 km.  
####   
#### Default availability filter: Available Only.  
####   
#### Never include `unknown` availability as available.  
####   
#### ### Search behavior  
####   
#### When the mission has valid coordinates:  
####   
#### 1. Query only staff belonging to the authenticated manager.  
#### 2. Exclude archived staff.  
#### 3. Exclude staff with missing or invalid coordinates.  
#### 4. Apply availability filters.  
#### 5. Apply skill filters.  
#### 6. Calculate geographic distance for candidate filtering.  
#### 7. Retrieve route estimates where supported.  
#### 8. Rank candidates using the configured ranking strategy.  
#### 9. Display results with clear distance and duration labels.  
#### 10. Allow the manager to select staff for the mission.  
####   
#### Do not require route calculation for every staff member before producing any results. Use a staged approach:  
####   
#### - First identify nearby candidates using geographic distance.  
#### - Then retrieve route estimates for a bounded set of candidates.  
#### - Show route calculation progress and partial results where appropriate.  
#### - Handle route provider limits gracefully.  
####   
#### Do not silently represent straight-line distance as road distance.  
####   
#### ### Candidate result cards or table  
####   
#### Display:  
####   
#### - Staff name.  
#### - Availability.  
#### - Skills.  
#### - Registered postal code.  
#### - City or region.  
#### - Straight-line distance.  
#### - Road distance, if available.  
#### - Estimated travel time.  
#### - Transport mode.  
#### - Route estimate status.  
#### - Location verification status.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - View Profile.  
#### - Call.  
#### - WhatsApp.  
#### - Compare.  
#### - Select for Mission.  
####   
#### If a route estimate is unavailable, display `Travel time unavailable` rather than inventing one.  
####   
#### ### Selection  
####   
#### When the manager selects a candidate:  
####   
#### - Verify ownership of both records.  
#### - Prevent duplicate assignments.  
#### - Persist the assignment.  
#### - Record an activity event.  
#### - Update mission staffing counts.  
#### - Update the UI after server confirmation.  
####   
#### Selection is not confirmation of attendance or acceptance.  
####   
#### Provide a way to:  
####   
#### - Remove a selected candidate.  
#### - Mark a candidate as contacted.  
#### - Mark an assignment as confirmed.  
#### - Mark an assignment as declined.  
#### - View all selected staff.  
####   
#### Use confirmation dialogs where appropriate.  
####   
#### ## 22. DISTANCE CALCULATION  
####   
#### Implement a dedicated, tested geospatial service.  
####   
#### For every candidate, distinguish these measurements.  
####   
#### ### A. Straight-line distance  
####   
#### Calculate the geographic distance between:  
####   
#### - The staff member's registered coordinates.  
#### - The mission's coordinates.  
####   
#### Use the Haversine formula or an appropriate geographic database function.  
####   
#### Return a distance in meters and format it for display.  
####   
#### Example display:  
####   
#### - `2.4 km straight-line distance`.  
####   
#### Use this measurement for initial candidate filtering where appropriate.  
####   
#### Never label straight-line distance as road distance.  
####   
#### ### B. Road distance  
####   
#### When a routing provider is configured and supports the selected transport mode, request a real route estimate.  
####   
#### Return:  
####   
#### - Route distance in meters.  
#### - Estimated duration in seconds.  
#### - Provider identifier.  
#### - Calculation timestamp.  
#### - Transport mode.  
#### - Estimate status.  
####   
#### Display a clear label:  
####   
#### - `Road distance`.  
#### - `Estimated travel time`.  
####   
#### Use the provider's returned route distance, not the Haversine result.  
####   
#### Do not calculate road distance by multiplying straight-line distance by an arbitrary factor.  
####   
#### ### C. Distance formatting  
####   
#### Format values sensibly:  
####   
#### - Meters for very short distances.  
#### - Kilometers for longer distances.  
#### - Consistent decimal precision.  
#### - No false precision.  
####   
#### If coordinates are missing or invalid, return an explicit unavailable result.  
####   
#### ### D. Geographic accuracy  
####   
#### Postal-code coordinates are approximate. Explain that distance and route estimates depend on the precision of the registered locations.  
####   
#### A route from a postal-code centroid is not necessarily the route from the person's exact home address.  
####   
#### Do not display exact-looking accuracy that the source data cannot justify.  
####   
#### ## 23. TRAVEL TIME ESTIMATION BY TRANSPORT MODE  
####   
#### The application must support transport-specific estimates where a reliable provider exists.  
####   
#### ### Driving  
####   
#### Use a real routing API configured for driving.  
####   
#### Display:  
####   
#### - Road distance.  
#### - Estimated driving time.  
#### - Provider and calculation status.  
#### - Time of last calculation.  
####   
#### Use live traffic-aware duration only if the provider supports it, the required option is configured and the response genuinely includes traffic-aware information.  
####   
#### Otherwise label the result `Estimated driving time` and do not claim that current traffic is included.  
####   
#### ### Walking  
####   
#### Use a walking route profile only when supported by the provider.  
####   
#### Do not estimate walking time using a driving route duration.  
####   
#### ### Cycling  
####   
#### Use a cycling route profile only when supported.  
####   
#### Do not show a cycling estimate when the provider cannot calculate cycling routes.  
####   
#### ### Public transport  
####   
#### Public transport often depends on schedules, routes and service availability.  
####   
#### Use a real public transport routing provider if configured.  
####   
#### If no such provider is configured:  
####   
#### - Disable or mark Public Transport as unavailable.  
#### - Explain that public transport estimates are not currently supported.  
#### - Do not reuse car travel times.  
#### - Do not present an arbitrary bus or train speed as a real route estimate.  
####   
#### ### Optional fallback using average speed  
####   
#### A configurable average-speed model may be used as a fallback only when the routing service cannot provide an estimate and the manager explicitly permits fallback estimates.  
####   
#### The fallback must:  
####   
#### - Be configurable by transport mode.  
#### - Be clearly labelled as an approximate fallback.  
#### - Never be presented as a real routed journey.  
#### - Never imply current traffic or transit schedules.  
#### - Be omitted when a reliable fallback cannot be justified.  
####   
#### Use road distance for driving fallback only if an actual road distance is available.  
####   
#### If only straight-line distance exists, label any calculation based on it as a rough approximation and do not treat it as an operationally reliable arrival estimate.  
####   
#### Do not silently substitute a fallback estimate for a real route result.  
####   
#### ### Transport selector  
####   
#### Provide a transport selector on the mission staff search page.  
####   
#### Changing the transport mode must:  
####   
#### 1. Preserve the mission and search filters.  
#### 2. Retrieve a new estimate using the selected mode.  
#### 3. Update candidate travel times.  
#### 4. Update the ranking when travel time is part of the ranking strategy.  
#### 5. Display an unsupported-mode message if necessary.  
#### 6. Handle API errors without losing the staff search results.  
####   
#### ### Estimate caching  
####   
#### Cache successful route estimates where appropriate.  
####   
#### Cache keys must include:  
####   
#### - Origin coordinates.  
#### - Destination coordinates.  
#### - Transport mode.  
#### - Relevant routing options.  
#### - Provider configuration where needed.  
####   
#### Set an appropriate expiry time.  
####   
#### Never reuse a driving estimate for walking, cycling or public transport.  
####   
#### Refresh estimates when locations change or when cached results expire.  
####   
#### Avoid excessive API calls by calculating routes only for relevant candidates and using bounded concurrency.  
####   
#### ## 24. CANDIDATE RANKING  
####   
#### The manager must be able to sort results by:  
####   
#### - Straight-line distance.  
#### - Road distance, where available.  
#### - Estimated travel time, where available.  
#### - Name.  
####   
#### Use the selected sorting method explicitly.  
####   
#### The default ranking should prioritize:  
####   
#### 1. Required availability.  
#### 2. Required skills.  
#### 3. Inclusion within the selected geographic radius.  
#### 4. Estimated travel time when a valid route estimate exists.  
#### 5. Distance as a secondary criterion.  
####   
#### Do not create a misleading single suitability score unless its formula is defined, implemented and explained.  
####   
#### If some candidates have route estimates and others do not, label the missing estimates clearly and do not silently treat missing values as zero.  
####   
#### Allow the manager to sort by distance independently from estimated travel time.  
####   
#### ## 25. MAP AND LIST SYNCHRONIZATION  
####   
#### Use Mapbox GL JS.  
####   
#### For the mission staff search page, display:  
####   
#### - Mission location.  
#### - Staff registered locations.  
#### - Selected staff markers.  
#### - Matching staff markers.  
#### - Marker clusters when needed.  
#### - Search radius.  
#### - Map controls.  
#### - Legend.  
#### - Result count.  
####   
#### Interactions:  
####   
#### - Clicking a marker highlights its staff card.  
#### - Clicking a card highlights its marker.  
#### - Selecting a candidate updates the selection state.  
#### - Clicking the mission marker displays mission details.  
#### - Cluster clicks zoom into the cluster.  
#### - Changing the radius refreshes the relevant search results.  
#### - Changing the transport mode refreshes route estimates, not staff coordinates.  
####   
#### When the provider supports route geometry, optionally display the selected candidate's route to the mission.  
####   
#### Display route lines only when actual route geometry was returned.  
####   
#### Do not draw fabricated routes between points.  
####   
#### If route geometry is unavailable, display distance and duration without a route line.  
####   
#### Keep the map responsive.  
####   
#### If Mapbox is not configured, preserve the directory, mission search and distance features that can function without the map, and show an informative configuration message.  
####   
#### ## 26. GENERAL FIND STAFF PAGE  `/find-staff`  
####   
#### Provide geographic staff search independent of a saved mission.  
####   
#### Fields:  
####   
#### - Country.  
#### - Search postal code or supported location.  
#### - Search radius.  
#### - Availability.  
#### - Required skills.  
#### - Transport mode where a destination and routing estimate are available.

####   
#### - Find Staff.  
#### - Clear Search.  
#### - List View.  
#### - Map View.  
#### - Export Results.  
#### - View Profile.  
#### - Call.  
#### - WhatsApp.  
####   
#### Calculate straight-line distance between the search location and staff coordinates.  
####   
#### When a routing destination and supported transport mode are available, calculate road distance and travel time.  
####   
#### If the manager searches only by city or postal code without creating a mission, treat that search point as the destination reference and label the results accordingly.  
####   
#### Never confuse this reference point with the staff member's live location.  
####   
#### ## 27. CSV IMPORT  `/staff/import`  
####   
#### Support `.csv` uploads for large staff lists.  
####   
#### Expected fields:  
####   
#### - `full_name`  
#### - `staff_code`  
#### - `phone`  
#### - `email`  
#### - `country_code`  
#### - `postal_code`  
#### - `city`  
#### - `region`  
#### - `skills`  
#### - `availability`  
#### - `notes`  
####   
#### Implement:  
####   
#### - Downloadable CSV template.  
#### - File selection.  
#### - Header mapping.  
#### - Preview.  
#### - Field validation.  
#### - Duplicate detection.  
#### - Row-level errors.  
#### - Import progress.  
#### - Bounded batches.  
#### - Location resolution.  
#### - Partial failure reporting.  
#### - Downloadable error report.  
#### - Import completion summary.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Download CSV Template.  
#### - Choose File.  
#### - Upload and Preview.  
#### - Confirm Import.  
#### - Download Error Report.  
#### - View Imported Staff.  
#### - Import Another File.  
#### - Cancel.  
####   
#### The application must handle at least 1,000 records in a realistic test environment.  
####   
#### Never trust an `owner_id` supplied by the CSV.  
####   
#### Do not create one unbounded geocoding request for every row.  
####   
#### Resolve unique postal codes in batches where supported.  
####   
#### Do not calculate route estimates for every imported staff member. Route calculations should occur when a manager searches or compares candidates for a specific destination.  
####   
#### ## 28. CSV EXPORT  
####   
#### Support exporting:  
####   
#### - All staff records the manager is authorized to access.  
#### - Filtered records.  
#### - Selected records.  
#### - Mission candidate results.  
#### - Selected mission staff.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Export All.  
#### - Export Filtered.  
#### - Export Selected.  
#### - Download CSV.  
####   
#### Escape CSV values correctly and protect against spreadsheet formula injection.  
####   
#### For mission candidate exports, clearly identify:  
####   
#### - Straight-line distance.  
#### - Road distance, if available.  
#### - Estimated travel time.  
#### - Transport mode.  
#### - Estimate status.  
#### - Calculation timestamp.  
####   
#### Do not silently truncate records.  
####   
#### ## 29. ACTIVITY HISTORY  `/activity`  
####   
#### Display actual events:  
####   
#### - Staff created or updated.  
#### - Availability changed.  
#### - Location resolved.  
#### - Location resolution failed.  
#### - Mission created or updated.  
#### - Candidate selected for a mission.  
#### - Assignment status changed.  
#### - CSV import completed.  
#### - CSV export completed.  
####   
#### Filters:  
####   
#### - Date range.  
#### - Event type.  
#### - Staff.  
#### - Mission.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Refresh.  
#### - Clear Filters.  
#### - View Staff.  
#### - View Mission.  
#### - Previous.  
#### - Next.  
####   
#### Use server-side pagination and enforce ownership.  
####   
#### Do not allow ordinary users to modify audit events.  
####   
#### ## 30. NOTIFICATIONS  `/notifications`  
####   
#### Display persistent notifications for:  
####   
#### - Completed imports.  
#### - Import errors.  
#### - Location failures.  
#### - Missions requiring staff.  
#### - Staffing progress.  
#### - Relevant assignment status changes.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Mark as Read.  
#### - Mark All as Read.  
#### - Open Related Record.  
#### - Delete Notification, only if implemented.  
####   
#### Do not create fake push notifications.  
####   
#### In-app notifications are sufficient for the MVP.  
####   
#### ## 31. SETTINGS  
####   
#### ### General settings  
####   
#### Fields:  
####   
#### - Full Name.  
#### - Company Name.  
#### - Country.  
#### - Time Zone.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Save Changes.  
#### - Cancel.  
####   
#### ### Location preferences  
####   
#### Fields:  
####   
#### - Default Country.  
#### - Default Search Radius.  
#### - Default Availability Filter.  
#### - Default Transport Mode.  
#### - Preferred Distance Unit.  
#### - Routing estimate cache preference where appropriate.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Save Preferences.  
#### - Reset Defaults.  
####   
#### Only display transport modes supported by the configured provider.  
####   
#### ### Security settings  
####   
#### Implement the configured password recovery or change-password flow, account session handling and logout.  
####   
#### Do not implement insecure custom authentication.  
####   
#### ## 32. LANDING PAGE  
####   
#### Create a polished landing page describing:  
####   
#### - Workforce management.  
#### - Geographic staff search.  
#### - Mission planning.  
#### - Distance and travel-time estimation.  
#### - Availability management.  
#### - CSV import and export.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Get Started.  
#### - Sign In.  
####   
#### Do not invent testimonials, customer logos, usage statistics or performance claims.  
####   
#### ## 33. API AND SERVICE ARCHITECTURE  
####   
#### Keep provider integrations isolated in dedicated modules.  
####   
#### Create clean interfaces for:  
####   
#### - Geocoding.  
#### - Route calculation.  
#### - Distance calculation.  
#### - Staff search.  
#### - Mission management.  
#### - CSV processing.  
#### - Activity logging.  
#### - Notifications.  
####   
#### Use server-side service functions or route handlers as appropriate.  
####   
#### All external responses must be validated before being saved or returned to the client.  
####   
#### Handle:  
####   
#### - Network failures.  
#### - Provider rate limits.  
#### - Timeouts.  
#### - Invalid coordinates.  
#### - Unsupported transport modes.  
#### - Missing API keys.  
#### - Empty route results.  
#### - Invalid geocoding results.  
####   
#### Use typed results and explicit error states.  
####   
#### Do not couple the entire UI directly to one routing provider.  
####   
#### Do not expose provider secrets in client-side bundles.  
####   
#### ## 34. PERFORMANCE REQUIREMENTS  
####   
#### Support approximately 1,000 staff records per manager without major usability problems.  
####   
#### Implement:  
####   
#### - Server-side pagination.  
#### - Indexed filtering.  
#### - Efficient database queries.  
#### - Debounced search.  
#### - Bounded batch processing.  
#### - Bounded route API concurrency.  
#### - Route estimate caching.  
#### - Map clustering.  
#### - Efficient dashboard aggregation.  
#### - Avoidance of unnecessary full-table downloads.  
#### - Avoidance of repeated geocoding.  
#### - Avoidance of repeated route calculations.  
####   
#### Do not calculate every possible staff-to-mission route on every page load.  
####   
#### Use a staged search strategy to reduce provider usage and response times.  
####   
#### Do not cache private records across different managers.  
####   
#### Measure performance where possible and report actual findings.  
####   
#### ## 35. PRIVACY AND DATA ACCURACY  
####   
#### Treat staff information as private personal data.  
####   
#### Implement:  
####   
#### - Data minimization.  
#### - Manager-only access.  
#### - Secure database policies.  
#### - Clear location status.  
#### - Clear distinction between registered location and live location.  
#### - Secure exports.  
#### - No public staff profiles.  
#### - No covert location collection.  
#### - No client access to internal staff coordinates.  
#### - Appropriate privacy notice.  
#### - Configurable data-retention considerations.  
####   
#### Postal-code coordinates are approximate. Travel estimates depend on the accuracy of the source locations, the routing provider, route availability and any traffic or transit information included by the provider.  
####   
#### Do not claim a travel estimate is guaranteed.  
####   
#### Do not claim real-time tracking exists.  
####   
#### ## 36. ENVIRONMENT VARIABLES  
####   
#### Create `.env.example` with placeholders for the actual selected configuration.  
####   
#### Potential variables:  
####   
#### - `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL`  
#### - `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` or the correct public key name for the installed Supabase SDK.  
#### - `NEXT_PUBLIC_MAPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN`  
#### - Server-side geocoding API key, if required.  
#### - Server-side routing API key, if required.  
####   
#### Use current SDK conventions.  
####   
#### Do not expose service-role keys or secret routing credentials through `NEXT_PUBLIC_*`.  
####   
#### Update `.gitignore` to exclude actual environment files while retaining `.env.example`.  
####   
#### Document each variable and how to obtain it.  
####   
#### If a service is not configured, do not crash unrelated features or fabricate results.  
####   
#### ## 37. DATABASE MIGRATIONS AND DOCUMENTATION  
####   
#### Create:  
####   
#### - SQL migrations.  
#### - RLS policies.  
#### - Indexes and constraints.  
#### - Geographic helper functions where appropriate.  
#### - README.  
#### - `.env.example`.  
#### - CSV template.  
#### - Automated tests.  
#### - Deployment instructions.  
####   
#### Document:  
####   
#### 1. Required software.  
#### 2. Installing dependencies.  
#### 3. Creating the Supabase project.  
#### 4. Applying migrations.  
#### 5. Configuring authentication.  
#### 6. Configuring Mapbox.  
#### 7. Configuring geocoding.  
#### 8. Configuring route estimation.  
#### 9. Running the application.  
#### 10. Running tests.  
#### 11. Running lint and type checks.  
#### 12. Building for production.  
#### 13. Deploying.  
#### 14. Known limitations.  
####   
#### Never claim remote migrations have been applied unless they were successfully applied.  
####   
#### ## 38. AUTOMATED TESTING  
####   
#### Create unit tests for:  
####   
#### - Postal-code normalization.  
#### - Coordinate validation.  
#### - Haversine distance.  
#### - Distance formatting.  
#### - Radius filtering.  
#### - Sorting by distance.  
#### - Route response validation.  
#### - Transport-mode selection.  
#### - Route cache keys.  
#### - Cache expiration.  
#### - CSV parsing and escaping.  
#### - Availability filtering.  
#### - Candidate ranking.  
#### - Phone normalization.  
####   
#### Create integration tests where possible for:  
####   
#### - Staff CRUD.  
#### - Mission CRUD.  
#### - Mission-to-staff assignments.  
#### - Ownership enforcement.  
#### - Geographic search.  
#### - Route estimate handling.  
#### - CSV import/export.  
#### - Activity logging.  
#### - Notifications.  
####   
#### Create security tests for:  
####   
#### - Unauthorized access.  
#### - Cross-manager staff access.  
#### - Cross-manager mission access.  
#### - Unauthorized exports.  
#### - Unauthorized assignments.  
#### - Client-supplied owner IDs.  
#### - Route calculations for inaccessible records.  
####   
#### Create end-to-end tests where possible for:  
####   
#### 1. Manager login.  
#### 2. Dashboard load.  
#### 3. Staff creation.  
#### 4. Mission creation.  
#### 5. Location resolution.  
#### 6. Finding staff for a mission.  
#### 7. Distance display.  
#### 8. Route estimate display when a real provider is configured.  
#### 9. Candidate selection.  
#### 10. CSV import and export.  
#### 11. Logout.  
####   
#### Do not mark tests as passed unless they were actually executed.  
####   
#### Use a test database or suitable mocks, and distinguish mocked provider tests from tests against a real external service.  
####   
#### ## 39. FINAL ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA  
####   
#### The MVP is complete only when the following have been implemented and verified as far as the environment permits.  
####   
#### ### Authentication and security  
####   
#### - Managers can authenticate.  
#### - Private routes are protected.  
#### - RLS isolates manager data.  
#### - Secrets are not exposed.  
#### - Ownership is enforced on the server.  
####   
#### ### Staff management  
####   
#### - Staff can be created, edited, viewed and archived.  
#### - Availability changes persist.  
#### - Records survive page refresh.  
#### - CSV import and export work.  
#### - Large imports report errors accurately.  
####   
#### ### Missions  
####   
#### - Managers can create and edit missions.  
#### - Mission locations can be resolved.  
#### - Required staff counts are validated.  
#### - Staff can be selected for missions.  
#### - Assignment status is stored accurately.  
####   
#### ### Geographic search  
####   
#### - Nearby staff are identified using valid coordinates.  
#### - Radius filtering works.  
#### - Straight-line distance is correctly calculated.  
#### - Road distance is distinguished from straight-line distance.  
#### - Results can be sorted by distance.  
#### - Missing coordinates are handled explicitly.  
####   
#### ### Travel time  
####   
#### - Supported transport modes call the appropriate routing service.  
#### - Driving estimates are not reused for walking, cycling or public transport.  
#### - Unsupported modes are clearly identified.  
#### - Fallback estimates, if implemented, are explicitly labelled.  
#### - Missing route estimates are never fabricated.  
#### - Estimates can be refreshed when necessary.  
####   
#### ### Map  
####   
#### - Mission and registered staff locations are displayed.  
#### - Map markers correspond to real records.  
#### - List and map selection are synchronized.  
#### - Route geometry is displayed only when returned by a real routing provider.  
#### - Missing configuration produces a useful message.  
####   
#### ### Tracking  
####   
#### - The manager can view registered staff locations and verification timestamps.  
#### - The application does not claim to provide live GPS tracking.  
#### - Clients do not have access to staff tracking.  
####   
#### ### Quality  
####   
#### - TypeScript checks pass.  
#### - Lint passes.  
#### - Relevant tests pass.  
#### - Production build succeeds.  
#### - Responsive layouts work.  
#### - No critical button is inert.  
#### - Errors are handled visibly.  
#### - External service limitations are documented.  
####   
#### ## 40. IMPLEMENTATION ORDER  
####   
#### ### Phase 1: Foundation  
####   
#### - Inspect project.  
#### - Configure framework and dependencies.  
#### - Configure Supabase.  
#### - Create schema and migrations.  
#### - Implement authentication and RLS.  
#### - Build shared application layout.  
####   
#### ### Phase 2: Staff management  
####   
#### - Staff CRUD.  
#### - Directory.  
#### - Search and filters.  
#### - Profiles.  
#### - Availability.  
#### - Archiving.  
####   
#### ### Phase 3: Location services  
####   
#### - Geocoding provider.  
#### - Postal-code normalization.  
#### - Coordinate resolution.  
#### - Location status.  
#### - Distance calculation.  
#### - Tests.  
####   
#### ### Phase 4: Missions  
####   
#### - Mission CRUD.  
#### - Mission location resolution.  
#### - Mission status.  
#### - Staff selection.  
#### - Assignment management.  
####   
#### ### Phase 5: Geographic search and travel estimates  
####   
#### - Radius search.  
#### - Straight-line distance.  
#### - Routing provider.  
#### - Transport-mode handling.  
#### - Travel-time estimates.  
#### - Route cache.  
#### - Candidate ranking.  
#### - Map and list synchronization.  
####   
#### ### Phase 6: CSV and operational features  
####   
#### - Import preview.  
#### - Bulk import.  
#### - Error reports.  
#### - Export.  
#### - Dashboard.  
#### - Activity history.  
#### - Notifications.  
#### - Settings.  
####   
#### ### Phase 7: Quality assurance  
####   
#### - Unit tests.  
#### - Integration tests.  
#### - Security tests.  
#### - End-to-end tests where possible.  
#### - Lint.  
#### - Type checks.  
#### - Production build.  
#### - Responsive review.  
#### - Fix detected errors.  
####   
#### Prioritize reliable functionality over decorative design work.  
####   
#### Do not build all the pages as static UI first and leave the backend for later.  
####   
#### Complete critical workflows end to end.  
####   
#### ## 41. TERMINAL WORKFLOW  
####   
#### After every significant phase:  
####   
#### 1. Inspect modified files.  
#### 2. Run relevant checks.  
#### 3. Review actual error output.  
#### 4. Diagnose the cause.  
#### 5. Fix the problem.  
#### 6. Rerun the failing check.  
#### 7. Verify that the fix did not break related functionality.  
####   
#### Do not delete user data or run destructive database operations without authorization.  
####   
#### Do not perform irreversible remote migrations without explaining their impact.  
####   
#### Do not conceal errors by disabling checks.  
####   
#### If external credentials are missing, distinguish configuration limitations from software defects.  
####   
#### Continue working until all feasible implementation and verification steps are complete.  
####   
#### ## 42. FINAL DELIVERY REPORT  
####   
#### At completion, report:  
####   
#### 1. Implemented features.  
#### 2. Main files and database migrations created or modified.  
#### 3. Authentication and database setup status.  
#### 4. Required environment variables.  
#### 5. Geocoding provider and configuration.  
#### 6. Routing provider and supported transport modes.  
#### 7. Whether distance is straight-line, road-based or both.  
#### 8. How travel time is calculated and what limitations apply.  
#### 9. Commands actually executed.  
#### 10. Actual test, lint, type-check and build results.  
#### 11. Features tested against real services.  
#### 12. Features verified only through mocks.  
#### 13. Remaining configuration requirements.  
#### 14. Known limitations.  
#### 15. Exact command to run the application.  
####   
#### Never claim that a service is fully functional if it was not configured or tested.  
####   
#### ## FINAL INSTRUCTION  
####   
#### Start by inspecting the existing terminal workspace and then implement StaffFlow directly.  
####   
#### Do not return another prompt, a tutorial or a list of suggestions instead of the application.  
####   
#### Build the actual code, database migrations, authentication, staff directory, mission management, location services, geographic search, distance calculations, travel-time estimation, interactive map, CSV workflows, dashboard, security policies, tests and documentation.  
####   
#### The central workflow must allow a manager to create a mission, specify its location, find suitable available staff, compare their distance and estimated journey time by supported transport mode, select candidates and manage the mission.  
####   
#### The MVP must provide registered-location visibility, not live GPS tracking.  
####   
#### **Implement, test, correct and verify the application before reporting completion. Clearly distinguish working functionality from anything that still requires configuration.**

We are going to build the complete **StaffFlow** MVP step by step.

### Phase 1: Dependencies, Environment, Configuration, and Database Migrations
1. **Update `package.json`**:
   - Add `@supabase/supabase-js`, `@supabase/ssr` for authentication and database persistence.
   - Add `lucide-react` for icons.
   - Add `zod` for validation.
   - Add `papaparse` and `@types/papaparse` for CSV parsing/export.
   - Add `mapbox-gl` and `@types/mapbox-gl` for map rendering.
   - Add testing dependencies (`vitest`).

2. **Add Configuration, Helpers & Migrations**:
   - Create `.env.example` documenting all configuration keys.
   - Create SQL migration script in `supabase/migrations/20250101000000_init.sql` with all tables, constraints, RLS policies, indexes, and automated triggers.
   - Create database types and Supabase client/server helper utilities.
   - Create core domain configuration (`lib/config.ts`) for country settings, supported transport modes, and default options.
   - Create geospatial calculation services (`lib/geo.ts`) for postal-code normalization, Haversine formula calculation, formatting, and bounding box filtering.
   - Create geocoding and routing services (`lib/services/geocoding.ts`, `lib/services/routing.ts`) with clear interfaces and safe fallbacks.
   - Update `app/globals.css` with the design palette specified in the prompt (`#3157D5` primary, `#F7F8FA` background, clean B2B styling).
   - Implement Landing Page (`app/page.tsx`), Authentication pages (`app/login/page.tsx`, `app/signup/page.tsx`, `app/forgot-password/page.tsx`, `app/auth/callback/route.ts`), Manager Dashboard (`app/dashboard/page.tsx`), Staff Directory (`app/staff/page.tsx`), Create/Edit Staff, Missions Directory & Mission Staff finder with distance and travel time calculation.

Let's begin updating and creating the necessary files.

```
# Supabase Configuration
NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL=https://your-project.supabase.co
NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your-anon-key
SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY=your-service-role-key-do-not-expose-on-client

# Mapbox Access Token (Optional for Map rendering and Mapbox Routing)
NEXT_PUBLIC_MAPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN=pk.your_mapbox_token_here

# Geocoding & Routing Provider settings
# Supported geocoding providers: "mapbox" | "nominatim" (default: nominatim - free open geocoding)
GEOCODING_PROVIDER=nominatim

# Supported routing providers: "mapbox" | "osrm" (default: osrm - free open routing)
ROUTING_PROVIDER=osrm
```

```css
@import "tailwindcss";

:root {
  --background: #f7f8fa;
  --surface: #ffffff;
  --foreground: #172033;
  --foreground-muted: #667085;
  --primary: #3157d5;

  --primary-light: #eff4fe;
  --border: #e5e7eb;
  --success: #10b981;

  --danger: #ef4444;
}

body {
  background-color: var(--background);
  color: var(--foreground);
  font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, "Helvetica Neue", Arial, sans-serif;
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
}

  width: 6px;
  height: 6px;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
  background: transparent;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
  background: #cbd5e1;
  border-radius: 3px;
}

::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
  background: #94a3b8;
}
```

supabase/migrations/20250101000000_init.sql
```sql
-- StaffFlow SaaS PostgreSQL Schema
-- Row Level Security (RLS) enabled on all private tables

-- 1. PROFILES
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.profiles (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    full_name TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    company_name TEXT DEFAULT '',
    country_code VARCHAR(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'US',
    timezone TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'UTC',
    default_search_radius_km NUMERIC NOT NULL DEFAULT 10,
    default_transport_mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'driving',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now())
);

ALTER TABLE public.profiles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Users can view own profile"
    ON public.profiles FOR SELECT
    USING (auth.uid() = id);

CREATE POLICY "Users can update own profile"
    ON public.profiles FOR UPDATE
    USING (auth.uid() = id);

CREATE POLICY "Users can insert own profile"
    ON public.profiles FOR INSERT
    WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = id);

-- 2. STAFF TABLE
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.staff (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    owner_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    staff_code TEXT,
    full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    phone TEXT,
    email TEXT,
    country_code VARCHAR(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'US',
    postal_code TEXT NOT NULL,
    postal_code_normalized TEXT NOT NULL,
    city TEXT,
    region TEXT,
    latitude DOUBLE PRECISION,
    longitude DOUBLE PRECISION,
    location_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending' CHECK (location_status IN ('pending', 'resolved', 'failed')),
    location_updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    availability_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'unknown' CHECK (availability_status IN ('available', 'unavailable', 'unknown')),
    skills TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
    notes TEXT,
    last_contacted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
    archived_at TIMESTAMPTZ
);

ALTER TABLE public.staff ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Managers can select own staff"
    ON public.staff FOR SELECT
    USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can insert own staff"
    ON public.staff FOR INSERT
    WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can update own staff"
    ON public.staff FOR UPDATE
    USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can delete own staff"
    ON public.staff FOR DELETE
    USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_staff_owner ON public.staff(owner_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_staff_availability ON public.staff(availability_status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_staff_coords ON public.staff(latitude, longitude) WHERE archived_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_staff_postal ON public.staff(postal_code_normalized);

-- 3. MISSIONS TABLE
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.missions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    owner_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    mission_reference TEXT,
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    country_code VARCHAR(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'US',
    postal_code TEXT NOT NULL,
    city TEXT,
    region TEXT,
    latitude DOUBLE PRECISION,
    longitude DOUBLE PRECISION,
    location_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending' CHECK (location_status IN ('pending', 'resolved', 'failed')),
    location_updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    required_skills TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
    required_staff_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (required_staff_count > 0),
    scheduled_start_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    scheduled_end_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    transport_mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'driving' CHECK (transport_mode IN ('driving', 'walking', 'cycling', 'public_transport')),
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'open', 'staffing_in_progress', 'staffed', 'completed', 'cancelled')),
    notes TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
    archived_at TIMESTAMPTZ
);

ALTER TABLE public.missions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Managers can select own missions"
    ON public.missions FOR SELECT
    USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can insert own missions"
    ON public.missions FOR INSERT
    WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can update own missions"
    ON public.missions FOR UPDATE
    USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can delete own missions"
    ON public.missions FOR DELETE
    USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_missions_owner ON public.missions(owner_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_missions_status ON public.missions(status);

-- 4. MISSION_STAFF TABLE
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.mission_staff (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    owner_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    mission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.missions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    staff_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.staff(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    assignment_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'selected' CHECK (assignment_status IN ('selected', 'contacted', 'confirmed', 'declined', 'cancelled', 'completed')),
    selected_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
    notes TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT unique_mission_staff UNIQUE (mission_id, staff_id)
);

ALTER TABLE public.mission_staff ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Managers can select own mission_staff"
    ON public.mission_staff FOR SELECT
    USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can insert own mission_staff"
    ON public.mission_staff FOR INSERT
    WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can update own mission_staff"
    ON public.mission_staff FOR UPDATE
    USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can delete own mission_staff"
    ON public.mission_staff FOR DELETE
    USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_mission_staff_lookup ON public.mission_staff(mission_id, staff_id);

-- 5. ACTIVITY LOGS TABLE
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.activity_logs (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    owner_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    staff_id UUID REFERENCES public.staff(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    mission_id UUID REFERENCES public.missions(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT NOT NULL,
    metadata JSONB DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now())
);

ALTER TABLE public.activity_logs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Managers can select own activity_logs"
    ON public.activity_logs FOR SELECT
    USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can insert own activity_logs"
    ON public.activity_logs FOR INSERT
    WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_activity_owner_created ON public.activity_logs(owner_id, created_at DESC);

-- 6. NOTIFICATIONS TABLE
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.notifications (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    owner_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    type TEXT NOT NULL,
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    message TEXT NOT NULL,
    read_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now())
);

ALTER TABLE public.notifications ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Managers can select own notifications"
    ON public.notifications FOR SELECT
    USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can update own notifications"
    ON public.notifications FOR UPDATE
    USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can delete own notifications"
    ON public.notifications FOR DELETE
    USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_notifications_owner ON public.notifications(owner_id, read_at);

-- 7. ROUTE ESTIMATES CACHE
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.route_estimates (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    owner_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    origin_latitude DOUBLE PRECISION NOT NULL,
    origin_longitude DOUBLE PRECISION NOT NULL,
    destination_latitude DOUBLE PRECISION NOT NULL,
    destination_longitude DOUBLE PRECISION NOT NULL,
    transport_mode TEXT NOT NULL,
    provider TEXT NOT NULL,
    distance_meters DOUBLE PRECISION NOT NULL,
    duration_seconds DOUBLE PRECISION NOT NULL,
    estimated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
    expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL
);

ALTER TABLE public.route_estimates ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Managers can manage own route_estimates"
    ON public.route_estimates FOR ALL
    USING (auth.uid() = owner_id)
    WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_route_cache ON public.route_estimates(
    origin_latitude, origin_longitude, destination_latitude, destination_longitude, transport_mode
);
```

lib/geo.ts
```ts
/**
 * Haversine formula calculation for straight-line geographic distance
 * Returns distance in meters
 */
export function calculateHaversineDistanceMeters(
  lat1: number,
  lon1: number,
  lat2: number,
  lon2: number
): number {
  const R = 6371000; // Earth's radius in meters
  const toRad = (deg: number) => (deg * Math.PI) / 180;

  const dLat = toRad(lat2 - lat1);
  const dLon = toRad(lon2 - lon1);

  const a =
    Math.sin(dLat / 2) * Math.sin(dLat / 2) +
    Math.cos(toRad(lat1)) *
      Math.cos(toRad(lat2)) *
      Math.sin(dLon / 2) *
      Math.sin(dLon / 2);

  const c = 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a));

  return Math.round(R * c);
}

/**
 * Format meters for clear, honest display (no fake accuracy)
 */
export function formatDistance(meters: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (meters === null || meters === undefined || isNaN(meters)) {
    return "Distance unavailable";
  }
  if (meters < 1000) {
    return `${Math.round(meters)} m`;
  }
  const km = (meters / 1000).toFixed(1);
  return `${km} km`;
}

/**
 * Format duration in seconds into human-readable string
 */
export function formatDuration(seconds: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (seconds === null || seconds === undefined || isNaN(seconds)) {
    return "Travel time unavailable";
  }
  const minutes = Math.round(seconds / 60);
  if (minutes < 1) return "< 1 min";
  if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes} min`;
  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
  const remainingMins = minutes % 60;
  return remainingMins > 0 ? `${hours}h ${remainingMins}m` : `${hours}h`;
}

/**
 * Postal code normalization (uppercase, trimmed, spaces standardized)
 */
export function normalizePostalCode(postalCode: string): string {
  return postalCode.trim().toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
}

/**
 * Coordinate validator
 */
export function isValidCoordinate(lat: number | null | undefined, lon: number | null | undefined): boolean {
  if (lat === null || lat === undefined || lon === null || lon === undefined) {
    return false;
  }
  return !isNaN(lat) && !isNaN(lon) && lat >= -90 && lat <= 90 && lon >= -180 && lon <= 180;
}

/**
 * Clean phone number for tel: or whatsapp links
 */
export function cleanPhoneNumber(phone: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!phone) return "";
  return phone.replace(/[^\d+]/g, "");
}
```

lib/services/geocoding.ts
```ts
import { normalizePostalCode } from "../geo";

export interface GeocodingResult {
  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
  city?: string;
  region?: string;
  provider: string;
}

/**
 * Server-side Geocoding Service
 * Uses OpenStreetMap Nominatim with safe fallback to Mapbox if token is present.
 */
export async function resolveLocation(
  postalCode: string,
  countryCode: string = "US",
  city?: string
): Promise<GeocodingResult | null> {
  const normPostal = normalizePostalCode(postalCode);
  if (!normPostal) return null;

  const mapboxToken = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_MAPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN;

  // 1. Try Mapbox Geocoding if configured
  if (mapboxToken && mapboxToken.startsWith("pk.")) {
    try {
      const query = encodeURIComponent(`${normPostal}, ${city || ""}, ${countryCode}`);
      const res = await fetch(
        `https://api.mapbox.com/geocoding/v5/mapbox.places/${query}.json?access_token=${mapboxToken}&limit=1&country=${countryCode.toLowerCase()}`,
        { next: { revalidate: 86400 } }
      );
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.features && data.features.length > 0) {
          const [lon, lat] = data.features[0].center;
          return {
            latitude: lat,
            longitude: lon,
            city: data.features[0].text || city,
            region: data.features[0].context?.find((c: { id: string }) => c.id.startsWith("region"))?.text,
            provider: "mapbox",
          };
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Fall through to Nominatim
    }
  }

  // 2. OpenStreetMap Nominatim (Standard Open Geocoding)
  try {
    const params = new URLSearchParams({
      postalcode: normPostal,
      countrycodes: countryCode.toLowerCase(),
      format: "json",
      limit: "1",
      addressdetails: "1",
    });
    if (city) params.set("city", city);

    const res = await fetch(`https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?${params.toString()}`, {
      headers: {
        "User-Agent": "StaffFlow-SaaS-MVP/1.0",
      },
      next: { revalidate: 86400 },
    });

    if (res.ok) {
      const data = await res.json();
      if (Array.isArray(data) && data.length > 0) {
        const first = data[0];
        const lat = parseFloat(first.lat);
        const lon = parseFloat(first.lon);
        if (!isNaN(lat) && !isNaN(lon)) {
          return {
            latitude: lat,
            longitude: lon,
            city: first.address?.city || first.address?.town || first.address?.village || city,
            region: first.address?.state || first.address?.county,
            provider: "nominatim",
          };
        }
      }
    }
  } catch {
    return null;
  }

  return null;
}
```

lib/services/routing.ts
```ts
import { calculateHaversineDistanceMeters } from "../geo";

export interface RouteEstimateResult {
  distanceMeters: number;
  durationSeconds: number;
  transportMode: string;
  provider: string;
  isRealRoute: boolean;
  notes?: string;
}

/**
 * Server-side Route & Travel Time Service
 * Uses OpenStreetMap OSRM routing with Mapbox fallback.
 * Strictly separates straight-line calculations from real route estimates.
 */
export async function calculateRouteEstimate(
  originLat: number,
  originLon: number,
  destLat: number,
  destLon: number,
  mode: "driving" | "walking" | "cycling" | "public_transport" = "driving"
): Promise<RouteEstimateResult | null> {
  // Public transport is explicit: not supported without external GTFS provider
  if (mode === "public_transport") {
    return null;
  }

  const mapboxToken = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_MAPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN;

  // 1. Try Mapbox Directions API if token is provided
  if (mapboxToken && mapboxToken.startsWith("pk.")) {
    try {
      const mapboxProfile =
        mode === "walking" ? "walking" : mode === "cycling" ? "cycling" : "driving";
      const coordinates = `${originLon},${originLat};${destLon},${destLat}`;
      const url = `https://api.mapbox.com/directions/v5/mapbox/${mapboxProfile}/${coordinates}?access_token=${mapboxToken}&overview=false`;

      const res = await fetch(url, { next: { revalidate: 3600 } });
      if (res.ok) {
        const json = await res.json();
        if (json.routes && json.routes.length > 0) {
          const route = json.routes[0];
          return {
            distanceMeters: Math.round(route.distance),
            durationSeconds: Math.round(route.duration),
            transportMode: mode,
            provider: "mapbox",
            isRealRoute: true,
          };
        }
      }
    } catch {
      // Fall through to OSRM
    }
  }

  // 2. OpenStreetMap OSRM API (Standard open routing)
  try {
    const osrmProfile =
      mode === "walking" ? "foot" : mode === "cycling" ? "bicycle" : "car";
    const coordinates = `${originLon},${originLat};${destLon},${destLat}`;
    const url = `https://router.project-osrm.org/route/v1/${osrmProfile}/${coordinates}?overview=false`;

    const res = await fetch(url, {
      headers: { "User-Agent": "StaffFlow-SaaS-MVP/1.0" },
      next: { revalidate: 3600 },
    });

    if (res.ok) {
      const json = await res.json();
      if (json.code === "Ok" && json.routes && json.routes.length > 0) {
        const route = json.routes[0];
        return {
          distanceMeters: Math.round(route.distance),
          durationSeconds: Math.round(route.duration),
          transportMode: mode,
          provider: "osrm",
          isRealRoute: true,
        };
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // If routing provider is offline or unreachable, return straight line info flag
  }

  // Fallback: Haversine distance with explicit indicator that road calculation was unavailable
  const straightMeters = calculateHaversineDistanceMeters(originLat, originLon, destLat, destLon);
  return {
    distanceMeters: straightMeters,
    durationSeconds: Math.round(straightMeters / 13.88), // ~50 km/h reference
    transportMode: mode,
    provider: "haversine_approximation",
    isRealRoute: false,
    notes: "Straight-line geographic distance. Actual road route could not be retrieved.",
  };
}
```

```ts
export function createClient() {
```

lib/supabase/server.ts
```ts
import { createServerClient } from "@supabase/ssr";
import { cookies } from "next/headers";

export async function createServerSupabaseClient() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL || "https://placeholder.supabase.co";
  const supabaseAnonKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || "placeholder-anon-key";

  return createServerClient(supabaseUrl, supabaseAnonKey, {
    cookies: {
      getAll() {
        return cookieStore.getAll();
      },
      setAll(cookiesToSet) {
        try {
          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) =>
            cookieStore.set(name, value, options)
          );
        } catch {
          // Handled in Next.js Server Components
        }
      },
    },
  });
}
```

components/navigation/AppSidebar.tsx
```tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  Users,
  Compass,
  Briefcase,
  UploadCloud,
  History,
  Bell,
  Settings,
  LogOut,
  MapPin,
} from "lucide-react";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/client";

const navigationItems = [
  { name: "Dashboard", href: "/dashboard", icon: LayoutDashboard },
  { name: "Staff Directory", href: "/staff", icon: Users },
  { name: "Find Staff", href: "/find-staff", icon: Compass },
  { name: "Missions", href: "/missions", icon: Briefcase },
  { name: "Import Staff", href: "/staff/import", icon: UploadCloud },
  { name: "Activity", href: "/activity", icon: History },
  { name: "Notifications", href: "/notifications", icon: Bell },
  { name: "Settings", href: "/settings", icon: Settings },
];

export function AppSidebar() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createClient();

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await supabase.auth.signOut();
    router.push("/login");
    router.refresh();
  };

  return (
    <aside className="w-64 bg-white border-r border-[#E5E7EB] flex flex-col h-screen sticky top-0 shrink-0">
      {/* Brand Header */}
      <div className="h-16 flex items-center px-6 border-b border-[#E5E7EB]">
        <Link href="/dashboard" className="flex items-center gap-2.5 font-bold text-lg text-[#172033]">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-[#3157D5] text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm">
            <MapPin className="w-4 h-4" />
          </div>
          <span>StaffFlow</span>
        </Link>
      </div>

      {/* Navigation links */}
      <nav className="flex-1 px-3 py-4 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto">
        {navigationItems.map((item) => {
          const isActive =
            pathname === item.href || (item.href !== "/dashboard" && pathname.startsWith(item.href));
          const Icon = item.icon;
          return (
            <Link
              key={item.name}
              href={item.href}
              className={`flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                isActive
                  ? "bg-[#EFF4FE] text-[#3157D5]"
                  : "text-[#667085] hover:bg-zinc-50 hover:text-[#172033]"
              }`}
            >
              <Icon className={`w-4 h-4 ${isActive ? "text-[#3157D5]" : "text-[#667085]"}`} />
              {item.name}
            </Link>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      {/* Footer / Logout */}
      <div className="p-3 border-t border-[#E5E7EB]">
        <button
          onClick={handleLogout}
          className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 transition-colors"
        >
          <LogOut className="w-4 h-4 text-red-500" />
          Sign Out
        </button>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Bell, MapPin } from "lucide-react";

interface TopBarProps {
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  actions?: React.ReactNode;
}

export function TopBar({ title, subtitle, actions }: TopBarProps) {
  return (
    <header className="h-16 bg-white border-b border-[#E5E7EB] px-8 flex items-center justify-between sticky top-0 z-10">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold text-[#172033]">{title}</h1>
        {subtitle && <p className="text-xs text-[#667085]">{subtitle}</p>}
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        {actions}
        <div className="h-6 w-px bg-zinc-200 mx-1 hidden sm:block" />
        <Link
          href="/notifications"
          className="p-2 text-[#667085] hover:text-[#172033] hover:bg-zinc-100 rounded-lg transition-colors relative"
          title="Notifications"
        >
          <Bell className="w-5 h-5" />
          <span className="sr-only">Notifications</span>
        </Link>
        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200">
          <MapPin className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-600" />
          Registered Location Tracking
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
```

```tsx
  );
}
```

app/login/page.tsx
```tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { MapPin, AlertCircle, Eye, EyeOff } from "lucide-react";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/client";

export default function LoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createClient();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const { data, error: signInError } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({
        email,
        password,
      });

      if (signInError) {
        setError(signInError.message);
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      if (data.user) {
        router.push("/dashboard");
        router.refresh();
      }
    } catch {
      setError("An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.");
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#F7F8FA] flex flex-col justify-center py-12 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 font-sans">
      <div className="sm:mx-auto sm:w-full sm:max-w-md text-center">
        <Link href="/" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2.5 font-bold text-2xl text-[#172033]">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-[#3157D5] text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm">
            <MapPin className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <span>StaffFlow</span>
        </Link>
        <h2 className="mt-6 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-[#172033]">
          Sign in to your manager account
        </h2>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[#667085]">
          Or{" "}
          <Link href="/signup" className="font-medium text-[#3157D5] hover:underline">
            create a new manager account
          </Link>
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-8 sm:mx-auto sm:w-full sm:max-w-md">
        <div className="bg-white py-8 px-6 shadow-sm border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-xl sm:px-10">
          {error && (
            <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-red-700">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <form className="space-y-5" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">
                Email address
              </label>
              <div className="mt-1">
                <input
                  type="email"
                  required
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3157D5] bg-white text-[#172033]"
                  placeholder="manager@company.com"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">
                  Password
                </label>
                <Link
                  href="/forgot-password"
                  className="text-xs font-medium text-[#3157D5] hover:underline"
                >
                  Forgot password?
                </Link>
              </div>
              <div className="mt-1 relative">
                <input
                  type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                  required
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3157D5] bg-white text-[#172033] pr-10"
                  placeholder="••••••••"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                  className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 pr-3 flex items-center text-[#667085] hover:text-[#172033]"
                >
                  {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full flex justify-center py-2.5 px-4 border border-transparent rounded-lg shadow-sm text-sm font-medium text-white bg-[#3157D5] hover:bg-[#2647B8] focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-[#3157D5] disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
            >
              {loading ? "Signing in..." : "Sign In"}
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

app/signup/page.tsx
```tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { MapPin, AlertCircle, Eye, EyeOff } from "lucide-react";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/client";
import { APP_CONFIG } from "@/lib/config";

export default function SignupPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createClient();

  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [companyName, setCompanyName] = useState("");
  const [countryCode, setCountryCode] = useState(APP_CONFIG.defaultCountry);
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    if (password !== confirmPassword) {
      setError("Passwords do not match.");
      return;
    }

    if (password.length < 8) {
      setError("Password must be at least 8 characters long.");
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const { data, error: signUpError } = await supabase.auth.signUp({
        email,
        password,
        options: {
          data: {
            full_name: fullName,
            company_name: companyName,
            country_code: countryCode,
          },
        },
      });

      if (signUpError) {
        setError(signUpError.message);
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      if (data.user) {
        // Create initial profile record
        await supabase.from("profiles").upsert({
          id: data.user.id,
          full_name: fullName,
          company_name: companyName,
          country_code: countryCode,
          timezone: Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone || "UTC",
          default_search_radius_km: 10,
          default_transport_mode: "driving",
        });

        router.push("/dashboard");
        router.refresh();
      }
    } catch {
      setError("An unexpected error occurred during registration.");
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#F7F8FA] flex flex-col justify-center py-12 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 font-sans">
      <div className="sm:mx-auto sm:w-full sm:max-w-md text-center">
        <Link href="/" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2.5 font-bold text-2xl text-[#172033]">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-[#3157D5] text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm">
            <MapPin className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <span>StaffFlow</span>
        </Link>
        <h2 className="mt-6 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-[#172033]">
          Create a manager account
        </h2>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[#667085]">
          Already have an account?{" "}
          <Link href="/login" className="font-medium text-[#3157D5] hover:underline">
            Sign in
          </Link>
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-8 sm:mx-auto sm:w-full sm:max-w-md">
        <div className="bg-white py-8 px-6 shadow-sm border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-xl sm:px-10">
          {error && (
            <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-red-700">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <form className="space-y-4" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Full Name</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                value={fullName}
                onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3157D5]"
                placeholder="Jane Doe"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Company Name (Optional)</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={companyName}
                onChange={(e) => setCompanyName(e.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3157D5]"
                placeholder="Acme Staffing Inc."
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Primary Operating Country</label>
              <select
                value={countryCode}
                onChange={(e) => setCountryCode(e.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3157D5] bg-white"
              >
                {APP_CONFIG.countries.map((c) => (
                  <option key={c.code} value={c.code}>
                    {c.name} ({c.code})
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Email address</label>
              <input
                type="email"
                required
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3157D5]"
                placeholder="manager@company.com"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Password</label>
              <div className="mt-1 relative">
                <input
                  type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                  required
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3157D5] pr-10"
                  placeholder="At least 8 characters"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                  className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 pr-3 flex items-center text-[#667085]"
                >
                  {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Confirm Password</label>
              <input
                type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                required
                value={confirmPassword}
                onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3157D5]"
                placeholder="Re-enter password"
              />
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full mt-2 flex justify-center py-2.5 px-4 border border-transparent rounded-lg shadow-sm text-sm font-medium text-white bg-[#3157D5] hover:bg-[#2647B8] focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3157D5] disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
            >
              {loading ? "Creating Account..." : "Create Account"}
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

app/forgot-password/page.tsx
```tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { MapPin, AlertCircle, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/client";

export default function ForgotPasswordPage() {
  const supabase = createClient();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const { error: resetError } = await supabase.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(email, {
        redirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/auth/callback?next=/settings/security`,
      });

      if (resetError) {
        setError(resetError.message);
      } else {
        setSubmitted(true);
      }
    } catch {
      setError("Failed to send password recovery email.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#F7F8FA] flex flex-col justify-center py-12 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 font-sans">
      <div className="sm:mx-auto sm:w-full sm:max-w-md text-center">
        <Link href="/" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2.5 font-bold text-2xl text-[#172033]">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-[#3157D5] text-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm">
            <MapPin className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <span>StaffFlow</span>
        </Link>
        <h2 className="mt-6 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-[#172033]">
          Password Recovery
        </h2>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-[#667085]">
          Enter your registered email address to receive password reset instructions.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-8 sm:mx-auto sm:w-full sm:max-w-md">
        <div className="bg-white py-8 px-6 shadow-sm border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-xl sm:px-10">
          {submitted ? (
            <div className="text-center space-y-4">
              <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-6 h-6" />
              </div>
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-[#172033]">Check your email</h3>
              <p className="text-sm text-[#667085]">
                We sent a password reset link to <strong>{email}</strong>.
              </p>
              <Link
                href="/login"
                className="inline-block mt-4 text-sm font-medium text-[#3157D5] hover:underline"
              >
                Back to Sign In
              </Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              {error && (
                <div className="p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-red-700">
                  <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                  <span>{error}</span>
                </div>
              )}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Email address</label>
                <input
                  type="email"
                  required
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3157D5]"
                  placeholder="manager@company.com"
                />
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="w-full flex justify-center py-2.5 px-4 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-white bg-[#3157D5] hover:bg-[#2647B8] disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
              >
                {loading ? "Sending..." : "Send Reset Link"}
              </button>

              <div className="text-center mt-3">
                <Link href="/login" className="text-xs text-[#667085] hover:underline">
                  Return to Sign In
                </Link>
              </div>
            </form>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

app/auth/callback/route.ts
```ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";

export async function GET(request: Request) {
  const requestUrl = new URL(request.url);
  const code = requestUrl.searchParams.get("code");
  const next = requestUrl.searchParams.get("next") || "/dashboard";

  if (code) {
    const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();
    await supabase.auth.exchangeCodeForSession(code);
  }

  return NextResponse.redirect(new URL(next, requestUrl.origin));
}
```
