app/dashboard/page.tsx
```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import {
  Users,
  Briefcase,
  CheckCircle2,
  XCircle,
  MapPin,
  Clock,
  Plus,
  Compass,
  UploadCloud,
  ArrowRight,
} from "lucide-react";
import { TopBar } from "@/components/navigation/TopBar";
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { Mission, Staff, ActivityLog } from "@/lib/types/database";

export default async function DashboardPage() {
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();

  // 1. Fetch KPI metrics from genuine database records
  const [
    { count: totalStaff },
    { count: availableStaff },
    { count: unavailableStaff },
    { count: verifiedLocationStaff },
    { count: activeMissions },
    { count: missionsNeedingStaff },
    { data: upcomingMissions },
    { data: recentActivity },
  ] = await Promise.all([
    supabase.from("staff").select("*", { count: "exact", head: true }).is("archived_at", null),
    supabase
      .from("staff")
      .select("*", { count: "exact", head: true })
      .is("archived_at", null)
      .eq("availability_status", "available"),
    supabase
      .from("staff")
      .select("*", { count: "exact", head: true })
      .is("archived_at", null)
      .eq("availability_status", "unavailable"),
    supabase
      .from("staff")
      .select("*", { count: "exact", head: true })
      .is("archived_at", null)
      .eq("location_status", "resolved"),
    supabase
      .from("missions")
      .select("*", { count: "exact", head: true })
      .is("archived_at", null)
      .in("status", ["open", "staffing_in_progress"]),
    supabase
      .from("missions")
      .select("*", { count: "exact", head: true })
      .is("archived_at", null)
      .in("status", ["open", "staffing_in_progress"]),
    supabase
      .from("missions")
      .select("*")
      .is("archived_at", null)
      .order("created_at", { ascending: false })
      .limit(5),
    supabase
      .from("activity_logs")
      .select("*")
      .order("created_at", { ascending: false })
      .limit(6),
  ]);

  const missions = (upcomingMissions as Mission[]) || [];
  const activities = (recentActivity as ActivityLog[]) || [];

  return (
    <div>
      <TopBar
        title="Dashboard"
        subtitle="Workforce operations, location status & active missions"
        actions={
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Link
              href="/staff/new"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-white border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:bg-zinc-50 text-[#172033] rounded-lg transition-colors"
            >
              <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              Add Staff
            </Link>
            <Link
              href="/staff/import"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-white border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:bg-zinc-50 text-[#172033] rounded-lg transition-colors"
            >
              <UploadCloud className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              Import CSV
            </Link>
            <Link
              href="/missions/new"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-[#3157D5] hover:bg-[#2647B8] text-white rounded-lg shadow-sm transition-colors"
            >
              <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              New Mission
            </Link>
          </div>
        }
      />

      <main className="p-8 space-y-8">
        {/* KPI Cards Grid */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-4">
          <Link
            href="/staff"
            className="p-5 bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:border-[#3157D5] transition-colors"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-xs font-medium text-[#667085]">Total Staff</span>
              <Users className="w-4 h-4 text-[#667085]" />
            </div>
            <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-[#172033]">{totalStaff ?? 0}</p>
          </Link>

          <Link
            href="/staff?availability=available"
            className="p-5 bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:border-emerald-500 transition-colors"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-xs font-medium text-[#667085]">Available</span>
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600" />
            </div>
            <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-emerald-600">{availableStaff ?? 0}</p>
          </Link>

          <Link
            href="/staff?availability=unavailable"
            className="p-5 bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:border-red-400 transition-colors"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-xs font-medium text-[#667085]">Unavailable</span>
              <XCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-red-500" />
            </div>
            <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-[#172033]">{unavailableStaff ?? 0}</p>
          </Link>

          <Link
            href="/staff?location_status=resolved"
            className="p-5 bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:border-blue-400 transition-colors"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-xs font-medium text-[#667085]">Verified Locations</span>
              <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 text-[#3157D5]" />
            </div>
            <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-[#3157D5]">{verifiedLocationStaff ?? 0}</p>
          </Link>

          <Link
            href="/missions?status=active"
            className="p-5 bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:border-purple-500 transition-colors"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-xs font-medium text-[#667085]">Active Missions</span>
              <Briefcase className="w-4 h-4 text-purple-600" />
            </div>
            <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-[#172033]">{activeMissions ?? 0}</p>
          </Link>

          <Link
            href="/missions?status=needs_staff"
            className="p-5 bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:border-amber-500 transition-colors"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-xs font-medium text-[#667085]">Needs Staffing</span>
              <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500" />
            </div>
            <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-amber-600">{missionsNeedingStaff ?? 0}</p>
          </Link>
        </div>

        {/* Missions & Recent Activity Section */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
          {/* Mission Overview */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-2 bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] p-6">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-5">
              <div>
                <h2 className="font-semibold text-base text-[#172033]">Mission Overview</h2>
                <p className="text-xs text-[#667085]">Upcoming & ongoing assignments</p>
              </div>
              <Link
                href="/missions"
                className="text-xs font-medium text-[#3157D5] hover:underline inline-flex items-center gap-1"
              >
                View all missions <ArrowRight className="w-3 h-3" />
              </Link>
            </div>

            {missions.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center py-12 border border-dashed border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg">
                <Briefcase className="w-8 h-8 text-[#667085] mx-auto mb-2 opacity-50" />
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">No missions created yet</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-[#667085] mt-1">
                  Create your first staffing mission to start assigning nearby candidates.
                </p>
                <Link
                  href="/missions/new"
                  className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-[#3157D5] text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-[#2647B8]"
                >
                  <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  Create Mission
                </Link>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="divide-y divide-[#E5E7EB]">
                {missions.map((m) => (
                  <div key={m.id} className="py-4 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="font-medium text-sm text-[#172033]">{m.title}</span>
                        {m.mission_reference && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] bg-zinc-100 text-[#667085] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-mono">
                            {m.mission_reference}
                          </span>
                        )}
                        <span className="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full capitalize bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 font-medium">
                          {m.status.replace(/_/g, " ")}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mt-1 text-xs text-[#667085]">
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                          <MapPin className="w-3 h-3" />
                          {m.postal_code} {m.city ? `(${m.city})` : ""}
                        </span>
                        <span>Requires {m.required_staff_count} staff</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <Link
                        href={`/missions/${m.id}/staff`}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-[#EFF4FE] text-[#3157D5] hover:bg-blue-100 rounded-lg inline-flex items-center gap-1"
                      >
                        <Compass className="w-3 h-3" />
                        Find Staff
                      </Link>
                      <Link
                        href={`/missions/${m.id}`}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium border border-[#E5E7EB] text-[#172033] hover:bg-zinc-50 rounded-lg"
                      >
                        Details
                      </Link>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Activity Feed */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] p-6">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-5">
              <div>
                <h2 className="font-semibold text-base text-[#172033]">Recent Activity</h2>
                <p className="text-xs text-[#667085]">Audit log of manager actions</p>
              </div>
              <Link
                href="/activity"
                className="text-xs font-medium text-[#3157D5] hover:underline"
              >
                All
              </Link>
            </div>

            {activities.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-xs text-[#667085] py-8 text-center">No recent activity recorded.</p>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                {activities.map((act) => (
                  <div key={act.id} className="text-xs">
                    <p className="font-medium text-[#172033]">{act.description}</p>
                    <p className="text-[#667085] mt-0.5">
                      {new Date(act.created_at).toLocaleDateString()} at{" "}
                      {new Date(act.created_at).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
```

app/staff/page.tsx
```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { Plus, UploadCloud, Download, Search, CheckCircle2, XCircle, HelpCircle, MapPin, Phone } from "lucide-react";
import { TopBar } from "@/components/navigation/TopBar";
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { Staff } from "@/lib/types/database";
import { cleanPhoneNumber } from "@/lib/geo";

export default async function StaffDirectoryPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ [key: string]: string | string[] | undefined }>;
}) {
  const params = await searchParams;
  const q = typeof params.q === "string" ? params.q.trim() : "";
  const availability = typeof params.availability === "string" ? params.availability : "all";
  const locationStatus = typeof params.location_status === "string" ? params.location_status : "all";

  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();

  let query = supabase
    .from("staff")
    .select("*")
    .is("archived_at", null)
    .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

  if (q) {
    query = query.or(
      `full_name.ilike.%${q}%,staff_code.ilike.%${q}%,postal_code.ilike.%${q}%,city.ilike.%${q}%`
    );
  }

  if (availability !== "all") {
    query = query.eq("availability_status", availability);
  }

  if (locationStatus !== "all") {
    query = query.eq("location_status", locationStatus);
  }

  const { data: staffList } = await query;
  const records: Staff[] = staffList || [];

  return (
    <div>
      <TopBar
        title="Staff Directory"
        subtitle={`Managing ${records.length} registered staff members`}
        actions={
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Link
              href="/staff/import"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-white border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:bg-zinc-50 text-[#172033] rounded-lg transition-colors"
            >
              <UploadCloud className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              Import CSV
            </Link>
            <Link
              href="/staff/new"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-[#3157D5] hover:bg-[#2647B8] text-white rounded-lg shadow-sm transition-colors"
            >
              <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              Add Staff
            </Link>
          </div>
        }
      />

      <main className="p-8 space-y-6">
        {/* Filters and Search Bar */}
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-4 items-center justify-between">
          <form method="GET" className="relative w-full sm:w-96">
            <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-[#667085] absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
            <input
              type="text"
              name="q"
              defaultValue={q}
              placeholder="Search by name, code, postal code, city..."
              className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3157D5]"
            />
          </form>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full sm:w-auto">
            <Link
              href="/staff"
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
                availability === "all" ? "bg-[#3157D5] text-white" : "bg-zinc-100 text-[#667085] hover:text-[#172033]"
              }`}
            >
              All
            </Link>
            <Link
              href="/staff?availability=available"
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
                availability === "available"
                  ? "bg-emerald-600 text-white"
                  : "bg-zinc-100 text-[#667085] hover:text-[#172033]"
              }`}
            >
              Available Only
            </Link>
            <Link
              href="/staff?availability=unavailable"
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
                availability === "unavailable"
                  ? "bg-red-600 text-white"
                  : "bg-zinc-100 text-[#667085] hover:text-[#172033]"
              }`}
            >
              Unavailable
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Staff Table */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] overflow-hidden shadow-sm">
          {records.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-16">
              <p className="text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">No staff members found</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-[#667085] mt-1">
                Try adjusting your search filters or create a new staff record.
              </p>
              <Link
                href="/staff/new"
                className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-medium bg-[#3157D5] text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-[#2647B8]"
              >
                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                Add Staff Member
              </Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-sm">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="border-b border-[#E5E7EB] bg-[#F7F8FA] text-xs font-semibold text-[#667085]">
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Name & Code</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Contact</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Registered Location</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Availability</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Skills</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4 text-right">Actions</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#E5E7EB]">
                  {records.map((s) => (
                    <tr key={s.id} className="hover:bg-zinc-50 transition-colors">
                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        <Link href={`/staff/${s.id}`} className="font-medium text-[#172033] hover:text-[#3157D5]">
                          {s.full_name}
                        </Link>
                        {s.staff_code && (
                          <div className="text-xs font-mono text-[#667085]">{s.staff_code}</div>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-xs text-[#667085]">
                        <div>{s.email || "No email"}</div>
                        {s.phone && (
                          <div className="text-[#172033] font-medium flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5">
                            <Phone className="w-3 h-3 text-[#667085]" />
                            {s.phone}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-xs">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[#172033] font-medium">
                          <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#3157D5]" />
                          <span>{s.postal_code}</span>
                          {s.city && <span className="text-[#667085]">({s.city})</span>}
                        </div>
                        <span
                          className={`inline-block mt-0.5 text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded font-medium ${
                            s.location_status === "resolved"
                              ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700"
                              : s.location_status === "failed"
                              ? "bg-red-50 text-red-700"
                              : "bg-amber-50 text-amber-700"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {s.location_status === "resolved"
                            ? "Verified"
                            : s.location_status === "failed"
                            ? "Resolution Failed"
                            : "Pending Resolution"}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        {s.availability_status === "available" ? (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700">
                            <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3" /> Available
                          </span>
                        ) : s.availability_status === "unavailable" ? (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-red-50 text-red-700">
                            <XCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> Unavailable
                          </span>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600">
                            <HelpCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> Unknown
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 max-w-xs">
                          {s.skills && s.skills.length > 0 ? (
                            s.skills.slice(0, 3).map((skill, idx) => (
                              <span
                                key={idx}
                                className="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-700 rounded"
                              >
                                {skill}
                              </span>
                            ))
                          ) : (
                            <span className="text-xs text-[#667085] italic">None listed</span>
                          )}
                          {s.skills && s.skills.length > 3 && (
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-[#667085]">+{s.skills.length - 3}</span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-right">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
                          <Link
                            href={`/staff/${s.id}`}
                            className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-[#3157D5] hover:bg-blue-50 rounded"
                          >
                            View
                          </Link>
                          <Link
                            href={`/staff/${s.id}/edit`}
                            className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-[#667085] hover:text-[#172033] hover:bg-zinc-100 rounded"
                          >
                            Edit
                          </Link>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
```

app/staff/new/page.tsx
```tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowLeft, MapPin, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { TopBar } from "@/components/navigation/TopBar";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/client";
import { APP_CONFIG } from "@/lib/config";
import { resolveLocation } from "@/lib/services/geocoding";
import { normalizePostalCode } from "@/lib/geo";

export default function NewStaffPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createClient();

  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState("");
  const [staffCode, setStaffCode] = useState("");
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [countryCode, setCountryCode] = useState(APP_CONFIG.defaultCountry);
  const [postalCode, setPostalCode] = useState("");
  const [city, setCity] = useState("");
  const [region, setRegion] = useState("");
  const [availability, setAvailability] = useState("available");
  const [skillsInput, setSkillsInput] = useState("");
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState("");

  const [validatingLocation, setValidatingLocation] = useState(false);
  const [resolvedCoords, setResolvedCoords] = useState<{ lat: number; lon: number } | null>(null);
  const [locationError, setLocationError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleValidateLocation = async () => {
    if (!postalCode) {
      setLocationError("Please enter a postal code first.");
      return;
    }
    setLocationError(null);
    setValidatingLocation(true);
    try {
      const res = await resolveLocation(postalCode, countryCode, city);
      if (res) {
        setResolvedCoords({ lat: res.latitude, lon: res.longitude });
        if (res.city && !city) setCity(res.city);
        if (res.region && !region) setRegion(res.region);
      } else {
        setLocationError("Could not resolve coordinates for this postal code. Location will be marked pending.");
        setResolvedCoords(null);
      }
    } catch {
      setLocationError("Geocoding service unavailable.");
    } finally {
      setValidatingLocation(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setFormError(null);
    setSubmitting(true);

    try {
      const {
        data: { user },
      } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

      if (!user) {
        setFormError("User session not found. Please log in.");
        setSubmitting(false);
        return;
      }

      // Pre-resolve location if not validated yet
      let lat = resolvedCoords?.lat || null;
      let lon = resolvedCoords?.lon || null;
      let locStatus = resolvedCoords ? "resolved" : "pending";

      if (!resolvedCoords && postalCode) {
        const geoRes = await resolveLocation(postalCode, countryCode, city);
        if (geoRes) {
          lat = geoRes.latitude;
          lon = geoRes.longitude;
          locStatus = "resolved";
        } else {
          locStatus = "failed";
        }
      }

      const skills = skillsInput
        .split(",")
        .map((s) => s.trim())
        .filter((s) => s.length > 0);

      const normPostal = normalizePostalCode(postalCode);

      const { data: inserted, error: insertError } = await supabase
        .from("staff")
        .insert({
          owner_id: user.id,
          full_name: fullName,
          staff_code: staffCode || null,
          phone: phone || null,
          email: email || null,
          country_code: countryCode,
          postal_code: postalCode,
          postal_code_normalized: normPostal,
          city: city || null,
          region: region || null,
          latitude: lat,
          longitude: lon,
          location_status: locStatus,
          location_updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          availability_status: availability,
          skills,
          notes: notes || null,
        })
        .select()
        .single();

      if (insertError) {
        setFormError(insertError.message);
        setSubmitting(false);
        return;
      }

      // Record activity log
      await supabase.from("activity_logs").insert({
        owner_id: user.id,
        staff_id: inserted.id,
        event_type: "staff_created",
        description: `Created staff member ${fullName}`,
        metadata: { staff_code: staffCode, location_status: locStatus },
      });

      router.push("/staff");
      router.refresh();
    } catch {
      setFormError("Failed to save staff record.");
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <TopBar
        title="Create Staff Member"
        subtitle="Add a new worker to your internal database"
        actions={
          <Link
            href="/staff"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:bg-zinc-50 text-[#172033] rounded-lg transition-colors"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            Back to Directory
          </Link>
        }
      />

      <main className="p-8 max-w-3xl mx-auto">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] p-8 shadow-sm">
          {formError && (
            <div className="mb-6 p-4 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-red-700">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>{formError}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Full Name *</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={fullName}
                  onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3157D5]"
                  placeholder="e.g. John Doe"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Staff Code (Optional)</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={staffCode}
                  onChange={(e) => setStaffCode(e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3157D5] font-mono"
                  placeholder="STF-001"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Phone Number</label>
                <input
                  type="tel"
                  value={phone}
                  onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3157D5]"
                  placeholder="+1 (555) 234-5678"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Email Address</label>
                <input
                  type="email"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3157D5]"
                  placeholder="worker@example.com"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Location Management Section */}
            <div className="p-4 bg-[#F7F8FA] rounded-lg border border-[#E5E7EB] space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#172033] flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 text-[#3157D5]" />
                  Registered Location Details
                </span>
                <span className="text-xs text-[#667085]">Used for route & distance estimates</span>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-[#172033]">Country</label>
                  <select
                    value={countryCode}
                    onChange={(e) => setCountryCode(e.target.value)}
                    className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-1.5 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-xs bg-white"
                  >
                    {APP_CONFIG.countries.map((c) => (
                      <option key={c.code} value={c.code}>
                        {c.name} ({c.code})
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-[#172033]">Postal Code *</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={postalCode}
                    onChange={(e) => setPostalCode(e.target.value)}
                    className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-1.5 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-xs"
                    placeholder="e.g. 94103"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-[#172033]">City</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={city}
                    onChange={(e) => setCity(e.target.value)}
                    className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-1.5 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-xs"
                    placeholder="e.g. San Francisco"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleValidateLocation}
                  disabled={validatingLocation || !postalCode}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-white border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:bg-zinc-100 rounded-lg text-[#172033] disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {validatingLocation ? "Resolving coordinates..." : "Validate Location"}
                </button>

                {resolvedCoords && (
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-emerald-700 font-medium">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    Coordinates resolved ({resolvedCoords.lat.toFixed(4)}, {resolvedCoords.lon.toFixed(4)})
                  </span>
                )}
                {locationError && (
                  <span className="text-xs text-red-600 font-medium">{locationError}</span>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Availability Status</label>
                <select
                  value={availability}
                  onChange={(e) => setAvailability(e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm bg-white"
                >
                  <option value="available">Available</option>
                  <option value="unavailable">Unavailable</option>
                  <option value="unknown">Unknown</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">
                  Skills (comma separated)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={skillsInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setSkillsInput(e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm"
                  placeholder="Forklift, Security, First Aid"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Notes</label>
              <textarea
                rows={3}
                value={notes}
                onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm"
                placeholder="Internal notes about licenses, certifications, preferences..."
              />
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 pt-4 border-t border-[#E5E7EB]">
              <Link
                href="/staff"
                className="px-4 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-[#667085] hover:bg-zinc-50"
              >
                Cancel
              </Link>
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={submitting}
                className="px-5 py-2 bg-[#3157D5] hover:bg-[#2647B8] text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium shadow-sm disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {submitting ? "Saving..." : "Save Staff Member"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
```

app/staff/[id]/page.tsx
```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  Edit,
  Phone,
  Mail,
  MapPin,
  CheckCircle2,
  XCircle,
  HelpCircle,
  Clock,
  Compass,
} from "lucide-react";
import { TopBar } from "@/components/navigation/TopBar";
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { Staff } from "@/lib/types/database";
import { cleanPhoneNumber } from "@/lib/geo";

export default async function StaffDetailPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
}) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();

  const { data: staff } = await supabase
    .from("staff")
    .select("*")
    .eq("id", id)
    .single();

  if (!staff) {
    notFound();
  }

  const record: Staff = staff;
  const rawPhone = cleanPhoneNumber(record.phone);
  const whatsappUrl = rawPhone ? `https://wa.me/${rawPhone.replace(/\+/g, "")}` : null;

  return (
    <div>
      <TopBar
        title={record.full_name}
        subtitle={record.staff_code ? `Code: ${record.staff_code}` : "Staff Profile"}
        actions={
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Link
              href="/staff"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:bg-zinc-50 text-[#172033] rounded-lg transition-colors"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              Directory
            </Link>
            <Link
              href={`/staff/${record.id}/edit`}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-[#3157D5] hover:bg-[#2647B8] text-white rounded-lg shadow-sm transition-colors"
            >
              <Edit className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              Edit Profile
            </Link>
          </div>
        }
      />

      <main className="p-8 max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-6">
        {/* Profile Card */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] p-6 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-6 border-b border-[#E5E7EB]">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#172033]">{record.full_name}</h2>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-1 text-sm text-[#667085]">
                {record.staff_code && <span className="font-mono bg-zinc-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs">{record.staff_code}</span>}
                <span>Registered in {record.country_code}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Quick Contact Buttons */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              {record.phone && (
                <>
                  <a
                    href={`tel:${record.phone}`}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium bg-[#3157D5] text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-[#2647B8]"
                  >
                    <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    Call
                  </a>
                  {whatsappUrl && (
                    <a
                      href={whatsappUrl}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noopener noreferrer"
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-700"
                    >
                      WhatsApp
                    </a>
                  )}
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-6 pt-6">
            <div>
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#667085] uppercase tracking-wider">Availability</span>
              <div className="mt-2">
                {record.availability_status === "available" ? (
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Available for missions
                  </span>
                ) : record.availability_status === "unavailable" ? (
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-red-50 text-red-700">
                    <XCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Currently Unavailable
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600">
                    <HelpCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Unknown
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#667085] uppercase tracking-wider">Registered Location</span>
              <div className="mt-2 text-sm text-[#172033]">
                <p className="font-medium flex items-center gap-1">
                  <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 text-[#3157D5]" />
                  {record.postal_code} {record.city ? `, ${record.city}` : ""}
                </p>
                <p className="text-xs text-[#667085] mt-1">
                  Status:{" "}
                  <strong className="text-[#172033] capitalize">{record.location_status}</strong>
                  {record.latitude && record.longitude && ` (${record.latitude.toFixed(4)}, ${record.longitude.toFixed(4)})`}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#667085] uppercase tracking-wider">Contact Info</span>
              <div className="mt-2 text-xs space-y-1 text-[#172033]">
                <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#667085]" />
                  {record.phone || "No phone provided"}
                </p>
                <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Mail className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#667085]" />
                  {record.email || "No email provided"}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Skills & Notes */}
          <div className="mt-8 pt-6 border-t border-[#E5E7EB] space-y-4">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#667085] uppercase tracking-wider">Skills & Badges</h3>
              <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {record.skills && record.skills.length > 0 ? (
                  record.skills.map((s, idx) => (
                    <span key={idx} className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-zinc-100 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-800 rounded-md">
                      {s}
                    </span>
                  ))
                ) : (
                  <p className="text-xs text-[#667085] italic">No skills listed for this staff member.</p>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {record.notes && (
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#667085] uppercase tracking-wider">Internal Notes</h3>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#172033] bg-[#F7F8FA] p-3 rounded-lg border border-[#E5E7EB]">
                  {record.notes}
                </p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
```

app/staff/[id]/edit/page.tsx
```tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowLeft, MapPin, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { TopBar } from "@/components/navigation/TopBar";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/client";
import { APP_CONFIG } from "@/lib/config";
import { resolveLocation } from "@/lib/services/geocoding";
import { normalizePostalCode } from "@/lib/geo";

export default function EditStaffPage() {
  const params = useParams();
  const staffId = params.id as string;
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createClient();

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState("");
  const [staffCode, setStaffCode] = useState("");
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [countryCode, setCountryCode] = useState("US");
  const [postalCode, setPostalCode] = useState("");
  const [city, setCity] = useState("");
  const [region, setRegion] = useState("");
  const [availability, setAvailability] = useState("available");
  const [skillsInput, setSkillsInput] = useState("");
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState("");

  const [validatingLocation, setValidatingLocation] = useState(false);
  const [resolvedCoords, setResolvedCoords] = useState<{ lat: number; lon: number } | null>(null);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadStaff() {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("staff").select("*").eq("id", staffId).single();
      if (!error && data) {
        setFullName(data.full_name);
        setStaffCode(data.staff_code || "");
        setPhone(data.phone || "");
        setEmail(data.email || "");
        setCountryCode(data.country_code);
        setPostalCode(data.postal_code);
        setCity(data.city || "");
        setRegion(data.region || "");
        setAvailability(data.availability_status);
        setSkillsInput((data.skills || []).join(", "));
        setNotes(data.notes || "");
        if (data.latitude && data.longitude) {
          setResolvedCoords({ lat: data.latitude, lon: data.longitude });
        }
      }
      setLoading(false);
    }
    loadStaff();
  }, [staffId, supabase]);

  const handleValidateLocation = async () => {
    if (!postalCode) return;
    setValidatingLocation(true);
    const res = await resolveLocation(postalCode, countryCode, city);
    if (res) {
      setResolvedCoords({ lat: res.latitude, lon: res.longitude });
      if (res.city && !city) setCity(res.city);
    }
    setValidatingLocation(false);
  };

  const handleUpdate = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSubmitting(true);
    setFormError(null);

    try {
      const skills = skillsInput
        .split(",")
        .map((s) => s.trim())
        .filter((s) => s.length > 0);

      const normPostal = normalizePostalCode(postalCode);

      const { error } = await supabase
        .from("staff")
        .update({
          full_name: fullName,
          staff_code: staffCode || null,
          phone: phone || null,
          email: email || null,
          country_code: countryCode,
          postal_code: postalCode,
          postal_code_normalized: normPostal,
          city: city || null,
          region: region || null,
          latitude: resolvedCoords?.lat || null,
          longitude: resolvedCoords?.lon || null,
          location_status: resolvedCoords ? "resolved" : "pending",
          location_updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          availability_status: availability,
          skills,
          notes: notes || null,
          updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        })
        .eq("id", staffId);

      if (error) {
        setFormError(error.message);
        setSubmitting(false);
        return;
      }

      router.push(`/staff/${staffId}`);
      router.refresh();
    } catch {
      setFormError("Failed to update staff record.");
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="p-12 text-center text-sm text-[#667085]">Loading staff record...</div>;
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <TopBar
        title="Edit Staff Member"
        subtitle={fullName}
        actions={
          <Link
            href={`/staff/${staffId}`}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:bg-zinc-50 text-[#172033] rounded-lg transition-colors"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            Cancel
          </Link>
        }
      />

      <main className="p-8 max-w-3xl mx-auto">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] p-8 shadow-sm">
          {formError && (
            <div className="mb-6 p-4 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-red-700">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>{formError}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <form onSubmit={handleUpdate} className="space-y-6">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Full Name *</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={fullName}
                  onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Staff Code</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={staffCode}
                  onChange={(e) => setStaffCode(e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm font-mono"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Phone</label>
                <input
                  type="tel"
                  value={phone}
                  onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Email</label>
                <input
                  type="email"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="p-4 bg-[#F7F8FA] rounded-lg border border-[#E5E7EB] space-y-3">
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#172033] flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#3157D5]" />
                Registered Location
              </span>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-[#172033]">Country</label>
                  <select
                    value={countryCode}
                    onChange={(e) => setCountryCode(e.target.value)}
                    className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-1.5 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-xs bg-white"
                  >
                    {APP_CONFIG.countries.map((c) => (
                      <option key={c.code} value={c.code}>
                        {c.name}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-[#172033]">Postal Code</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={postalCode}
                    onChange={(e) => setPostalCode(e.target.value)}
                    className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-1.5 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-xs"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-[#172033]">City</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={city}
                    onChange={(e) => setCity(e.target.value)}
                    className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-1.5 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-xs"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-1">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleValidateLocation}
                  disabled={validatingLocation}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-white border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:bg-zinc-100 rounded-lg text-[#172033]"
                >
                  {validatingLocation ? "Revalidating..." : "Revalidate Location"}
                </button>
                {resolvedCoords && (
                  <span className="text-xs text-emerald-700 font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Resolved
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Availability Status</label>
                <select
                  value={availability}
                  onChange={(e) => setAvailability(e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm bg-white"
                >
                  <option value="available">Available</option>
                  <option value="unavailable">Unavailable</option>
                  <option value="unknown">Unknown</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Skills</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={skillsInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setSkillsInput(e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Notes</label>
              <textarea
                rows={3}
                value={notes}
                onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 pt-4 border-t border-[#E5E7EB]">
              <Link
                href={`/staff/${staffId}`}
                className="px-4 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-[#667085] hover:bg-zinc-50"
              >
                Cancel
              </Link>
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={submitting}
                className="px-5 py-2 bg-[#3157D5] hover:bg-[#2647B8] text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium shadow-sm disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {submitting ? "Saving..." : "Save Changes"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
```

app/staff/import/page.tsx
```tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import Papa from "papaparse";
import { UploadCloud, FileText, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle, ArrowLeft, Download } from "lucide-react";
import { TopBar } from "@/components/navigation/TopBar";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/client";
import { resolveLocation } from "@/lib/services/geocoding";
import { normalizePostalCode } from "@/lib/geo";

interface ParsedRow {
  full_name: string;
  staff_code?: string;
  phone?: string;
  email?: string;
  country_code?: string;
  postal_code: string;
  city?: string;
  region?: string;
  skills?: string;
  availability?: string;
  notes?: string;
}

export default function ImportStaffPage() {
  const supabase = createClient();
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [previewRows, setPreviewRows] = useState<ParsedRow[]>([]);
  const [importing, setImporting] = useState(false);
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState<{ current: number; total: number } | null>(null);
  const [resultSummary, setResultSummary] = useState<{ success: number; failed: number } | null>(null);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const downloadTemplate = () => {
    const csvContent =
      "full_name,staff_code,phone,email,country_code,postal_code,city,region,skills,availability,notes\n" +
      "Alice Smith,STF-101,+1555123456,alice@example.com,US,94103,San Francisco,CA,Forklift;Logistics,available,Experienced worker\n" +
      "Bob Jones,STF-102,+1555987654,bob@example.com,US,94107,San Francisco,CA,Security;Driver,available,Weekend preference\n";

    const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8;" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement("a");
    link.setAttribute("href", url);
    link.setAttribute("download", "staffflow_staff_template.csv");
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
  };

  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.target.files && e.target.files[0]) {
      const selected = e.target.files[0];
      setFile(selected);
      setErrorMsg(null);
      setResultSummary(null);

      Papa.parse<ParsedRow>(selected, {
        header: true,
        skipEmptyLines: true,
        complete: (results) => {
          if (results.data && results.data.length > 0) {
            setPreviewRows(results.data);
          } else {
            setErrorMsg("No valid records found in CSV file.");
          }
        },
        error: (err) => {
          setErrorMsg(`Failed to parse CSV: ${err.message}`);
        },
      });
    }
  };

  const handleImport = async () => {
    if (previewRows.length === 0) return;
    setImporting(true);
    setErrorMsg(null);

    const {
      data: { user },
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (!user) {
      setErrorMsg("Session missing. Please log in.");
      setImporting(false);
      return;
    }

    let successCount = 0;
    let failedCount = 0;
    const total = previewRows.length;
    setProgress({ current: 0, total });

    // Process rows sequentially to avoid geocoding flood/rate limit
    for (let i = 0; i < total; i++) {
      const row = previewRows[i];
      if (!row.full_name || !row.postal_code) {
        failedCount++;
        setProgress({ current: i + 1, total });
        continue;
      }

      const country = row.country_code ? row.country_code.toUpperCase().trim() : "US";
      const normPostal = normalizePostalCode(row.postal_code);

      // Attempt to resolve location coordinates
      let lat: number | null = null;
      let lon: number | null = null;
      let locStatus = "pending";

      try {
        const geo = await resolveLocation(normPostal, country, row.city);
        if (geo) {
          lat = geo.latitude;
          lon = geo.longitude;
          locStatus = "resolved";
        } else {
          locStatus = "failed";
        }
      } catch {
        locStatus = "failed";
      }

      const skills = row.skills
        ? row.skills.split(/[;,]/).map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)
        : [];

      const availability =
        row.availability === "available" || row.availability === "unavailable"
          ? row.availability
          : "unknown";

      const { error } = await supabase.from("staff").insert({
        owner_id: user.id,
        full_name: row.full_name.trim(),
        staff_code: row.staff_code?.trim() || null,
        phone: row.phone?.trim() || null,
        email: row.email?.trim() || null,
        country_code: country,
        postal_code: row.postal_code.trim(),
        postal_code_normalized: normPostal,
        city: row.city?.trim() || null,
        region: row.region?.trim() || null,
        latitude: lat,
        longitude: lon,
        location_status: locStatus,
        location_updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
        availability_status: availability,
        skills,
        notes: row.notes?.trim() || null,
      });

      if (error) {
        failedCount++;
      } else {
        successCount++;
      }

      setProgress({ current: i + 1, total });
    }

    // Log completion activity
    await supabase.from("activity_logs").insert({
      owner_id: user.id,
      event_type: "staff_imported",
      description: `Imported ${successCount} staff records via CSV`,
      metadata: { total, success: successCount, failed: failedCount },
    });

    setResultSummary({ success: successCount, failed: failedCount });
    setImporting(false);
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <TopBar
        title="Import Staff CSV"
        subtitle="Batch upload workers and resolve registered postal codes"
        actions={
          <Link
            href="/staff"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:bg-zinc-50 text-[#172033] rounded-lg transition-colors"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            Staff Directory
          </Link>
        }
      />

      <main className="p-8 max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-6">
        {/* Template & Upload Section */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] p-6 shadow-sm flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-[#172033]">Need the CSV format?</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-[#667085] mt-0.5">
              Download our template with required postal_code and full_name headers.
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={downloadTemplate}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-medium bg-zinc-100 text-[#172033] hover:bg-zinc-200 rounded-lg transition-colors"
          >
            <Download className="w-4 h-4 text-[#667085]" />
            Download CSV Template
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Upload Box */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] p-8 shadow-sm">
          {errorMsg && (
            <div className="mb-6 p-4 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-red-700">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>{errorMsg}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {resultSummary && (
            <div className="mb-6 p-4 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-emerald-800 font-semibold text-sm">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                Import Complete
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-emerald-700">
                Successfully imported <strong>{resultSummary.success}</strong> records.{" "}
                {resultSummary.failed > 0 && (
                  <span>({resultSummary.failed} records had invalid or duplicate data).</span>
                )}
              </p>
              <div className="pt-2">
                <Link
                  href="/staff"
                  className="inline-block px-3 py-1.5 bg-emerald-600 text-white text-xs font-medium rounded-lg hover:bg-emerald-700"
                >
                  View Imported Staff in Directory
                </Link>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="border-2 border-dashed border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-xl p-8 text-center hover:border-[#3157D5] transition-colors">
            <UploadCloud className="w-10 h-10 text-[#667085] mx-auto mb-3" />
            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">
              {file ? file.name : "Select a CSV file from your computer"}
            </p>
            <p className="text-xs text-[#667085] mt-1">Supports files with up to 1,000+ rows</p>
            <input
              type="file"
              accept=".csv"
              onChange={handleFileChange}
              className="mt-4 text-xs text-[#667085] file:mr-4 file:py-2 file:px-4 file:rounded-lg file:border-0 file:text-xs file:font-semibold file:bg-[#3157D5] file:text-white hover:file:bg-[#2647B8] cursor-pointer"
            />
          </div>

          {previewRows.length > 0 && !resultSummary && (
            <div className="mt-8 space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#172033]">
                  Preview ({previewRows.length} records ready to import)
                </h3>
                <button
                  onClick={handleImport}
                  disabled={importing}
                  className="px-5 py-2 bg-[#3157D5] hover:bg-[#2647B8] text-white text-xs font-medium rounded-lg disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {importing
                    ? `Importing (${progress?.current}/${progress?.total})...`
                    : "Confirm & Import Records"}
                </button>
              </div>

              {importing && progress && (
                <div className="w-full bg-zinc-200 rounded-full h-2.5">
                  <div
                    className="bg-[#3157D5] h-2.5 rounded-full transition-all duration-200"
                    style={{ width: `${(progress.current / progress.total) * 100}%` }}
                  />
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="max-h-60 overflow-y-auto border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg">
                <table className="w-full text-xs text-left">
                  <thead className="bg-[#F7F8FA] border-b border-[#E5E7EB] text-[#667085]">
                    <tr>
                      <th className="p-2.5">Name</th>
                      <th className="p-2.5">Postal Code</th>
                      <th className="p-2.5">City</th>
                      <th className="p-2.5">Availability</th>
                      <th className="p-2.5">Skills</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#E5E7EB]">
                    {previewRows.slice(0, 10).map((r, i) => (
                      <tr key={i} className="hover:bg-zinc-50">
                        <td className="p-2.5 font-medium text-[#172033]">{r.full_name}</td>
                        <td className="p-2.5 font-mono">{r.postal_code}</td>
                        <td className="p-2.5">{r.city || "-"}</td>
                        <td className="p-2.5">{r.availability || "unknown"}</td>
                        <td className="p-2.5">{r.skills || "-"}</td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
              {previewRows.length > 10 && (
                <p className="text-xs text-[#667085] text-right">
                  + {previewRows.length - 10} more rows not shown in preview
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
```

app/missions/page.tsx
```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { Plus, Briefcase, MapPin, Compass, Users } from "lucide-react";
import { TopBar } from "@/components/navigation/TopBar";
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { Mission } from "@/lib/types/database";

export default async function MissionsDirectoryPage() {
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();

  const { data: missions } = await supabase
    .from("missions")
    .select("*")
    .is("archived_at", null)
    .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

  const records: Mission[] = missions || [];

  return (
    <div>
      <TopBar
        title="Missions"
        subtitle={`Managing ${records.length} operational assignments`}
        actions={
          <Link
            href="/missions/new"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-[#3157D5] hover:bg-[#2647B8] text-white rounded-lg shadow-sm transition-colors"
          >
            <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            New Mission
          </Link>
        }
      />

      <main className="p-8 space-y-6">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] overflow-hidden shadow-sm">
          {records.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-16">
              <Briefcase className="w-10 h-10 text-[#667085] mx-auto mb-2 opacity-50" />
              <p className="text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">No missions created yet</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-[#667085] mt-1">
                Create a mission with a destination postal code to match and route nearby staff.
              </p>
              <Link
                href="/missions/new"
                className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-medium bg-[#3157D5] text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-[#2647B8]"
              >
                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                Create Mission
              </Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-sm">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="border-b border-[#E5E7EB] bg-[#F7F8FA] text-xs font-semibold text-[#667085]">
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Title & Reference</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Mission Location</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Staff Needed</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Status</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4">Mode</th>
                    <th className="py-3 px-4 text-right">Actions</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#E5E7EB]">
                  {records.map((m) => (
                    <tr key={m.id} className="hover:bg-zinc-50 transition-colors">
                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        <Link href={`/missions/${m.id}`} className="font-medium text-[#172033] hover:text-[#3157D5]">
                          {m.title}
                        </Link>
                        {m.mission_reference && (
                          <div className="text-xs font-mono text-[#667085]">{m.mission_reference}</div>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-xs">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[#172033] font-medium">
                          <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#3157D5]" />
                          <span>{m.postal_code}</span>
                          {m.city && <span className="text-[#667085]">({m.city})</span>}
                        </div>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-[#667085]">
                          {m.location_status === "resolved" ? "Coordinates verified" : "Pending resolution"}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-xs text-[#172033]">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 font-medium">
                          <Users className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#667085]" />
                          {m.required_staff_count} staff
                        </div>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium capitalize bg-blue-50 text-blue-700">
                          {m.status.replace(/_/g, " ")}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-xs capitalize text-[#667085]">
                        {m.transport_mode}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-right">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
                          <Link
                            href={`/missions/${m.id}/staff`}
                            className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium bg-[#EFF4FE] text-[#3157D5] hover:bg-blue-100 rounded inline-flex items-center gap-1"
                          >
                            <Compass className="w-3 h-3" />
                            Find Staff
                          </Link>
                          <Link
                            href={`/missions/${m.id}`}
                            className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-[#667085] hover:text-[#172033] hover:bg-zinc-100 rounded"
                          >
                            Details
                          </Link>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
```
