app/missions/new/page.tsx
```tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ArrowLeft, MapPin, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { TopBar } from "@/components/navigation/TopBar";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/client";
import { APP_CONFIG, TransportMode } from "@/lib/config";
import { resolveLocation } from "@/lib/services/geocoding";
import { normalizePostalCode } from "@/lib/geo";

export default function NewMissionPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const supabase = createClient();

  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [reference, setReference] = useState("");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState("");
  const [requiredCount, setRequiredCount] = useState(1);
  const [skillsInput, setSkillsInput] = useState("");
  const [transportMode, setTransportMode] = useState<TransportMode>("driving");

  const [countryCode, setCountryCode] = useState(APP_CONFIG.defaultCountry);
  const [postalCode, setPostalCode] = useState("");
  const [city, setCity] = useState("");
  const [region, setRegion] = useState("");

  const [validatingLocation, setValidatingLocation] = useState(false);
  const [resolvedCoords, setResolvedCoords] = useState<{ lat: number; lon: number } | null>(null);
  const [locationError, setLocationError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleValidateLocation = async () => {
    if (!postalCode) {
      setLocationError("Please enter a postal code.");
      return;
    }
    setLocationError(null);
    setValidatingLocation(true);
    const res = await resolveLocation(postalCode, countryCode, city);
    if (res) {
      setResolvedCoords({ lat: res.latitude, lon: res.longitude });
      if (res.city && !city) setCity(res.city);
      if (res.region && !region) setRegion(res.region);
    } else {
      setLocationError("Could not resolve coordinates for this postal code.");
      setResolvedCoords(null);
    }
    setValidatingLocation(false);
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setFormError(null);
    setSubmitting(true);

    try {
      const {
        data: { user },
      } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

      if (!user) {
        setFormError("User session not found.");
        setSubmitting(false);
        return;
      }

      let lat = resolvedCoords?.lat || null;
      let lon = resolvedCoords?.lon || null;
      let locStatus = resolvedCoords ? "resolved" : "pending";

      if (!resolvedCoords && postalCode) {
        const geoRes = await resolveLocation(postalCode, countryCode, city);
        if (geoRes) {
          lat = geoRes.latitude;
          lon = geoRes.longitude;
          locStatus = "resolved";
        } else {
          locStatus = "failed";
        }
      }

      const skills = skillsInput
        .split(",")
        .map((s) => s.trim())
        .filter((s) => s.length > 0);

      const normPostal = normalizePostalCode(postalCode);

      const { data: mission, error } = await supabase
        .from("missions")
        .insert({
          owner_id: user.id,
          title,
          mission_reference: reference || null,
          description: description || null,
          country_code: countryCode,
          postal_code: postalCode,
          city: city || null,
          region: region || null,
          latitude: lat,
          longitude: lon,
          location_status: locStatus,
          location_updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          required_skills: skills,
          required_staff_count: requiredCount,
          transport_mode: transportMode,
          status: "open",
        })
        .select()
        .single();

      if (error) {
        setFormError(error.message);
        setSubmitting(false);
        return;
      }

      await supabase.from("activity_logs").insert({
        owner_id: user.id,
        mission_id: mission.id,
        event_type: "mission_created",
        description: `Created mission "${title}" requiring ${requiredCount} staff`,
        metadata: { postal_code: postalCode, transport_mode: transportMode },
      });

      router.push(`/missions/${mission.id}/staff`);
      router.refresh();
    } catch {
      setFormError("Failed to save mission.");
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div>
      <TopBar
        title="Create New Mission"
        subtitle="Set up staffing requirement and target assignment location"
        actions={
          <Link
            href="/missions"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:bg-zinc-50 text-[#172033] rounded-lg transition-colors"
          >
            <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            Back to Missions
          </Link>
        }
      />

      <main className="p-8 max-w-3xl mx-auto">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] p-8 shadow-sm">
          {formError && (
            <div className="mb-6 p-4 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-red-700">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
              <span>{formError}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Mission Title *</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={title}
                  onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm"
                  placeholder="e.g. Warehouse Inventory Night Shift"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">
                  Mission Reference (Optional)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={reference}
                  onChange={(e) => setReference(e.target.value)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm font-mono"
                  placeholder="MSN-2025-01"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Description</label>
              <textarea
                rows={2}
                value={description}
                onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm"
                placeholder="Mission objectives, dress code, report-to instructions..."
              />
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">
                  Required Staff Count *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  min={1}
                  required
                  value={requiredCount}
                  onChange={(e) => setRequiredCount(parseInt(e.target.value) || 1)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">Preferred Transport Mode</label>
                <select
                  value={transportMode}
                  onChange={(e) => setTransportMode(e.target.value as TransportMode)}
                  className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm bg-white"
                >
                  {APP_CONFIG.transportModes.map((m) => (
                    <option key={m.id} value={m.id} disabled={!m.supported}>
                      {m.label} {!m.supported ? "(Unsupported)" : ""}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Target Location Box */}
            <div className="p-4 bg-[#F7F8FA] rounded-lg border border-[#E5E7EB] space-y-4">
              <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#172033] flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 text-[#3157D5]" />
                Mission Assignment Location
              </span>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-[#172033]">Country</label>
                  <select
                    value={countryCode}
                    onChange={(e) => setCountryCode(e.target.value)}
                    className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-1.5 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-xs bg-white"
                  >
                    {APP_CONFIG.countries.map((c) => (
                      <option key={c.code} value={c.code}>
                        {c.name} ({c.code})
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-[#172033]">Postal Code *</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={postalCode}
                    onChange={(e) => setPostalCode(e.target.value)}
                    className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-1.5 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-xs"
                    placeholder="e.g. 94102"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-[#172033]">City</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={city}
                    onChange={(e) => setCity(e.target.value)}
                    className="mt-1 w-full px-3 py-1.5 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-xs"
                    placeholder="e.g. San Francisco"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleValidateLocation}
                  disabled={validatingLocation || !postalCode}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-white border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:bg-zinc-100 rounded-lg text-[#172033]"
                >
                  {validatingLocation ? "Resolving coordinates..." : "Validate Location"}
                </button>

                {resolvedCoords && (
                  <span className="text-xs text-emerald-700 font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    Coordinates resolved ({resolvedCoords.lat.toFixed(4)}, {resolvedCoords.lon.toFixed(4)})
                  </span>
                )}
                {locationError && (
                  <span className="text-xs text-red-600 font-medium">{locationError}</span>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-[#172033]">
                Required Skills (comma separated)
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={skillsInput}
                onChange={(e) => setSkillsInput(e.target.value)}
                className="mt-1 w-full px-3.5 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm"
                placeholder="Forklift, Security"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 pt-4 border-t border-[#E5E7EB]">
              <Link
                href="/missions"
                className="px-4 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-[#667085] hover:bg-zinc-50"
              >
                Cancel
              </Link>
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={submitting}
                className="px-5 py-2 bg-[#3157D5] hover:bg-[#2647B8] text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium shadow-sm disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {submitting ? "Creating Mission..." : "Create Mission & Find Staff"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
```

app/missions/[id]/page.tsx
```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  Compass,
  MapPin,
  Users,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
  Car,
  Briefcase,
} from "lucide-react";
import { TopBar } from "@/components/navigation/TopBar";
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { Mission, MissionStaff } from "@/lib/types/database";

export default async function MissionDetailPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
}) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();

  const [{ data: mission }, { data: assignedStaff }] = await Promise.all([
    supabase.from("missions").select("*").eq("id", id).single(),
    supabase
      .from("mission_staff")
      .select("*, staff(*)")
      .eq("mission_id", id),
  ]);

  if (!mission) {
    notFound();
  }

  const m: Mission = mission;
  const assignments = (assignedStaff as MissionStaff[]) || [];

  return (
    <div>
      <TopBar
        title={m.title}
        subtitle={m.mission_reference ? `Ref: ${m.mission_reference}` : "Mission Details"}
        actions={
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Link
              href="/missions"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:bg-zinc-50 text-[#172033] rounded-lg transition-colors"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              Missions
            </Link>
            <Link
              href={`/missions/${m.id}/staff`}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-[#3157D5] hover:bg-[#2647B8] text-white rounded-lg shadow-sm transition-colors"
            >
              <Compass className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              Find & Compare Staff
            </Link>
          </div>
        }
      />

      <main className="p-8 max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-6">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] p-6 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 pb-6 border-b border-[#E5E7EB]">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#172033]">{m.title}</h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-[#667085] mt-1">{m.description || "No description provided."}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium capitalize bg-blue-50 text-blue-700">
                {m.status.replace(/_/g, " ")}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-6 pt-6">
            <div>
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#667085] uppercase tracking-wider">Mission Location</span>
              <div className="mt-2 text-sm text-[#172033]">
                <p className="font-medium flex items-center gap-1">
                  <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 text-[#3157D5]" />
                  {m.postal_code} {m.city ? `, ${m.city}` : ""}
                </p>
                <p className="text-xs text-[#667085] mt-0.5">
                  {m.location_status === "resolved" ? "Coordinates verified" : "Pending resolution"}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#667085] uppercase tracking-wider">Staffing Progress</span>
              <div className="mt-2 text-sm text-[#172033]">
                <p className="font-semibold text-base text-[#3157D5]">
                  {assignments.length} of {m.required_staff_count} selected
                </p>
                <p className="text-xs text-[#667085] mt-0.5">
                  {assignments.length >= m.required_staff_count
                    ? "Target headcount selected"
                    : `${m.required_staff_count - assignments.length} candidates still needed`}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#667085] uppercase tracking-wider">Preferences</span>
              <div className="mt-2 text-xs space-y-1 text-[#172033]">
                <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 capitalize">
                  <Car className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#667085]" />
                  Transport: {m.transport_mode}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Assigned Staff List */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] p-6 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-base text-[#172033]">
              Selected Staff ({assignments.length})
            </h3>
            <Link
              href={`/missions/${m.id}/staff`}
              className="text-xs font-medium text-[#3157D5] hover:underline"
            >
              + Add More Candidates
            </Link>
          </div>

          {assignments.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-xs text-[#667085] py-6 text-center">
              No staff members selected yet. Use &quot;Find & Compare Staff&quot; to calculate travel times and select workers.
            </p>
          ) : (
            <div className="divide-y divide-[#E5E7EB]">
              {assignments.map((asg) => (
                <div key={asg.id} className="py-3 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div>
                    <p className="font-medium text-sm text-[#172033]">
                      {asg.staff?.full_name || "Unknown Worker"}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-[#667085]">
                      Postal Code: {asg.staff?.postal_code || "-"} | Status:{" "}
                      <strong className="capitalize text-[#172033]">{asg.assignment_status}</strong>
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full capitalize bg-zinc-100 text-[#172033]">
                    {asg.assignment_status}
                  </span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
```

app/missions/[id]/staff/page.tsx
```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  MapPin,
  Car,
  Compass,
  CheckCircle2,
  Phone,
  AlertCircle,
  HelpCircle,
} from "lucide-react";
import { TopBar } from "@/components/navigation/TopBar";
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { Mission, Staff, MissionStaff } from "@/lib/types/database";
import { calculateHaversineDistanceMeters, formatDistance, formatDuration, cleanPhoneNumber } from "@/lib/geo";
import { calculateRouteEstimate } from "@/lib/services/routing";
import { StaffCandidateCard } from "@/components/missions/StaffCandidateCard";

export default async function MissionStaffSearchPage({
  params,
  searchParams,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
  searchParams: Promise<{ [key: string]: string | string[] | undefined }>;
}) {
  const { id } = await params;
  const sParams = await searchParams;
  const radiusKm = parseInt(typeof sParams.radius === "string" ? sParams.radius : "10") || 10;
  const mode = (typeof sParams.mode === "string" ? sParams.mode : "driving") as
    | "driving"
    | "walking"
    | "cycling"
    | "public_transport";

  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();

  // 1. Load mission
  const { data: mission } = await supabase.from("missions").select("*").eq("id", id).single();
  if (!mission) notFound();
  const m: Mission = mission;

  // 2. Load already selected staff for this mission
  const { data: currentAssignments } = await supabase
    .from("mission_staff")
    .select("staff_id, assignment_status")
    .eq("mission_id", id);

  const assignedMap = new Map<string, string>();
  (currentAssignments || []).forEach((asg: { staff_id: string; assignment_status: string }) => {
    assignedMap.set(asg.staff_id, asg.assignment_status);
  });

  // 3. Ensure mission has valid coordinates
  if (!m.latitude || !m.longitude) {
    return (
      <div>
        <TopBar
          title={`Find Staff for ${m.title}`}
          subtitle="Geographic matching and travel time estimation"
          actions={
            <Link
              href={`/missions/${m.id}`}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:bg-zinc-50 rounded-lg"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              Back to Mission
            </Link>
          }
        />
        <main className="p-8 max-w-3xl mx-auto">
          <div className="p-6 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl text-center space-y-3">
            <AlertCircle className="w-8 h-8 text-amber-600 mx-auto" />
            <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-amber-900">Mission Coordinates Unresolved</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-amber-800 max-w-md mx-auto">
              This mission has no valid geographic coordinates stored for postal code{" "}
              <strong>{m.postal_code}</strong>. Distance and route calculations cannot run until
              the location is resolved.
            </p>
            <Link
              href={`/missions/${m.id}/edit`}
              className="inline-block px-4 py-2 bg-amber-600 text-white text-xs font-medium rounded-lg hover:bg-amber-700"
            >
              Resolve Mission Location
            </Link>
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // 4. Query available staff records with resolved coordinates
  const { data: availableStaff } = await supabase
    .from("staff")
    .select("*")
    .is("archived_at", null)
    .not("latitude", "is", null)
    .not("longitude", "is", null);

  const allCandidates: Staff[] = (availableStaff as Staff[]) || [];

  // 5. Stage 1: Haversine distance calculation and radius filtering
  interface RankedCandidate {
    staff: Staff;
    straightLineMeters: number;
    roadMeters: number | null;
    durationSeconds: number | null;
    routeNotes?: string;
    isRealRoute: boolean;
  }

  const radiusMeters = radiusKm * 1000;
  const initialCandidates: RankedCandidate[] = [];

  for (const staff of allCandidates) {
    if (staff.latitude && staff.longitude) {
      const dist = calculateHaversineDistanceMeters(
        staff.latitude,
        staff.longitude,
        m.latitude,
        m.longitude
      );

      if (dist <= radiusMeters) {
        initialCandidates.push({
          staff,
          straightLineMeters: dist,
          roadMeters: null,
          durationSeconds: null,
          isRealRoute: false,
        });
      }
    }
  }

  // Sort initially by straight-line distance
  initialCandidates.sort((a, b) => a.straightLineMeters - b.straightLineMeters);

  // 6. Stage 2: Retrieve real road route & travel time for top candidates (bounded concurrency)
  const topCandidates = initialCandidates.slice(0, 15);

  await Promise.all(
    topCandidates.map(async (cand) => {
      const routeRes = await calculateRouteEstimate(
        cand.staff.latitude!,
        cand.staff.longitude!,
        m.latitude!,
        m.longitude!,
        mode
      );

      if (routeRes) {
        cand.roadMeters = routeRes.distanceMeters;
        cand.durationSeconds = routeRes.durationSeconds;
        cand.isRealRoute = routeRes.isRealRoute;
        cand.routeNotes = routeRes.notes;
      }
    })
  );

  return (
    <div>
      <TopBar
        title={`Staff Matching: ${m.title}`}
        subtitle={`Location: ${m.postal_code} ${m.city || ""} | Goal: ${m.required_staff_count} staff (${assignedMap.size} selected)`}
        actions={
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Link
              href={`/missions/${m.id}`}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium border border-[#E5E7EB] hover:bg-zinc-50 rounded-lg"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              Mission Details
            </Link>
          </div>
        }
      />

      <main className="p-8 space-y-6 max-w-6xl mx-auto">
        {/* Radius & Mode Filter Bar */}
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#172033]">Search Radius:</span>
            {[5, 10, 20, 30, 50].map((r) => (
              <Link
                key={r}
                href={`/missions/${m.id}/staff?radius=${r}&mode=${mode}`}
                className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium rounded-lg transition-colors ${
                  radiusKm === r
                    ? "bg-[#3157D5] text-white"
                    : "bg-zinc-100 text-[#667085] hover:text-[#172033]"
                }`}
              >
                {r} km
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#172033]">Transport Mode:</span>
            {(["driving", "walking", "cycling", "public_transport"] as const).map((mMode) => {
              const isUnsupported = mMode === "public_transport";
              return (
                <Link
                  key={mMode}
                  href={isUnsupported ? "#" : `/missions/${m.id}/staff?radius=${radiusKm}&mode=${mMode}`}
                  className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium rounded-lg capitalize transition-colors ${
                    mode === mMode
                      ? "bg-[#3157D5] text-white"
                      : isUnsupported
                      ? "bg-zinc-50 text-zinc-400 cursor-not-allowed"
                      : "bg-zinc-100 text-[#667085] hover:text-[#172033]"
                  }`}
                  title={isUnsupported ? "No real public transit provider configured" : ""}
                >
                  {mMode.replace(/_/g, " ")}
                </Link>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Results Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-[#667085]">
          <p>
            Found <strong>{topCandidates.length}</strong> qualified candidates within {radiusKm} km radius.
          </p>
          <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-emerald-700 font-medium">
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            Distances computed from verified registered postal codes
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Candidate Cards Grid */}
        {topCandidates.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] p-12 text-center">
            <Compass className="w-10 h-10 text-[#667085] mx-auto mb-2 opacity-50" />
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-[#172033]">No candidates within {radiusKm} km</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-[#667085] mt-1 max-w-sm mx-auto">
              Try expanding the search radius to 20 or 50 km, or add workers with postal codes closer to this mission.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
            {topCandidates.map((cand) => (
              <StaffCandidateCard
                key={cand.staff.id}
                candidate={cand.staff}
                missionId={m.id}
                straightLineMeters={cand.straightLineMeters}
                roadMeters={cand.roadMeters}
                durationSeconds={cand.durationSeconds}
                isRealRoute={cand.isRealRoute}
                routeNotes={cand.routeNotes}
                transportMode={mode}
                isAssigned={assignedMap.has(cand.staff.id)}
                assignmentStatus={assignedMap.get(cand.staff.id)}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}
```

components/missions/StaffCandidateCard.tsx
```tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import {
  MapPin,
  Car,
  Clock,
  CheckCircle2,
  Phone,
  Plus,
  Trash2,
} from "lucide-react";
import { Staff } from "@/lib/types/database";
import { formatDistance, formatDuration, cleanPhoneNumber } from "@/lib/geo";
import { createClient } from "@/lib/supabase/client";

interface StaffCandidateCardProps {
  candidate: Staff;
  missionId: string;
  straightLineMeters: number;
  roadMeters: number | null;
  durationSeconds: number | null;
  isRealRoute: boolean;
  routeNotes?: string;
  transportMode: string;
  isAssigned: boolean;
  assignmentStatus?: string;
}

export function StaffCandidateCard({
  candidate,
  missionId,
  straightLineMeters,
  roadMeters,
  durationSeconds,
  isRealRoute,
  routeNotes,
  transportMode,
  isAssigned: initialAssigned,
  assignmentStatus,
}: StaffCandidateCardProps) {
  const supabase = createClient();
  const [assigned, setAssigned] = useState(initialAssigned);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const phone = cleanPhoneNumber(candidate.phone);
  const whatsappUrl = phone ? `https://wa.me/${phone.replace(/\+/g, "")}` : null;

  const handleToggleSelect = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    const {
      data: { user },
    } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

    if (!user) {
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    if (assigned) {
      // Remove assignment
      await supabase
        .from("mission_staff")
        .delete()
        .eq("mission_id", missionId)
        .eq("staff_id", candidate.id);

      await supabase.from("activity_logs").insert({
        owner_id: user.id,
        mission_id: missionId,
        staff_id: candidate.id,
        event_type: "staff_unassigned",
        description: `Removed candidate ${candidate.full_name} from mission`,
      });

      setAssigned(false);
    } else {
      // Create assignment
      await supabase.from("mission_staff").insert({
        owner_id: user.id,
        mission_id: missionId,
        staff_id: candidate.id,
        assignment_status: "selected",
      });

      await supabase.from("activity_logs").insert({
        owner_id: user.id,
        mission_id: missionId,
        staff_id: candidate.id,
        event_type: "staff_selected",
        description: `Selected ${candidate.full_name} for mission assignment`,
      });

      setAssigned(true);
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  return (
    <div
      className={`bg-white rounded-xl border p-5 flex flex-col justify-between transition-all ${
        assigned ? "border-emerald-500 ring-1 ring-emerald-500 shadow-sm" : "border-[#E5E7EB]"
      }`}
    >
      <div>
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
          <div>
            <Link
              href={`/staff/${candidate.id}`}
              className="font-semibold text-sm text-[#172033] hover:text-[#3157D5]"
            >
              {candidate.full_name}
            </Link>
            {candidate.staff_code && (
              <span className="block text-xs font-mono text-[#667085]">{candidate.staff_code}</span>
            )}
          </div>
          <span
            className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase ${
              candidate.availability_status === "available"
                ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700"
                : "bg-zinc-100 text-[#667085]"
            }`}
          >
            {candidate.availability_status}
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Location & Distances */}
        <div className="mt-4 p-3 bg-[#F7F8FA] rounded-lg border border-[#E5E7EB] space-y-1.5 text-xs">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[#172033]">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-medium">
              <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#3157D5]" />
              {candidate.postal_code} {candidate.city ? `(${candidate.city})` : ""}
            </span>
            <span className="text-[#667085]">
              {formatDistance(straightLineMeters)} straight-line
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-1 border-t border-zinc-200 text-[#172033]">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-semibold text-[#3157D5]">
              <Car className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              {roadMeters ? formatDistance(roadMeters) : formatDistance(straightLineMeters)} road
            </span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-semibold text-emerald-700">
              <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              {formatDuration(durationSeconds)}
            </span>
          </div>

          {!isRealRoute && (
            <p className="text-[10px] text-amber-600 italic">
              Road route calculation fallback ({transportMode})
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Skills */}
        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-1">
          {candidate.skills && candidate.skills.length > 0 ? (
            candidate.skills.slice(0, 3).map((s, idx) => (
              <span key={idx} className="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-zinc-100 text-[10px] text-zinc-700 rounded">
                {s}
              </span>
            ))
          ) : (
            <span className="text-[10px] text-[#667085] italic">No skills listed</span>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Action Footer */}
      <div className="mt-5 pt-3 border-t border-[#E5E7EB] flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          {candidate.phone && (
            <a
              href={`tel:${candidate.phone}`}
              className="p-1.5 text-[#667085] hover:text-[#3157D5] hover:bg-zinc-100 rounded"
              title="Call"
            >
              <Phone className="w-4 h-4" />
            </a>
          )}
          {whatsappUrl && (
            <a
              href={whatsappUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-medium bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-100 rounded"
            >
              WhatsApp
            </a>
          )}
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={handleToggleSelect}
          disabled={loading}
          className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium rounded-lg inline-flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors ${
            assigned
              ? "bg-red-50 text-red-600 hover:bg-red-100"
              : "bg-[#3157D5] hover:bg-[#2647B8] text-white"
          }`}
        >
          {assigned ? (
            <>
              <Trash2 className="w-3 h-3" /> Selected (Remove)
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Plus className="w-3 h-3" /> Select for Mission
            </>
          )}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

import Link from "next/link";
import { Compass, MapPin, Search, CheckCircle2, Car, Clock } from "lucide-react";
import { TopBar } from "@/components/navigation/TopBar";
import { createServerSupabaseClient } from "@/lib/supabase/server";
import { resolveLocation } from "@/lib/services/geocoding";
import { calculateHaversineDistanceMeters, formatDistance, formatDuration } from "@/lib/geo";
import { Staff } from "@/lib/types/database";

export default async function GeneralFindStaffPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ [key: string]: string | string[] | undefined }>;
}) {
  const params = await searchParams;
  const postalCode = typeof params.postal_code === "string" ? params.postal_code.trim() : "";
  const radiusKm = parseInt(typeof params.radius === "string" ? params.radius : "20") || 20;

  const supabase = await createServerSupabaseClient();

  let resolvedOrigin: { lat: number; lon: number; city?: string } | null = null;
  let matches: { staff: Staff; distanceMeters: number }[] = [];

  if (postalCode) {
    const geo = await resolveLocation(postalCode, "US");
    if (geo) {
      resolvedOrigin = { lat: geo.latitude, lon: geo.longitude, city: geo.city };

      const { data: staffList } = await supabase
        .from("staff")
        .select("*")
        .is("archived_at", null)
        .not("latitude", "is", null)
        .not("longitude", "is", null);

      if (staffList) {
        const radiusMeters = radiusKm * 1000;
        for (const s of staffList as Staff[]) {
          const d = calculateHaversineDistanceMeters(
            s.latitude!,
            s.longitude!,
            resolvedOrigin.lat,
            resolvedOrigin.lon
          );
          if (d <= radiusMeters) {
            matches.push({ staff: s, distanceMeters: d });
          }
        }
        matches.sort((a, b) => a.distanceMeters - b.distanceMeters);
      }
    }
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <TopBar
        title="Find Staff"
        subtitle="Geographic workforce exploration from any postal code"
      />

      <main className="p-8 max-w-5xl mx-auto space-y-6">
        {/* Search Input Box */}
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-[#E5E7EB] p-6 shadow-sm">
          <form method="GET" className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-4 items-end">
            <div className="sm:col-span-2">
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[#172033] mb-1">
                Reference Postal Code
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 text-[#667085] absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  name="postal_code"
                  defaultValue={postalCode}
                  placeholder="e.g. 94103"
                  className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm"
                  required
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[#172033] mb-1">
                Radius (km)
              </label>
              <select
                name="radius"
                defaultValue={radiusKm}
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 border border-[#E5E7EB] rounded-lg text-sm bg-white"
              >
                <option value="5">5 km</option>
                <option value="10">10 km</option>
                <option value="20">20 km</option>
                <option value="30">30 km</option>
                <option value="50">50 km</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#3157D5] hover:bg-

#### # MASTER PROMPT   
####   
#### ## Complete Staff Management, Mission Planning, Geographic Search and Travel Estimation SaaS MVP  
####   
#### ## 1. ROLE AND OBJECTIVE  
####   
#### Act as a senior full-stack software engineer, software architect, database engineer, geospatial engineer, UI/UX designer and QA engineer.  
####   
#### Your task is to build a complete, functional and professionally designed SaaS application directly inside the current terminal workspace.  
####   
#### The application must allow staffing managers to manage their workforce, maintain staff location records, check availability, find the most suitable staff for a mission, calculate the distance between a staff member's registered location and the mission location, and estimate travel time according to the selected mode of transport.  
####   
#### This is an implementation task. Do not merely produce a plan, a mockup, pseudocode or another prompt.  
####   
#### You must implement the actual application, database, authentication, business logic, pages, forms, integrations, validation, tests and documentation.  
####   
#### The final product must be a usable MVP with real database persistence and real functionality.  
####   
#### ## 2. CORE BUSINESS PROBLEM  
####   
#### A manager may manage hundreds or thousands of staff members distributed across different cities, regions and countries.  
####   
#### When a new mission is received, the manager needs to know quickly:  
####   
#### - Which staff members are available?  
#### - Which staff members have the required skills?  
#### - Which staff members are closest to the mission location?  
#### - What is the distance between each candidate's registered location and the mission?  
#### - What is the estimated journey time by car, public transport or another supported mode?  
#### - Which candidates can reasonably reach the mission location?  
#### - How can the manager contact suitable staff immediately?  
#### - Which staff members have missing or outdated location information?  
#### - How can the manager manage all these operations without manually inspecting hundreds of records?  
####   
#### The application's primary objective is to help the manager find and evaluate suitable staff for a mission in seconds.  
####   
#### ## 3. MANDATORY DEVELOPMENT RULES  
####   
#### Before writing code:  
####   
#### 1. Inspect the current working directory and existing project.  
#### 2. Identify the framework, package manager, dependencies, existing components, environment variables and database configuration.  
#### 3. Preserve valid existing code wherever practical.  
#### 4. Check the official documentation for the installed versions of all unfamiliar libraries and APIs.  
#### 5. Identify which integrations are configured and which require credentials.  
#### 6. Create an implementation checklist.  
#### 7. Start implementing the application directly in the workspace.  
####   
#### Do not ask questions when sensible technical defaults are sufficient.  
####   
#### Do not replace the application with static mockups.  
####   
#### Do not fabricate data, coordinates, routes, travel times, staff availability or successful API responses.  
####   
#### Do not create buttons that do nothing.  
####   
#### Do not expose private API keys or privileged database credentials in client-side code.  
####   
#### Do not disable TypeScript, linting or tests to conceal errors.  
####   
#### Do not claim that a feature works unless it has been implemented and appropriately verified.  
####   
#### Work in coherent phases, run checks frequently, diagnose errors and correct them before proceeding.  
####   
#### If a service cannot be tested because credentials are missing, implement the integration, document the configuration requirement and distinguish unverified behavior from verified behavior.  
####   
#### ## 4. TECHNOLOGY STACK  
####   
#### Use the following stack where compatible with the existing project:  
####   
#### - Next.js App Router.  
#### - React.  
#### - TypeScript with strict mode.  
#### - Tailwind CSS.  
#### - shadcn/ui.  
#### - Lucide React.  
#### - Supabase Authentication.  
#### - Supabase PostgreSQL.  
#### - Supabase Row Level Security.  
#### - Zod.  
#### - React Hook Form.  
#### - Mapbox GL JS.  
#### - A suitable route and travel-time provider, such as Mapbox Directions or another compatible routing API.  
#### - A geocoding provider suitable for the selected country.  
#### - Papa Parse or another maintained CSV parser.  
#### - Vitest and React Testing Library where compatible.  
#### - Playwright for end-to-end tests where the environment permits.  
####   
#### Use server components by default and client components where interactivity requires them.  
####   
#### Keep geocoding and routing providers behind server-side service interfaces so providers can be changed without rewriting the application.  
####   
#### Do not assume that postal codes, units, road networks or transport options are identical in every country.  
####   
#### ## 5. PRODUCT NAME AND CONFIGURATION  
####   
#### Working product name: StaffFlow.  
####   
#### Keep the product name, default preferences and feature configuration in centralized configuration files.  
####   
#### The application must not be permanently tied to one country.  
####   
#### Support country-specific:  
####   
#### - Postal-code validation.  
#### - Location resolution.  
#### - Phone-number formatting.  
#### - Distance units where appropriate.  
#### - Currency and locale settings if needed in future.  
#### - Routing and travel-mode availability.  
####   
#### The initial setup must be configurable for the launch market, without hardcoding the application architecture to a single country.  
####   
#### ## 6. USER ROLES AND MVP SCOPE  
####   
#### ### Manager  
####   
#### The manager can:  
####   
#### - Register and log in.  
#### - Create and manage staff records.  
#### - Import and export staff.  
#### - Update staff availability.  
#### - Maintain registered location information.  
#### - Create and manage missions.  
#### - Search for suitable staff near a mission.  
#### - Compare distances and estimated travel times.  
#### - View staff and mission locations on a map.  
#### - Contact staff.  
#### - Review activity history and notifications.  
#### - Manage account and search preferences.  
####   
#### ### Staff member  
####   
#### For the MVP, staff members are records created or imported by a manager.  
####   
#### Staff members do not have their own accounts, login pages or self-registration.  
####   
#### ### Client  
####   
#### Clients do not have accounts or access to staff tracking in the MVP.  
####   
#### Do not build a client portal or expose internal staff locations to clients.  
####   
#### ### Future architecture  
####   
#### Keep the data model extensible for future staff accounts, client accounts, recruitment, job listings, organizational memberships and additional countries.  
####   
#### Do not implement these future modules now.  
####   
#### ## 7. LOCATION TRACKING  IMPORTANT SCOPE LIMITATION  
####   
#### The MVP must implement **registered-location management, not real-time GPS tracking**.  
####   
#### The manager can consult the latest location information recorded for a staff member, based on the postal code and coordinates stored in that person's profile.  
####   
#### The application must distinguish between:  
####   
#### 1. **Registered location:** the location entered or maintained in the staff record.  
#### 2. **Last location verification:** the last time the postal code was successfully resolved or verified.  
#### 3. **Mission location:** the location associated with a staffing assignment.  
#### 4. **Live location:** not implemented in the MVP.  
####   
#### Do not implement:  
####   
#### - Continuous GPS tracking.  
#### - Background location collection.  
#### - Live staff movement on the map.  
#### - Automatic collection of staff device coordinates.  
#### - A live location feed for clients.  
#### - Claims that a staff member is physically present at a location.  
####   
#### Never imply that a registered postal code proves a person's current physical position.  
####   
#### Display clear labels such as:  
####   
#### - Registered Location.  
#### - Location Verified.  
#### - Location Needs Review.  
#### - Last Verified.  
####   
#### If a staff member's registered location changes, update the record only through a valid manager action or a properly authorized future integration.  
####   
#### Location timestamps must describe the actual operation performed. Do not use a recent timestamp to make an old location appear current.  
####   
#### ## 8. APPLICATION ROUTES  
####   
#### Public routes:  
####   
#### - `/`  landing page.  
#### - `/login`  login.  
#### - `/signup`  manager registration.  
#### - `/auth/callback`  authentication callback if required.  
#### - `/auth/verify`  verification flow where applicable.  
#### - `/forgot-password`  password recovery if password authentication is enabled.  
####   
#### Protected routes:  
####   
#### - `/dashboard`  operational dashboard.  
#### - `/staff`  staff directory.  
#### - `/staff/new`  create staff.  
#### - `/staff/[id]`  staff profile.  
#### - `/staff/[id]/edit`  edit staff.  
#### - `/staff/import`  CSV import.  
#### - `/find-staff`  geographic staff search.  
#### - `/missions`  mission directory.  
#### - `/missions/new`  create mission.  
#### - `/missions/[id]`  mission details.  
#### - `/missions/[id]/edit`  edit mission.  
#### - `/missions/[id]/staff`  find, compare and select staff for a mission.  
#### - `/activity`  activity history.  
#### - `/notifications`  notifications.  
#### - `/settings`  general settings.  
#### - `/settings/profile`  manager profile.  
#### - `/settings/security`  account security.  
#### - `/settings/location`  geographic search and travel preferences.  
####   
#### You may combine related screens where that improves usability, but all required capabilities must remain accessible.  
####   
#### Protect every private route and enforce authorization on the server.  
####   
#### ## 9. DESIGN SYSTEM  
####   
#### Create a premium, professional B2B SaaS interface.  
####   
#### Suggested palette:  
####   
#### - Background: `#F7F8FA`.  
#### - Surface: `#FFFFFF`.  
#### - Main text: `#172033`.  
#### - Secondary text: `#667085`.  
#### - Primary action: `#3157D5`.  
#### - Primary hover: `#2647B8`.  
#### - Border: `#E5E7EB`.  
####   
#### Use Inter or a similarly readable font.  
####   
#### Use:  
####   
#### - Consistent spacing.  
#### - Clear hierarchy.  
#### - Compact data tables.  
#### - Accessible status indicators.  
#### - Responsive cards.  
#### - Clear form labels.  
#### - Subtle shadows.  
#### - Consistent button sizes.  
#### - Skeleton loading states.  
#### - Empty states.  
#### - Useful error messages.  
#### - Confirmation dialogs for destructive actions.  
#### - Toasts for completed operations.  
####   
#### Avoid excessive gradients, unnecessary animations and decorative elements that interfere with operational work.  
####   
#### Respect keyboard accessibility and `prefers-reduced-motion`.  
####   
#### ### Global navigation  
####   
#### Sidebar:  
####   
#### 1. Dashboard.  
#### 2. Staff Directory.  
#### 3. Find Staff.  
#### 4. Missions.  
#### 5. Import Staff.  
#### 6. Activity.  
#### 7. Notifications.  
#### 8. Settings.  
####   
#### Include:  
####   
#### - Product logo and name.  
#### - Current manager profile.  
#### - Account menu.  
#### - Logout.  
#### - Active navigation state.  
#### - Mobile navigation.  
####   
#### Every button must perform its advertised action, show a pending state when needed and report errors appropriately.  
####   
#### ## 10. AUTHENTICATION  
####   
#### Use Supabase Authentication.  
####   
#### Implement:  
####   
#### - Manager signup.  
#### - Login.  
#### - Email verification or OTP according to the configured authentication method.  
#### - Session persistence.  
#### - Protected routes.  
#### - Logout.  
#### - Password recovery if password authentication is enabled.  
####   
#### Signup fields:  
####   
#### - Full name.  
#### - Email.  
#### - Password when applicable.  
#### - Confirm password when applicable.  
#### - Company name, optional.  
#### - Country or primary operating region.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Create Account.  
#### - Sign In.  
#### - Show/Hide Password.  
#### - Forgot Password.  
#### - Verify Email or Verify Code, when applicable.  
#### - Resend Code, when applicable.  
####   
#### Validate all fields.  
####   
#### Handle expired sessions, incorrect credentials, duplicate accounts, expired verification codes and rate limits.  
####   
#### Do not create unauthorized manager profiles or trust a client-supplied owner ID.  
####   
#### ## 11. DATABASE SCHEMA  
####   
#### Create version-controlled SQL migrations, constraints, indexes and RLS policies.  
####   
#### ### `profiles`  
####   
#### Fields:  
####   
#### - `id`: UUID matching the authenticated user.  
#### - `full_name`.  
#### - `company_name`.  
#### - `country_code`.  
#### - `timezone`.  
#### - `default_search_radius_km`.  
#### - `default_transport_mode`.  
#### - `created_at`.  
#### - `updated_at`.  
####   
#### ### `staff`  
####   
#### Fields:  
####   
#### - `id`: UUID.  
#### - `owner_id`: UUID.  
#### - `staff_code`: optional.  
#### - `full_name`: required.  
#### - `phone`: optional.  
#### - `email`: optional.  
#### - `country_code`: required.  
#### - `postal_code`: required where geographic search is used.  
#### - `postal_code_normalized`.  
#### - `city`.  
#### - `region`.  
#### - `latitude`: nullable.  
#### - `longitude`: nullable.  
#### - `location_status`: `pending`, `resolved`, or `failed`.  
#### - `location_updated_at`: nullable.  
#### - `availability_status`: `available`, `unavailable`, or `unknown`.  
#### - `skills`.  
#### - `notes`.  
#### - `last_contacted_at`: nullable.  
#### - `created_at`.  
#### - `updated_at`.  
#### - `archived_at`: nullable.  
####   
#### A missing availability value must never be treated as available.  
####   
#### A record with unresolved coordinates must not appear in geographic search results as if its location were verified.  
####   
#### ### `missions`  
####   
#### Fields:  
####   
#### - `id`: UUID.  
#### - `owner_id`: UUID.  
#### - `mission_reference`: optional unique identifier within the manager's account.  
#### - `title`: required.  
#### - `description`: optional.  
#### - `country_code`: required.  
#### - `postal_code`: required where available.  
#### - `city`: optional.  
#### - `region`: optional.  
#### - `latitude`: nullable until resolved.  
#### - `longitude`: nullable until resolved.  
#### - `location_status`: `pending`, `resolved`, or `failed`.  
#### - `location_updated_at`: nullable.  
#### - `required_skills`: array or normalized relation.  
#### - `required_staff_count`: integer greater than zero.  
#### - `scheduled_start_at`: nullable.  
#### - `scheduled_end_at`: nullable.  
#### - `transport_mode`: configured default for this mission.  
#### - `status`: `draft`, `open`, `staffing_in_progress`, `staffed`, `completed`, or `cancelled`.  
#### - `notes`: optional.  
#### - `created_at`.  
#### - `updated_at`.  
#### - `archived_at`: nullable.  
####   
#### ### `mission_staff`  
####   
#### This table records the manager's staff selections for a mission.  
####   
#### Fields:  
####   
#### - `id`: UUID.  
#### - `owner_id`: UUID.  
#### - `mission_id`: UUID.  
#### - `staff_id`: UUID.  
#### - `assignment_status`: `selected`, `contacted`, `confirmed`, `declined`, `cancelled`, or `completed`.  
#### - `selected_at`.  
#### - `updated_at`.  
#### - `notes`: optional.  
####   
#### Prevent duplicate active assignments for the same staff member and mission where appropriate.  
####   
#### A staff selection is not proof that the person accepted or attended the mission.  
####   
#### ### `activity_logs`  
####   
#### Fields:  
####   
#### - `id`.  
#### - `owner_id`.  
#### - `staff_id`: nullable.  
#### - `mission_id`: nullable.  
#### - `event_type`.  
#### - `description`.  
#### - `metadata`: JSONB with a data-minimization policy.  
#### - `created_at`.  
####   
#### Log relevant events:  
####   
#### - Staff created or updated.  
#### - Availability changed.  
#### - Location resolved or failed.  
#### - Mission created or updated.  
#### - Staff selected for a mission.  
#### - Staff contacted.  
#### - Assignment status changed.  
#### - CSV import or export completed.  
#### - Routing request failed where operationally useful.  
####   
#### Never store passwords, API secrets or unnecessary personal information in logs.  
####   
#### ### `notifications`  
####   
#### Fields:  
####   
#### - `id`.  
#### - `owner_id`.  
#### - `type`.  
#### - `title`.  
#### - `message`.  
#### - `read_at`: nullable.  
#### - `created_at`.  
####   
#### Create appropriate notifications for imports, location failures and mission-related operations.  
####   
#### ### Optional route calculation cache  
####   
#### If appropriate, create a private `route_estimates` table or equivalent cache.  
####   
#### Potential fields:  
####   
#### - `id`.  
#### - `owner_id`.  
#### - `origin_latitude`.  
#### - `origin_longitude`.  
#### - `destination_latitude`.  
#### - `destination_longitude`.  
#### - `transport_mode`.  
#### - `provider`.  
#### - `distance_meters`.  
#### - `duration_seconds`.  
#### - `estimated_at`.  
#### - `expires_at`.  
####   
#### Only cache valid successful responses.  
####   
#### Avoid storing unnecessary personal details in cache keys.  
####   
#### Cache access must be scoped securely. Use a deterministic cache key based on normalized coordinates and transport mode when appropriate.  
####   
#### Do not persist estimates indefinitely. Configure an expiry policy and refresh stale results when needed.  
####   
#### ### Database indexes  
####   
#### Add suitable indexes for:  
####   
#### - `owner_id`.  
#### - Staff availability.  
#### - Normalized postal code.  
#### - Country and city.  
#### - Staff location coordinates or spatial indexes where supported.  
#### - Mission status and scheduled time.  
#### - Mission location.  
#### - Mission-to-staff relationships.  
#### - Activity timestamps.  
#### - Route cache lookup and expiry.  
####   
#### ## 12. SECURITY AND ROW LEVEL SECURITY  
####   
#### Enable RLS on all private tables.  
####   
#### Ensure managers can access only their own:  
####   
#### - Profiles.  
#### - Staff.  
#### - Missions.  
#### - Mission assignments.  
#### - Activity logs.  
#### - Notifications.  
#### - Private route estimates.  
####   
#### Validate ownership for both the mission and the staff member when creating a mission assignment.  
####   
#### Do not trust IDs submitted by the browser without checking ownership.  
####   
#### Never expose the Supabase service-role key.  
####   
#### Never expose private staff data through public API endpoints.  
####   
#### Use least privilege, input validation and server-side authorization.  
####   
#### Protect against IDOR, injection, XSS, unsafe exports and excessively large requests.  
####   
#### Do not allow a manager to calculate a route between records they are not authorized to access.  
####   
#### ## 13. DASHBOARD  `/dashboard`  
####   
#### Build a real dashboard using authenticated manager data.  
####   
#### ### Header buttons  
####   
#### - `Add Staff`.  
#### - `Import Staff`.  
#### - `New Mission`.  
#### - `Find Staff`.  
####   
#### ### KPI cards

#### - Available Staff.  
#### - Unavailable Staff.  
#### - Staff with Verified Locations.  
#### - Active Missions.  
#### - Missions Requiring Staff.  
####   
#### Every KPI must come from actual database queries.  
####   
#### Clicking a KPI should navigate to the corresponding filtered page.  
####   
#### Do not fabricate growth percentages or statistics.  
####   
#### ### Mission overview  
####   
#### Show:  
####   
#### - Upcoming missions.  
#### - Missions awaiting staff.  
#### - Missions currently being staffed.  
#### - Completed missions.  
####   
#### Each mission card displays:  
####   
#### - Title.  
#### - Location.  
#### - Scheduled start, if available.  
#### - Required staff count.  
#### - Selected staff count.  
#### - Mission status.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - View Mission.  
#### - Find Staff.  
#### - Edit Mission, where authorized.  
####   
#### ### Recent activity  
####   
#### Show real recent events with:  
####   
#### - Description.  
#### - Related staff or mission.  
#### - Timestamp.  
#### - Link to the relevant record.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - View All Activity.  
#### - Refresh.  
####   
#### ### Dashboard states  
####   
#### Implement:  
####   
#### - Loading skeletons.  
#### - Empty state with Add Staff and New Mission.  
#### - Error state with Retry.  
#### - Clear explanations for unresolved locations.  
####   
#### ## 14. STAFF DIRECTORY  `/staff`  
####   
#### Build a real, paginated staff directory.  
####   
#### ### Header buttons  
####   
#### - `Add Staff`.  
#### - `Import CSV`.  
#### - `Export`.  
#### - `Refresh`.  
####   
#### ### Search  
####   
#### Search by:  
####   
#### - Full name.  
#### - Staff code.  
#### - Phone.  
#### - Email.  
#### - Postal code.  
#### - City.  
#### - Skills.  
####   
#### Use debounced, server-side search.  
####   
#### ### Filters  
####   
#### - Availability: All, Available, Unavailable, Unknown.  
#### - Country.  
#### - City.  
#### - Region.  
#### - Skills.  
#### - Location status.  
#### - Archived status.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Apply Filters where appropriate.  
#### - Clear Filters.  
####   
#### ### Table columns  
####   
#### - Selection checkbox.  
#### - Name.  
#### - Staff Code.  
#### - Phone.  
#### - Registered Location.  
#### - Skills.  
#### - Availability.  
#### - Location Status.  
#### - Last Updated.  
#### - Actions.  
####   
#### Row actions:  
####   
#### - View.  
#### - Edit.  
#### - Change Availability.  
#### - Archive.  
####   
#### Bulk actions:  
####   
#### - Export Selected.  
#### - Change Availability.  
#### - Archive Selected.  
#### - Clear Selection.  
####   
#### Use server-side pagination with 25, 50 and 100 records per page.  
####   
#### Preserve filters while navigating.  
####   
#### Do not select records across multiple pages without making that behavior explicit.  
####   
#### ## 15. CREATE AND EDIT STAFF  
####   
#### Routes:  
####   
#### - `/staff/new`  
#### - `/staff/[id]/edit`  
####   
#### Fields:  
####   
#### - Full Name.  
#### - Staff Code.  
#### - Phone.  
#### - Email.  
#### - Country.  
#### - Postal Code.  
#### - City.  
#### - Region.  
#### - Skills.  
#### - Availability.  
#### - Notes.  
####   
#### Location buttons:  
####   
#### - Validate Location.  
#### - Revalidate Location.  
#### - Clear Location.  
####   
#### Form buttons:  
####   
#### - Save Staff.  
#### - Save and Add Another.  
#### - Save Changes.  
#### - Cancel.  
####   
#### When a postal code is entered or changed:  
####   
#### 1. Normalize it.  
#### 2. Validate it for the selected country where possible.  
#### 3. Resolve the location using the configured provider.  
#### 4. Store valid returned coordinates.  
#### 5. Show the resolved location.  
#### 6. Record success or failure.  
#### 7. Never invent coordinates.  
####   
#### A record may be saved without coordinates only if the business rules permit it. Mark it unresolved and exclude it from geographic results until resolved.  
####   
#### Prevent duplicate submissions and preserve form data after recoverable errors.  
####   
#### Warn before discarding unsaved changes.  
####   
#### ## 16. STAFF PROFILE  `/staff/[id]`  
####   
#### Display:  
####   
#### - Full name.  
#### - Staff code.  
#### - Contact information.  
#### - Registered location.  
#### - Availability.  
#### - Skills.  
#### - Notes.  
#### - Location verification status.  
#### - Last location verification.  
#### - Creation and update dates.  
#### - Relevant activity history.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Edit Staff.  
#### - Change Availability.  
#### - Call.  
#### - WhatsApp.  
#### - Copy Phone.  
#### - Find Missions Nearby, if implemented as a real action.  
#### - Archive Staff.  
#### - Back to Directory.  
####   
#### Call and WhatsApp buttons must open external applications using correctly formatted phone numbers.  
####   
#### Do not claim a call was completed or a message was sent.  
####   
#### Display the registered location on a map if coordinates are available.  
####   
#### Clearly label it as the registered location, not a live GPS position.  
####   
#### ## 17. LOCATION MANAGEMENT  
####   
#### Implement a dedicated server-side location service.  
####   
#### It must support:  
####   
#### - Country-specific postal-code normalization.  
#### - Geocoding.  
#### - Coordinate validation.  
#### - Location status management.  
#### - Caching.  
#### - Bounded concurrency for bulk resolution.  
#### - Provider-specific error handling.  
#### - Rate-limit handling.  
####   
#### When a location changes:  
####   
#### 1. Mark the previous result stale or pending.  
#### 2. Resolve the new location.  
#### 3. Validate the result.  
#### 4. Update coordinates only on success.  
#### 5. Update the verification timestamp.  
#### 6. Record the event.  
#### 7. Report failure clearly.  
####   
#### Allow the manager to:  
####   
#### - View unresolved staff locations.  
#### - Resolve pending locations.  
#### - Retry failed locations.  
#### - Revalidate a selected record.  
#### - Review location failures.  
####   
#### Never repeatedly geocode an unchanged postal code without a reason.  
####   
#### Respect provider terms and quotas.  
####

#### ## 19. CREATE MISSION  `/missions/new`  
####   
#### Create a mission form with the following fields.  
####   
#### ### Mission details  
####   
#### - Mission Title  required.  
#### - Mission Reference  optional.  
#### - Description  optional.  
#### - Required Staff Count  required, integer greater than zero.  
#### - Required Skills  optional multi-select.  
#### - Scheduled Start  optional.  
#### - Scheduled End  optional.  
#### - Notes  optional.  
####   
#### Validate that the end time is not earlier than the start time.  
####   
#### ### Mission location  
####   
#### Fields:  
####   
#### - Country.  
#### - Postal Code.  
#### - City.  
#### - Region.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Validate Location.  
#### - Clear Location.  
####   
#### Resolve coordinates using the configured geocoding provider.  
####   
#### Do not permit distance-based search until the mission has valid coordinates.  
####   
#### ### Travel preferences  
####   
#### Field:  
####   
#### - Preferred Transport Mode.  
####   
#### Supported modes depend on the configured routing provider. Potential modes:  
####   
#### - Driving.  
#### - Walking.  
#### - Cycling.  
#### - Public Transport, only if a real provider supports it.  
####   
#### If public transport is unsupported, disable the option and explain why. Never simulate public transport routes using car-routing data.  
####   
#### ### Mission status  
####   
#### New missions begin as `draft` or `open`, according to the selected workflow.  
####   
#### ### Form buttons  
####   
#### - Save Draft.  
#### - Create Mission.  
#### - Cancel.  
####   
#### On successful creation:  
####   
#### - Persist the mission.  
#### - Record an activity event.  
#### - Redirect to the mission detail page.  
#### - Offer `Find Staff for This Mission`.  
####   
#### ## 20. MISSION DETAILS  `/missions/[id]`

#### Display:
