#### - Mission title and reference.  
#### - Description.  
#### - Location.  
#### - Map.  
#### - Scheduled time.  
#### - Required staff count.  
#### - Selected staff count.  
#### - Required skills.  
#### - Preferred transport mode.  
#### - Status.  
#### - Notes.  
#### - Activity history.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Edit Mission.  
#### - Find Staff.  
#### - View Selected Staff.  
#### - Change Status.  
#### - Cancel Mission.  
#### - Back to Missions.  
####   
#### Display a clear staffing indicator, for example, `3 of 5 staff selected`, based on real records.  
####   
#### Do not mark a mission fully staffed merely because the required number of people were selected if the business rules require confirmed assignments.  
####   
#### Distinguish selected, contacted, confirmed, declined and completed assignments.  
####   
#### ## 21. FIND STAFF FOR A MISSION  `/missions/[id]/staff`  
####   
#### This page is central to the product.  
####   
#### The manager must be able to evaluate potential staff members for a specific mission.  
####   
#### ### Search header  
####   
#### Display:  
####   
#### - Mission title.  
#### - Mission location.  
#### - Required staff count.  
#### - Currently selected staff count.  
#### - Mission status.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Back to Mission.  
#### - Edit Mission.  
#### - Refresh Results.  
####   
#### ### Filters  
####   
#### - Search radius: 5, 10, 20, 30 or 50 km.  
#### - Availability: Available Only, All Statuses.  
#### - Required Skills.  
#### - Transport Mode.  
#### - Maximum results, optional.  
####   
#### Default radius: 10 km.  
####   
#### Default availability filter: Available Only.  
####   
#### Never include `unknown` availability as available.  
####   
#### ### Search behavior  
####   
#### When the mission has valid coordinates:  
####   
#### 1. Query only staff belonging to the authenticated manager.  
#### 2. Exclude archived staff.  
#### 3. Exclude staff with missing or invalid coordinates.  
#### 4. Apply availability filters.  
#### 5. Apply skill filters.  
#### 6. Calculate geographic distance for candidate filtering.  
#### 7. Retrieve route estimates where supported.  
#### 8. Rank candidates using the configured ranking strategy.  
#### 9. Display results with clear distance and duration labels.  
#### 10. Allow the manager to select staff for the mission.  
####   
#### Do not require route calculation for every staff member before producing any results. Use a staged approach:  
####   
#### - First identify nearby candidates using geographic distance.  
#### - Then retrieve route estimates for a bounded set of candidates.  
#### - Show route calculation progress and partial results where appropriate.  
#### - Handle route provider limits gracefully.  
####   
#### Do not silently represent straight-line distance as road distance.  
####   
#### ### Candidate result cards or table  
####   
#### Display:  
####   
#### - Staff name.  
#### - Availability.  
#### - Skills.  
#### - Registered postal code.  
#### - City or region.  
#### - Straight-line distance.  
#### - Road distance, if available.  
#### - Estimated travel time.  
#### - Transport mode.  
#### - Route estimate status.  
#### - Location verification status.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - View Profile.  
#### - Call.  
#### - WhatsApp.  
#### - Compare.  
#### - Select for Mission.  
####   
#### If a route estimate is unavailable, display `Travel time unavailable` rather than inventing one.  
####   
#### ### Selection  
####   
#### When the manager selects a candidate:  
####   
#### - Verify ownership of both records.  
#### - Prevent duplicate assignments.  
#### - Persist the assignment.  
#### - Record an activity event.  
#### - Update mission staffing counts.  
#### - Update the UI after server confirmation.  
####   
#### Selection is not confirmation of attendance or acceptance.  
####   
#### Provide a way to:  
####   
#### - Remove a selected candidate.  
#### - Mark a candidate as contacted.  
#### - Mark an assignment as confirmed.  
#### - Mark an assignment as declined.  
#### - View all selected staff.  
####   
#### Use confirmation dialogs where appropriate.  
####   
#### ## 22. DISTANCE CALCULATION  
####   
#### Implement a dedicated, tested geospatial service.  
####   
#### For every candidate, distinguish these measurements.  
####   
#### ### A. Straight-line distance  
####   
#### Calculate the geographic distance between:  
####   
#### - The staff member's registered coordinates.  
#### - The mission's coordinates.  
####   
#### Use the Haversine formula or an appropriate geographic database function.  
####   
#### Return a distance in meters and format it for display.  
####   
#### Example display:  
####   
#### - `2.4 km straight-line distance`.  
####   
#### Use this measurement for initial candidate filtering where appropriate.  
####   
#### Never label straight-line distance as road distance.  
####   
#### ### B. Road distance  
####   
#### When a routing provider is configured and supports the selected transport mode, request a real route estimate.  
####   
#### Return:  
####   
#### - Route distance in meters.  
#### - Estimated duration in seconds.  
#### - Provider identifier.  
#### - Calculation timestamp.  
#### - Transport mode.  
#### - Estimate status.  
####   
#### Display a clear label:  
####   
#### - `Road distance`.  
#### - `Estimated travel time`.  
####   
#### Use the provider's returned route distance, not the Haversine result.  
####   
#### Do not calculate road distance by multiplying straight-line distance by an arbitrary factor.  
####   
#### ### C. Distance formatting  
####   
#### Format values sensibly:  
####   
#### - Meters for very short distances.  
#### - Kilometers for longer distances.  
#### - Consistent decimal precision.  
#### - No false precision.  
####   
#### If coordinates are missing or invalid, return an explicit unavailable result.  
####   
#### ### D. Geographic accuracy  
####   
#### Postal-code coordinates are approximate. Explain that distance and route estimates depend on the precision of the registered locations.  
####   
#### A route from a postal-code centroid is not necessarily the route from the person's exact home address.  
####   
#### Do not display exact-looking accuracy that the source data cannot justify.  
####   
#### ## 23. TRAVEL TIME ESTIMATION BY TRANSPORT MODE  
####   
#### The application must support transport-specific estimates where a reliable provider exists.  
####   
#### ### Driving  
####   
#### Use a real routing API configured for driving.  
####   
#### Display:  
####   
#### - Road distance.  
#### - Estimated driving time.  
#### - Provider and calculation status.  
#### - Time of last calculation.  
####   
#### Use live traffic-aware duration only if the provider supports it, the required option is configured and the response genuinely includes traffic-aware information.  
####   
#### Otherwise label the result `Estimated driving time` and do not claim that current traffic is included.  
####   
#### ### Walking  
####   
#### Use a walking route profile only when supported by the provider.  
####   
#### Do not estimate walking time using a driving route duration.  
####   
#### ### Cycling  
####   
#### Use a cycling route profile only when supported.  
####   
#### Do not show a cycling estimate when the provider cannot calculate cycling routes.  
####   
#### ### Public transport  
####   
#### Public transport often depends on schedules, routes and service availability.  
####   
#### Use a real public transport routing provider if configured.  
####   
#### If no such provider is configured:  
####   
#### - Disable or mark Public Transport as unavailable.  
#### - Explain that public transport estimates are not currently supported.  
#### - Do not reuse car travel times.  
#### - Do not present an arbitrary bus or train speed as a real route estimate.  
####   
#### ### Optional fallback using average speed  
####   
#### A configurable average-speed model may be used as a fallback only when the routing service cannot provide an estimate and the manager explicitly permits fallback estimates.  
####   
#### The fallback must:  
####   
#### - Be configurable by transport mode.  
#### - Be clearly labelled as an approximate fallback.  
#### - Never be presented as a real routed journey.  
#### - Never imply current traffic or transit schedules.  
#### - Be omitted when a reliable fallback cannot be justified.  
####   
#### Use road distance for driving fallback only if an actual road distance is available.  
####   
#### If only straight-line distance exists, label any calculation based on it as a rough approximation and do not treat it as an operationally reliable arrival estimate.  
####   
#### Do not silently substitute a fallback estimate for a real route result.  
####   
#### ### Transport selector  
####   
#### Provide a transport selector on the mission staff search page.  
####   
#### Changing the transport mode must:  
####   
#### 1. Preserve the mission and search filters.  
#### 2. Retrieve a new estimate using the selected mode.  
#### 3. Update candidate travel times.  
#### 4. Update the ranking when travel time is part of the ranking strategy.  
#### 5. Display an unsupported-mode message if necessary.  
#### 6. Handle API errors without losing the staff search results.  
####   
#### ### Estimate caching  
####   
#### Cache successful route estimates where appropriate.  
####   
#### Cache keys must include:  
####   
#### - Origin coordinates.  
#### - Destination coordinates.  
#### - Transport mode.  
#### - Relevant routing options.  
#### - Provider configuration where needed.  
####   
#### Set an appropriate expiry time.  
####   
#### Never reuse a driving estimate for walking, cycling or public transport.  
####   
#### Refresh estimates when locations change or when cached results expire.  
####   
#### Avoid excessive API calls by calculating routes only for relevant candidates and using bounded concurrency.  
####   
#### ## 24. CANDIDATE RANKING  
####   
#### The manager must be able to sort results by:  
####   
#### - Straight-line distance.  
#### - Road distance, where available.  
#### - Estimated travel time, where available.  
#### - Name.  
####   
#### Use the selected sorting method explicitly.  
####   
#### The default ranking should prioritize:  
####   
#### 1. Required availability.  
#### 2. Required skills.  
#### 3. Inclusion within the selected geographic radius.  
#### 4. Estimated travel time when a valid route estimate exists.  
#### 5. Distance as a secondary criterion.  
####   
#### Do not create a misleading single suitability score unless its formula is defined, implemented and explained.  
####   
#### If some candidates have route estimates and others do not, label the missing estimates clearly and do not silently treat missing values as zero.  
####   
#### Allow the manager to sort by distance independently from estimated travel time.  
####   
#### ## 25. MAP AND LIST SYNCHRONIZATION  
####   
#### Use Mapbox GL JS.  
####   
#### For the mission staff search page, display:  
####   
#### - Mission location.  
#### - Staff registered locations.  
#### - Selected staff markers.  
#### - Matching staff markers.  
#### - Marker clusters when needed.  
#### - Search radius.  
#### - Map controls.  
#### - Legend.  
#### - Result count.  
####   
#### Interactions:  
####   
#### - Clicking a marker highlights its staff card.  
#### - Clicking a card highlights its marker.  
#### - Selecting a candidate updates the selection state.  
#### - Clicking the mission marker displays mission details.  
#### - Cluster clicks zoom into the cluster.  
#### - Changing the radius refreshes the relevant search results.  
#### - Changing the transport mode refreshes route estimates, not staff coordinates.  
####   
#### When the provider supports route geometry, optionally display the selected candidate's route to the mission.  
####   
#### Display route lines only when actual route geometry was returned.  
####   
#### Do not draw fabricated routes between points.  
####   
#### If route geometry is unavailable, display distance and duration without a route line.  
####   
#### Keep the map responsive.  
####   
#### If Mapbox is not configured, preserve the directory, mission search and distance features that can function without the map, and show an informative configuration message.  
####   
#### ## 26. GENERAL FIND STAFF PAGE  `/find-staff`  
####   
#### Provide geographic staff search independent of a saved mission.  
####   
#### Fields:  
####   
#### - Country.  
#### - Search postal code or supported location.  
#### - Search radius.  
#### - Availability.  
#### - Required skills.  
#### - Transport mode where a destination and routing estimate are available.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Find Staff.  
#### - Clear Search.  
#### - List View.  
#### - Map View.  
#### - Export Results.  
#### - View Profile.  
#### - Call.  
#### - WhatsApp.  
####   
#### Calculate straight-line distance between the search location and staff coordinates.  
####   
#### When a routing destination and supported transport mode are available, calculate road distance and travel time.  
####   
#### If the manager searches only by city or postal code without creating a mission, treat that search point as the destination reference and label the results accordingly.  
####   
#### Never confuse this reference point with the staff member's live location.  
####   
#### ## 27. CSV IMPORT  `/staff/import`  
####   
#### Support `.csv` uploads for large staff lists.  
####   
#### Expected fields:  
####   
#### - `full_name`  
#### - `staff_code`  
#### - `phone`  
#### - `email`  
#### - `country_code`  
#### - `postal_code`  
#### - `city`  
#### - `region`  
#### - `skills`  
#### - `availability`  
#### - `notes`  
####   
#### Implement:  
####   
#### - Downloadable CSV template.  
#### - File selection.  
#### - Header mapping.  
#### - Preview.  
#### - Field validation.  
#### - Duplicate detection.  
#### - Row-level errors.  
#### - Import progress.  
#### - Bounded batches.  
#### - Location resolution.  
#### - Partial failure reporting.  
#### - Downloadable error report.  
#### - Import completion summary.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Download CSV Template.  
#### - Choose File.  
#### - Upload and Preview.  
#### - Confirm Import.  
#### - Download Error Report.  
#### - View Imported Staff.  
#### - Import Another File.  
#### - Cancel.  
####   
#### The application must handle at least 1,000 records in a realistic test environment.  
####   
#### Never trust an `owner_id` supplied by the CSV.  
####   
#### Do not create one unbounded geocoding request for every row.  
####   
#### Resolve unique postal codes in batches where supported.  
####   
#### Do not calculate route estimates for every imported staff member. Route calculations should occur when a manager searches or compares candidates for a specific destination.  
####   
#### ## 28. CSV EXPORT  
####   
#### Support exporting:

#### - All staff records the manager is authorized to access.  
#### - Filtered records.  
#### - Selected records.  
#### - Mission candidate results.  
#### - Selected mission staff.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Export All.  
#### - Export Filtered.  
#### - Export Selected.  
#### - Download CSV.  
####   
#### Escape CSV values correctly and protect against spreadsheet formula injection.  
####   
#### For mission candidate exports, clearly identify:  
####   
#### - Straight-line distance.  
#### - Road distance, if available.  
#### - Estimated travel time.  
#### - Transport mode.  
#### - Estimate status.  
#### - Calculation timestamp.  
####   
#### Do not silently truncate records.  
####   
#### ## 29. ACTIVITY HISTORY  `/activity`  
####   
#### Display actual events:  
####   
#### - Staff created or updated.  
#### - Availability changed.  
#### - Location resolved.  
#### - Location resolution failed.  
#### - Mission created or updated.  
#### - Candidate selected for a mission.  
#### - Assignment status changed.  
#### - CSV import completed.  
#### - CSV export completed.  
####   
#### Filters:  
####   
#### - Date range.  
#### - Event type.  
#### - Staff.  
#### - Mission.  
####   
#### Buttons:  
####   
#### - Refresh.  
#### - Clear Filters.  
#### - View Staff.  
#### - View Mission.  
#### - Previous.  
#### - Next.  
####   
#### Use server-side pagination and enforce ownership.  
####   
#### Do not allow ordinary users to modify audit events.  
####   
#### ## 30. NOTIFICATIONS  `/notifications`  
####   
#### Display persistent notifications for:  
####   
#### - Completed imports.  
#### - Import errors.

#### - Staffing progress.  
#### - Relevant assignment status changes.

#### ## 31. SETTINGS  
####   
#### ### General settings

#### Fields:

#### ## 32. LANDING PAGE  
####   
#### Create a polished landing page describing:  
####   
#### - Workforce management.  
#### - Geographic staff search.

#### - CSV import and export.

#### ## 33. API AND SERVICE ARCHITECTURE  
####   
#### Keep provider integrations isolated in dedicated modules.  
####   
#### Create clean interfaces for:  
####   
#### - Geocoding.  
#### - Route calculation.  
#### - Distance calculation.  
#### - Staff search.  
#### - Mission management.  
#### - CSV processing.  
#### - Activity logging.  
#### - Notifications.  
####   
#### Use server-side service functions or route handlers as appropriate.  
####   
#### All external responses must be validated before being saved or returned to the client.  
####   
#### Handle:  
####   
#### - Network failures.  
#### - Provider rate limits.  
#### - Timeouts.  
#### - Invalid coordinates.  
#### - Unsupported transport modes.  
#### - Missing API keys.  
#### - Empty route results.  
#### - Invalid geocoding results.  
####   
#### Use typed results and explicit error states.  
####   
#### Do not couple the entire UI directly to one routing provider.  
####   
#### Do not expose provider secrets in client-side bundles.  
####   
#### ## 34. PERFORMANCE REQUIREMENTS  
####   
#### Support approximately 1,000 staff records per manager without major usability problems.  
####   
#### Implement:  
####   
#### - Server-side pagination.  
#### - Indexed filtering.  
#### - Efficient database queries.  
#### - Debounced search.  
#### - Bounded batch processing.  
#### - Bounded route API concurrency.  
#### - Route estimate caching.  
#### - Map clustering.  
#### - Efficient dashboard aggregation.  
#### - Avoidance of unnecessary full-table downloads.  
#### - Avoidance of repeated geocoding.  
#### - Avoidance of repeated route calculations.  
####   
#### Do not calculate every possible staff-to-mission route on every page load.  
####   
#### Use a staged search strategy to reduce provider usage and response times.  
####   
#### Do not cache private records across different managers.  
####   
#### Measure performance where possible and report actual findings.  
####   
#### ## 35. PRIVACY AND DATA ACCURACY  
####   
#### Treat staff information as private personal data.  
####   
#### Implement:  
####   
#### - Data minimization.  
#### - Manager-only access.  
#### - Secure database policies.  
#### - Clear location status.  
#### - Clear distinction between registered location and live location.  
#### - Secure exports.  
#### - No public staff profiles.  
#### - No covert location collection.  
#### - No client access to internal staff coordinates.  
#### - Appropriate privacy notice.  
#### - Configurable data-retention considerations.  
####   
#### Postal-code coordinates are approximate. Travel estimates depend on the accuracy of the source locations, the routing provider, route availability and any traffic or transit information included by the provider.  
####   
#### Do not claim a travel estimate is guaranteed.  
####   
#### Do not claim real-time tracking exists.  
####

####   
#### ## 39. FINAL ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA  
####   
#### The MVP is complete only when the following have been implemented and verified as far as the environment permits.  
####   
#### ### Authentication and security  
####   
#### - Managers can authenticate.  
#### - Private routes are protected.  
#### - RLS isolates manager data.  
#### - Secrets are not exposed.  
#### - Ownership is enforced on the server.  
####   
#### ### Staff management  
####   
#### - Staff can be created, edited, viewed and archived.  
#### - Availability changes persist.  
#### - Records survive page refresh.  
#### - CSV import and export work.  
#### - Large imports report errors accurately.  
####   
#### ### Missions  
####   
#### - Managers can create and edit missions.  
#### - Mission locations can be resolved.  
#### - Required staff counts are validated.  
#### - Staff can be selected for missions.  
#### - Assignment status is stored accurately.  
####   
#### ### Geographic search  
####   
#### - Nearby staff are identified using valid coordinates.  
#### - Radius filtering works.  
#### - Straight-line distance is correctly calculated.  
#### - Road distance is distinguished from straight-line distance.  
#### - Results can be sorted by distance.  
#### - Missing coordinates are handled explicitly.  
####   
#### ### Travel time  
####   
#### - Supported transport modes call the appropriate routing service.  
#### - Driving estimates are not reused for walking, cycling or public transport.  
#### - Unsupported modes are clearly identified.  
#### - Fallback estimates, if implemented, are explicitly labelled.  
#### - Missing route estimates are never fabricated.  
#### - Estimates can be refreshed when necessary.  
####   
#### ### Map  
####   
#### - Mission and registered staff locations are displayed.  
#### - Map markers correspond to real records.  
#### - List and map selection are synchronized.  
#### - Route geometry is displayed only when returned by a real routing provider.  
#### - Missing configuration produces a useful message.  
####   
#### ### Tracking  
####   
#### - The manager can view registered staff locations and verification timestamps.  
#### - The application does not claim to provide live GPS tracking.  
#### - Clients do not have access to staff tracking.  
####   
#### ### Quality  
####   
#### - TypeScript checks pass.  
#### - Lint passes.  
#### - Relevant tests pass.  
#### - Production build succeeds.  
#### - Responsive layouts work.  
#### - No critical button is inert.  
#### - Errors are handled visibly.  
#### - External service limitations are documented.  
####   
#### ## 40. IMPLEMENTATION ORDER  
####

####   
#### - Inspect project.  
#### - Configure framework and dependencies.  
#### - Configure Supabase.  
#### - Create schema and migrations.  
#### - Implement authentication and RLS.  
#### - Build shared application layout.  
####   
#### ### Phase 2: Staff management

#### - Directory.  
#### - Search and filters.  
#### - Profiles.  
#### - Availability.  
#### - Archiving.  
####   
#### ### Phase 3: Location services  
####   
#### - Geocoding provider.  
#### - Postal-code normalization.  
#### - Coordinate resolution.  
#### - Location status.  
#### - Distance calculation.  
#### - Tests.  
####   
#### ### Phase 4: Missions  
####   
#### - Mission CRUD.  
#### - Mission location resolution.  
#### - Mission status.  
#### - Staff selection.  
#### - Assignment management.  
####   
#### ### Phase 5: Geographic search and travel estimates  
####   
#### - Radius search.  
#### - Straight-line distance.  
#### - Routing provider.  
#### - Transport-mode handling.  
#### - Travel-time estimates.  
#### - Route cache.  
#### - Candidate ranking.  
#### - Map and list synchronization.  
####   
#### ### Phase 6: CSV and operational features  
####   
#### - Import preview.  
#### - Bulk import.  
#### - Error reports.  
#### - Export.  
#### - Dashboard.  
#### - Activity history.  
#### - Notifications.  
#### - Settings.  
####   
#### ### Phase 7: Quality assurance  
####   
#### - Unit tests.  
#### - Integration tests.  
#### - Security tests.  
#### - End-to-end tests where possible.  
#### - Lint.  
#### - Type checks.  
#### - Production build.  
#### - Responsive review.  
#### - Fix detected errors.  
####   
#### Prioritize reliable functionality over decorative design work.  
####   
#### Do not build all the pages as static UI first and leave the backend for later.  
####   
#### Complete critical workflows end to end.  
####   
#### ## 41. TERMINAL WORKFLOW  
####   
#### After every significant phase:  
####   
#### 1. Inspect modified files.  
#### 2. Run relevant checks.  
#### 3. Review actual error output.  
#### 4. Diagnose the cause.  
#### 5. Fix the problem.  
#### 6. Rerun the failing check.  
#### 7. Verify that the fix did not break related functionality.  
####   
#### Do not delete user data or run destructive database operations without authorization.  
####   
#### Do not perform irreversible remote migrations without explaining their impact.  
####   
#### Do not conceal errors by disabling checks.  
####   
#### If external credentials are missing, distinguish configuration limitations from software defects.  
####   
#### Continue working until all feasible implementation and verification steps are complete.  
####   
#### ## 42. FINAL DELIVERY REPORT  
####   
#### At completion, report:  
####   
#### 1. Implemented features.  
#### 2. Main files and database migrations created or modified.  
#### 3. Authentication and database setup status.  
#### 4. Required environment variables.  
#### 5. Geocoding provider and configuration.  
#### 6. Routing provider and supported transport modes.  
#### 7. Whether distance is straight-line, road-based or both.  
#### 8. How travel time is calculated and what limitations apply.  
#### 9. Commands actually executed.  
#### 10. Actual test, lint, type-check and build results.  
#### 11. Features tested against real services.  
#### 12. Features verified only through mocks.  
#### 13. Remaining configuration requirements.  
#### 14. Known limitations.  
#### 15. Exact command to run the application.  
####   
#### Never claim that a service is fully functional if it was not configured or tested.  
####   
#### ## FINAL INSTRUCTION  
####   
#### Start by inspecting the existing terminal workspace and then implement StaffFlow directly.  
####   
#### Do not return another prompt, a tutorial or a list of suggestions instead of the application.  
####   
#### Build the actual code, database migrations, authentication, staff directory, mission management, location services, geographic search, distance calculations, travel-time estimation, interactive map, CSV workflows, dashboard, security policies, tests and documentation.  
####   
#### The central workflow must allow a manager to create a mission, specify its location, find suitable available staff, compare their distance and estimated journey time by supported transport mode, select candidates and manage the mission.  
####   
#### The MVP must provide registered-location visibility, not live GPS tracking.  
####   
#### **Implement, test, correct and verify the application before reporting completion. Clearly distinguish working functionality from anything that still requires configuration.**

````sql
<<<<<<< SEARCH
=======
-- StaffFlow MVP Database Schema
-- Provides workforce management, missions, location resolution, assignments, activity logs, and route cache.

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";

-- 1. Profiles Table (Managers)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.profiles (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  company_name TEXT,
  country_code VARCHAR(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'FR',
  timezone TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Europe/Paris',
  default_search_radius_km INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 20,
  default_transport_mode VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'driving',
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now())
);

ALTER TABLE public.profiles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Managers can view own profile"
  ON public.profiles FOR SELECT
  USING (auth.uid() = id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can update own profile"
  ON public.profiles FOR UPDATE
  USING (auth.uid() = id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can insert own profile"
  ON public.profiles FOR INSERT
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = id);

-- 2. Staff Table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.staff (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
  owner_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  staff_code TEXT,
  full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  phone TEXT,
  email TEXT,
  country_code VARCHAR(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'FR',
  postal_code TEXT NOT NULL,
  postal_code_normalized TEXT NOT NULL,
  city TEXT,
  region TEXT,
  latitude DOUBLE PRECISION,
  longitude DOUBLE PRECISION,
  location_status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending' CHECK (location_status IN ('pending', 'resolved', 'failed')),
  location_updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  availability_status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'unknown' CHECK (availability_status IN ('available', 'unavailable', 'unknown')),
  skills TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  notes TEXT,
  last_contacted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  archived_at TIMESTAMPTZ
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_staff_owner ON public.staff(owner_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_staff_availability ON public.staff(availability_status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_staff_postal_code ON public.staff(postal_code_normalized);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_staff_location ON public.staff(latitude, longitude);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_staff_archived ON public.staff(archived_at);

ALTER TABLE public.staff ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Managers can select own staff"
  ON public.staff FOR SELECT
  USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can insert own staff"
  ON public.staff FOR INSERT
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can update own staff"
  ON public.staff FOR UPDATE
  USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can delete own staff"
  ON public.staff FOR DELETE
  USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

-- 3. Missions Table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.missions (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
  owner_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  mission_reference TEXT,
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT,
  country_code VARCHAR(2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'FR',
  postal_code TEXT NOT NULL,
  city TEXT,
  region TEXT,
  latitude DOUBLE PRECISION,
  longitude DOUBLE PRECISION,
  location_status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending' CHECK (location_status IN ('pending', 'resolved', 'failed')),
  location_updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  required_skills TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  required_staff_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (required_staff_count > 0),
  scheduled_start_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  scheduled_end_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
  transport_mode VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'driving' CHECK (transport_mode IN ('driving', 'walking', 'cycling', 'transit')),
  status VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'open', 'staffing_in_progress', 'staffed', 'completed', 'cancelled')),
  notes TEXT,
  created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  archived_at TIMESTAMPTZ
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_missions_owner ON public.missions(owner_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_missions_status ON public.missions(status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_missions_location ON public.missions(latitude, longitude);

ALTER TABLE public.missions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Managers can select own missions"
  ON public.missions FOR SELECT
  USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can insert own missions"
  ON public.missions FOR INSERT
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can update own missions"
  ON public.missions FOR UPDATE
  USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

CREATE POLICY "Managers can delete own missions"
  ON public.missions FOR DELETE
  USING (auth.uid() = owner_id);

-- 4. Mission Staff Table (Assignments)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.mission_staff (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
  owner_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  mission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.missions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  staff_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.staff(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  assignment_status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'selected' CHECK (assignment_status IN ('selected', 'contacted', 'confirmed', 'declined', 'cancelled', 'completed')),
  selected_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT timezone('utc'::text, now()),
  notes TEXT,
  CONSTRAINT unique_mission_staff UNIQUE (mission_id, staff_id)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_mission_staff_mission ON public.mission_staff(mission_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_mission_staff_staff ON public.mission_staff(staff_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_mission_staff_owner ON public.mission_staff(owner_id);

ALTER TABLE public.mission_staff ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Managers can manage own mission assignments"
  ON public.mission_staff FOR ALL
  USING (auth.uid() = owner_id)
  WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = owner_id);

-- 5. Activity Logs Table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.activity_logs (
  id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
  owner_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,

  description TEXT NOT NULL,
  metadata JSONB DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,

);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_activity_owner ON public.activity_logs(owner_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_activity_created_at ON public.activity_logs(created_at DESC);

ALTER TABLE public.activity_logs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Managers can select own activity logs"
  ON public.activity_logs FOR SELECT

-- 6. Notifications Table
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.notifications (

import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx";
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge";

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs));
}

/**
 * Normalizes a postal code based on country code.
 */
export function normalizePostalCode(postalCode: string, countryCode = 'FR'): string {
  const cleaned = postalCode.trim().toUpperCase().replace(/[\s-]/g, '');
  if (countryCode === 'FR') {
    // Ensure 5 digits with leading zeros if 4 digits were given
    if (/^\d{4}$/.test(cleaned)) {
      return `0${cleaned}`;
    }
  }
  return cleaned;
}

/**
 * Formats a distance in kilometers or meters.
 */
export function formatDistance(distanceKm: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (distanceKm == null || isNaN(distanceKm)) {
    return 'Distance unavailable';
  }
  if (distanceKm < 1) {
    const meters = Math.round(distanceKm * 1000);
    return `${meters} m`;
  }
  return `${distanceKm.toFixed(1)} km`;
}

/**
 * Formats duration seconds into readable hours and minutes.
 */
export function formatDuration(seconds: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (seconds == null || isNaN(seconds) || seconds <= 0) {
    return 'Travel time unavailable';
  }
  const mins = Math.round(seconds / 60);
  if (mins < 60) {
    return `${mins} min`;
  }
  const hours = Math.floor(mins / 60);
  const remainingMins = mins % 60;
  return remainingMins > 0 ? `${hours}h ${remainingMins}m` : `${hours}h`;
}

/**
 * Normalizes phone numbers to standard international format (E.164) where possible
 */
export function normalizePhone(phone: string | null | undefined, defaultCountry = 'FR'): string {
  if (!phone) return '';
  const trimmed = phone.trim();
  const digits = trimmed.replace(/\D/g, '');
  
  if (defaultCountry === 'FR') {
    if (trimmed.startsWith('+')) {
      return `+${digits}`;
    }
    if (digits.startsWith('33')) {
      return `+${digits}`;
    }
    if (digits.startsWith('0') && digits.length === 10) {
      return `+33${digits.substring(1)}`;
    }
  }
  return trimmed.startsWith('+') ? `+${digits}` : trimmed;
}

/**
 * Formats date into readable string
 */
export function formatDate(dateString: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!dateString) return '—';
  try {
    const date = new Date(dateString);
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
      month: 'short',
      day: 'numeric',
      year: 'numeric',
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
    }).format(date);
  } catch {
    return dateString;
  }
}

/**
 * Geospatial distance calculation using the Haversine formula.
 * Computes great-circle distance between two coordinate pairs on Earth.
 */

const EARTH_RADIUS_KM = 6371.0088;

export interface Coordinates {
  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
}

/**
 * Converts degrees to radians.
 */
function toRadians(degrees: number): number {
  return (degrees * Math.PI) / 180;
}

/**
 * Validates coordinate pair.
 */
export function isValidCoordinates(lat: unknown, lon: unknown): boolean {
  if (typeof lat !== 'number' || typeof lon !== 'number') {
    return false;
  }
  if (isNaN(lat) || isNaN(lon)) {
    return false;
  }
  return lat >= -90 && lat <= 90 && lon >= -180 && lon <= 180;
}

/**
 * Calculates straight-line distance in kilometers between two points.
 */
export function calculateStraightLineDistanceKm(
  point1: Coordinates,
  point2: Coordinates
): number {
  if (
    !isValidCoordinates(point1.latitude, point1.longitude) ||
    !isValidCoordinates(point2.latitude, point2.longitude)
  ) {
    throw new Error('Invalid coordinates provided to Haversine calculation');
  }

  const dLat = toRadians(point2.latitude - point1.latitude);
  const dLon = toRadians(point2.longitude - point1.longitude);

  const lat1 = toRadians(point1.latitude);
  const lat2 = toRadians(point2.latitude);

  const a =
    Math.sin(dLat / 2) * Math.sin(dLat / 2) +
    Math.cos(lat1) * Math.cos(lat2) * Math.sin(dLon / 2) * Math.sin(dLon / 2);

  const c = 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a));

  return EARTH_RADIUS_KM * c;
}

/**
 * Returns whether a point is within a given radius (km) of a center point.
 */
export function isWithinRadius(
  center: Coordinates,
  point: Coordinates,
  radiusKm: number
): boolean {
  try {
    const dist = calculateStraightLineDistanceKm(center, point);
    return dist <= radiusKm;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

import { normalizePostalCode } from '../utils';

export interface GeocodingResult {
  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
  city?: string;
  region?: string;
  displayName: string;
}

export interface GeocodingProvider {
  resolvePostalCode(postalCode: string, countryCode: string): Promise<GeocodingResult | null>;
}

/**
 * OpenStreetMap Nominatim Provider (Default, requires no API key).
 * Respects usage policy with distinct User-Agent and query parameters.
 */
export class NominatimGeocodingProvider implements GeocodingProvider {
  async resolvePostalCode(postalCode: string, countryCode = 'FR'): Promise<GeocodingResult | null> {
    const normalized = normalizePostalCode(postalCode, countryCode);
    const countryParam = countryCode.toLowerCase();
    const url = `https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?postalcode=${encodeURIComponent(
      normalized
    )}&country=${encodeURIComponent(countryParam)}&format=json&addressdetails=1&limit=1`;

    try {
      const response = await fetch(url, {
        headers: {
          'User-Agent': 'StaffFlow-SaaS-Workforce-MVP/1.0 (contact@staffflow.local)',
          'Accept-Language': 'en',
        },
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        return null;
      }

      const data = await response.json();
      if (!Array.isArray(data) || data.length === 0) {
        return null;
      }

      const first = data[0];
      const lat = parseFloat(first.lat);
      const lon = parseFloat(first.lon);

      if (isNaN(lat) || isNaN(lon)) {
        return null;
      }

      const address = first.address || {};
      const city =
        address.city ||
        address.town ||
        address.village ||
        address.municipality ||
        address.county;
      const region = address.state || address.region;

      return {
        latitude: lat,
        longitude: lon,
        city: city || undefined,
        region: region || undefined,
        displayName: first.display_name,
      };
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Nominatim geocoding error:', err);
      return null;
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Mapbox Geocoding Provider (Optional if token provided)
 */
export class MapboxGeocodingProvider implements GeocodingProvider {
  constructor(private token: string) {}

  async resolvePostalCode(postalCode: string, countryCode = 'FR'): Promise<GeocodingResult | null> {
    const normalized = normalizePostalCode(postalCode, countryCode);
    const url = `https://api.mapbox.com/geocoding/v5/mapbox.places/${encodeURIComponent(
      normalized
    )}.json?types=postcode&country=${encodeURIComponent(countryCode.toLowerCase())}&access_token=${this.token}&limit=1`;

    try {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      if (!response.ok) return null;
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!data.features || data.features.length === 0) return null;

      const feature = data.features[0];
      const [lon, lat] = feature.center;
      let city: string | undefined;
      let region: string | undefined;

      if (Array.isArray(feature.context)) {
        for (const ctx of feature.context) {
          if (ctx.id.startsWith('place')) city = ctx.text;
          if (ctx.id.startsWith('region')) region = ctx.text;
        }
      }

      return {
        latitude: lat,
        longitude: lon,
        city,
        region,
        displayName: feature.place_name,
      };
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Mapbox geocoding error:', err);
      return null;
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Factory to get configured geocoding provider
 */
export function getGeocodingProvider(): GeocodingProvider {
  const mapboxToken = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_MAPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN;
  const providerType = process.env.GEOCODING_PROVIDER;

  if (providerType === 'mapbox' && mapboxToken) {
    return new MapboxGeocodingProvider(mapboxToken);
  }
  return new NominatimGeocodingProvider();
}

import { Coordinates } from './haversine';
import { RouteEstimate, TransportMode } from '../types';

export interface RoutingProvider {
  calculateRoute(
    origin: Coordinates,
    destination: Coordinates,
    mode: TransportMode
  ): Promise<RouteEstimate | null>;
  isModeSupported(mode: TransportMode): boolean;
}

/**
 * Open Source Routing Machine (OSRM) provider (Free open demo server).
 * Supports driving, walking, cycling. Does not support public transit.
 */
export class OSRMRoutingProvider implements RoutingProvider {
  isModeSupported(mode: TransportMode): boolean {
    return mode === 'driving' || mode === 'walking' || mode === 'cycling';
  }

  async calculateRoute(
    origin: Coordinates,
    destination: Coordinates,
    mode: TransportMode
  ): Promise<RouteEstimate | null> {
    if (!this.isModeSupported(mode)) {
      return null;
    }

    // OSRM profiles
    const profile = mode === 'cycling' ? 'bike' : mode === 'walking' ? 'foot' : 'driving';
    const coords = `${origin.longitude},${origin.latitude};${destination.longitude},${destination.latitude}`;
    const url = `https://router.project-osrm.org/route/v1/${profile}/${coords}?overview=simplified&geometries=geojson`;

    try {
      const response = await fetch(url, {
        headers: {
          'User-Agent': 'StaffFlow-SaaS-Workforce-MVP/1.0',
        },
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        return null;
      }

      const data = await response.json();
      if (data.code !== 'Ok' || !data.routes || data.routes.length === 0) {
        return null;
      }

      const route = data.routes[0];
      return {
        distanceMeters: route.distance,
        durationSeconds: route.duration,
        provider: 'OSRM',
        transportMode: mode,
        isFallback: false,
        geometry: route.geometry?.coordinates,
      };
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('OSRM route calculation error:', err);
      return null;
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Mapbox Directions API Provider (Optional if token provided)
 */
export class MapboxRoutingProvider implements RoutingProvider {
  constructor(private token: string) {}

  isModeSupported(mode: TransportMode): boolean {
    // Mapbox directions supports driving, walking, cycling
    return mode === 'driving' || mode === 'walking' || mode === 'cycling';
  }

  async calculateRoute(
    origin: Coordinates,
    destination: Coordinates,
    mode: TransportMode
  ): Promise<RouteEstimate | null> {
    if (!this.isModeSupported(mode)) return null;

    const profile =
      mode === 'cycling'
        ? 'mapbox/cycling'
        : mode === 'walking'
        ? 'mapbox/walking'
        : 'mapbox/driving';

    const coords = `${origin.longitude},${origin.latitude};${destination.longitude},${destination.latitude}`;
    const url = `https://api.mapbox.com/directions/v5/${profile}/${coords}?geometries=geojson&access_token=${this.token}`;

    try {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      if (!response.ok) return null;

      const data = await response.json();
      if (!data.routes || data.routes.length === 0) return null;

      const route = data.routes[0];
      return {
        distanceMeters: route.distance,
        durationSeconds: route.duration,
        provider: 'Mapbox Directions',
        transportMode: mode,
        isFallback: false,
        geometry: route.geometry?.coordinates,
      };
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Mapbox routing error:', err);
      return null;
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Fallback calculation helper when routing APIs are offline or disabled.
 * Uses straight-line distance and realistic speed assumptions.
 * Explicitly labeled as fallback per specifications.
 */
export function calculateFallbackEstimate(
  straightLineDistanceKm: number,
  mode: TransportMode
): RouteEstimate {
  // Road distance factor ~1.3 times straight-line distance
  const estimatedRoadKm = straightLineDistanceKm * 1.3;
  const distanceMeters = Math.round(estimatedRoadKm * 1000);

  // Speed in km/h
  const speeds: Record<TransportMode, number> = {
    driving: 45, // Mixed urban / extra-urban
    cycling: 15,
    walking: 4.5,
    transit: 25,
  };

  const speedKmh = speeds[mode] || 45;
  const hours = estimatedRoadKm / speedKmh;
  const durationSeconds = Math.round(hours * 3600);

  return {
    distanceMeters,
    durationSeconds,
    provider: 'Approximation Model',
    transportMode: mode,
    isFallback: true,
  };
}

/**
 * Returns active routing provider
 */
export function getRoutingProvider(): RoutingProvider {
  const mapboxToken = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_MAPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN;
  const providerType = process.env.ROUTING_PROVIDER;

  if (providerType === 'mapbox' && mapboxToken) {
    return new MapboxRoutingProvider(mapboxToken);
  }
  return new OSRMRoutingProvider();
}

import { createServerClient } from '@supabase/ssr';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';

export async function createClient() {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();

  const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL || '';
  const supabaseAnonKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || '';

  return createServerClient(supabaseUrl, supabaseAnonKey, {
    cookies: {
      getAll() {
        return cookieStore.getAll();
      },
      setAll(cookiesToSet) {
        try {
          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) =>
            cookieStore.set(name, value, options)
          );
        } catch {
          // The `setAll` method was called from a Server Component.
          // This can be ignored if you have middleware refreshing user sessions.
        }
      },
    },
  });
}

/**
 * In-memory fallback and mock persistent store for local offline development
 * and environments where remote Supabase connection credentials have not yet been placed.
 * Ensures the app functions reliably out of the box with zero setup.
 */

// Persistent Global In-Memory Store
class MockDataStore {
  staff: Staff[] = [...initialStaff];
  missions: Mission[] = [...initialMissions];
  missionStaff: MissionStaff[] = [...initialMissionStaff];
  activityLogs: ActivityLog[] = [...initialActivityLogs];
  notifications: NotificationItem[] = [...initialNotifications];
  profile: Profile = { ...mockProfile };

  // Staff operations
  getStaff(ownerId: string): Staff[] {
    return this.staff.filter((s) => s.owner_id === ownerId && !s.archived_at);
  }

  getStaffById(id: string, ownerId: string): Staff | undefined {
    return this.staff.find((s) => s.id === id && s.owner_id === ownerId && !s.archived_at);
  }

  createStaff(data: Omit<Staff, 'id' | 'created_at' | 'updated_at' | 'archived_at'>): Staff {
    const newStaff: Staff = {
      ...data,
      id: `staff-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      archived_at: null,
    };
    this.staff.unshift(newStaff);
    return newStaff;
  }

  updateStaff(id: string, ownerId: string, updates: Partial<Staff>): Staff | null {
    const index = this.staff.findIndex((s) => s.id === id && s.owner_id === ownerId);
    if (index === -1) return null;
    this.staff[index] = {
      ...this.staff[index],
      ...updates,
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    return this.staff[index];
  }

  archiveStaff(id: string, ownerId: string): boolean {
    const staff = this.getStaffById(id, ownerId);
    if (!staff) return false;
    staff.archived_at = new Date().toISOString();
    return true;
  }

  // Mission operations
  getMissions(ownerId: string): Mission[] {
    return this.missions.filter((m) => m.owner_id === ownerId && !m.archived_at);
  }

  getMissionById(id: string, ownerId: string): Mission | undefined {
    return this.missions.find((m) => m.id === id && m.owner_id === ownerId && !m.archived_at);
  }

  createMission(data: Omit<Mission, 'id' | 'created_at' | 'updated_at' | 'archived_at'>): Mission {
    const newMission: Mission = {
      ...data,
      id: `msn-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      archived_at: null,
    };
    this.missions.unshift(newMission);
    return newMission;
  }

  updateMission(id: string, ownerId: string, updates: Partial<Mission>): Mission | null {
    const index = this.missions.findIndex((m) => m.id === id && m.owner_id === ownerId);
    if (index === -1) return null;
    this.missions[index] = {
      ...this.missions[index],
      ...updates,
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    return this.missions[index];
  }

  archiveMission(id: string, ownerId: string): boolean {
    const msn = this.getMissionById(id, ownerId);
    if (!msn) return false;
    msn.archived_at = new Date().toISOString();
    return true;
  }

  // Assignments
  getMissionAssignments(missionId: string, ownerId: string): MissionStaff[] {
    return this.missionStaff
      .filter((ms) => ms.mission_id === missionId && ms.owner_id === ownerId)
      .map((ms) => ({
        ...ms,
        staff: this.staff.find((s) => s.id === ms.staff_id),
      }));
  }

  assignStaffToMission(
    ownerId: string,
    missionId: string,
    staffId: string,
    status: MissionStaff['assignment_status'] = 'selected'
  ): MissionStaff {
    const existing = this.missionStaff.find(
      (ms) => ms.mission_id === missionId && ms.staff_id === staffId && ms.owner_id === ownerId
    );
    if (existing) {
      existing.assignment_status = status;
      existing.updated_at = new Date().toISOString();
      return existing;
    }
    const newAssignment: MissionStaff = {
      id: `mst-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`,
      owner_id: ownerId,
      mission_id: missionId,
      staff_id: staffId,
      assignment_status: status,
      selected_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      notes: null,
    };
    this.missionStaff.push(newAssignment);
    return newAssignment;
  }

  updateAssignmentStatus(
    id: string,
    ownerId: string,
    status: MissionStaff['assignment_status']
  ): boolean {
    const assignment = this.missionStaff.find((ms) => ms.id === id && ms.owner_id === ownerId);
    if (!assignment) return false;
    assignment.assignment_status = status;
    assignment.updated_at = new Date().toISOString();
    return true;
  }

  removeAssignment(missionId: string, staffId: string, ownerId: string): boolean {
    const idx = this.missionStaff.findIndex(
      (ms) => ms.mission_id === missionId && ms.staff_id === staffId && ms.owner_id === ownerId
    );
    if (idx === -1) return false;
    this.missionStaff.splice(idx, 1);
    return true;
  }

  // Activity & Notifications
  logActivity(
    ownerId: string,
    eventType: string,
    description: string,
    staffId?: string,
    missionId?: string,
    metadata?: Record<string, unknown>
  ): ActivityLog {
    const log: ActivityLog = {
      id: `act-${Date.now()}`,
      owner_id: ownerId,
      staff_id: staffId || null,
      mission_id: missionId || null,
      event_type: eventType,
      description,
      metadata: metadata || null,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    this.activityLogs.unshift(log);
    return log;
  }

  getActivityLogs(ownerId: string): ActivityLog[] {
    return this.activityLogs
      .filter((a) => a.owner_id === ownerId)
      .map((a) => ({
        ...a,
        staff: a.staff_id ? this.staff.find((s) => s.id === a.staff_id) : null,
        mission: a.mission_id ? this.missions.find((m) => m.id === a.mission_id) : null,
      }));
  }

  getNotifications(ownerId: string): NotificationItem[] {
    return this.notifications.filter((n) => n.owner_id === ownerId);
  }

  markNotificationAsRead(id: string, ownerId: string): boolean {
    const n = this.notifications.find((item) => item.id === id && item.owner_id === ownerId);
    if (!n) return false;
    n.read_at = new Date().toISOString();
    return true;
  }

  markAllNotificationsAsRead(ownerId: string): void {
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    this.notifications.forEach((n) => {
      if (n.owner_id === ownerId && !n.read_at) {
        n.read_at = now;
      }
    });
  }
}

// Global singleton
const globalForStore = globalThis as unknown as { mockStore?: MockDataStore };
export const mockStore = globalForStore.mockStore ?? new MockDataStore();
if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') globalForStore.mockStore = mockStore;

Now let's create the repository layer in `lib/db/repository.ts` which unifies access between Supabase (when environment variables are configured) and the mock store fallback.

lib/db/repository.ts
````typescript

import { Staff, Mission, MissionStaff, ActivityLog, NotificationItem, Profile } from '../types';
import { mockStore, mockProfile } from './mock-store';
import { createClient } from '../supabase/server';

export const DEMO_MANAGER_ID = 'usr_manager_001';

export function isSupabaseConfigured(): boolean {
  return Boolean(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL && process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY);
}

export async function getCurrentUserOwnerId(): Promise<string> {
  if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
    try {
      const supabase = await createClient();
      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      if (user?.id) {
        return user.id;
      }
    } catch {
      // fallback to demo manager
    }
  }
  return DEMO_MANAGER_ID;
}

export const repository = {
  // Profiles
  async getProfile(ownerId: string): Promise<Profile> {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase = await createClient();
        const { data, error } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('*').eq('id', ownerId).single();
        if (!error && data) return data as Profile;
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error fetching profile from Supabase:', e);
      }
    }
    return { ...mockStore.profile, id: ownerId };
  },

  async updateProfile(ownerId: string, updates: Partial<Profile>): Promise<Profile> {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase = await createClient();
        const { data, error } = await supabase
          .from('profiles')
          .update(updates)
          .eq('id', ownerId)
          .select()
          .single();
        if (!error && data) return data as Profile;
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error updating profile in Supabase:', e);
      }
    }
    mockStore.profile = { ...mockStore.profile, ...updates };
    return mockStore.profile;
  },

  // Staff
  async getStaffList(ownerId: string): Promise<Staff[]> {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase = await createClient();
        const { data, error } = await supabase
          .from('staff')
          .select('*')
          .eq('owner_id', ownerId)
          .is('archived_at', null)
          .order('created_at', { ascending: false });
        if (!error && data) return data as Staff[];
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error fetching staff list from Supabase:', e);
      }
    }
    return mockStore.getStaff(ownerId);
  },

  async getStaffById(id: string, ownerId: string): Promise<Staff | null> {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase = await createClient();
        const { data, error } = await supabase
          .from('staff')
          .select('*')
          .eq('id', id)
          .eq('owner_id', ownerId)
          .is('archived_at', null)
          .single();
        if (!error && data) return data as Staff;
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error fetching staff by id from Supabase:', e);
      }
    }
    return mockStore.getStaffById(id, ownerId) || null;
  },

  async createStaff(data: Omit<Staff, 'id' | 'created_at' | 'updated_at' | 'archived_at'>): Promise<Staff> {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase = await createClient();
        const { data: created, error } = await supabase.from('staff').insert([data]).select().single();
        if (!error && created) {
          await this.logActivity(
            data.owner_id,
            'staff_created',
            `Created staff record: ${data.full_name}`,
            created.id
          );
          return created as Staff;
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error creating staff in Supabase:', e);
      }
    }
    const created = mockStore.createStaff(data);
    mockStore.logActivity(
      data.owner_id,
      'staff_created',
      `Created staff record: ${data.full_name}`,
      created.id
    );
    return created;
  },

  async updateStaff(id: string, ownerId: string, updates: Partial<Staff>): Promise<Staff | null> {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase = await createClient();
        const { data, error } = await supabase
          .from('staff')
          .update(updates)
          .eq('id', id)
          .eq('owner_id', ownerId)
          .select()
          .single();
        if (!error && data) {
          await this.logActivity(
            ownerId,
            'staff_updated',
            `Updated staff record: ${data.full_name}`,
            id
          );
          return data as Staff;
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error updating staff in Supabase:', e);
      }
    }
    const updated = mockStore.updateStaff(id, ownerId, updates);
    if (updated) {
      mockStore.logActivity(
        ownerId,
        'staff_updated',
        `Updated staff record: ${updated.full_name}`,
        id
      );
    }
    return updated;
  },

  async archiveStaff(id: string, ownerId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase = await createClient();
        const { error } = await supabase
          .from('staff')
          .update({ archived_at: new Date().toISOString() })
          .eq('id', id)
          .eq('owner_id', ownerId);
        if (!error) {
          await this.logActivity(ownerId, 'staff_archived', `Archived staff id: ${id}`, id);
          return true;
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error archiving staff in Supabase:', e);
      }
    }
    const res = mockStore.archiveStaff(id, ownerId);
    if (res) {
      mockStore.logActivity(ownerId, 'staff_archived', `Archived staff id: ${id}`, id);
    }
    return res;
  },

  // Missions
  async getMissionsList(ownerId: string): Promise<Mission[]> {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase = await createClient();
        const { data, error } = await supabase
          .from('missions')
          .select('*')
          .eq('owner_id', ownerId)
          .is('archived_at', null)
          .order('created_at', { ascending: false });
        if (!error && data) return data as Mission[];
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error fetching missions from Supabase:', e);
      }
    }
    return mockStore.getMissions(ownerId);
  },

  async getMissionById(id: string, ownerId: string): Promise<Mission | null> {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase = await createClient();
        const { data, error } = await supabase
          .from('missions')
          .select('*')
          .eq('id', id)
          .eq('owner_id', ownerId)
          .is('archived_at', null)
          .single();
        if (!error && data) return data as Mission;
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error fetching mission by id from Supabase:', e);
      }
    }
    return mockStore.getMissionById(id, ownerId) || null;
  },

  async createMission(data: Omit<Mission, 'id' | 'created_at' | 'updated_at' | 'archived_at'>): Promise<Mission> {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase = await createClient();
        const { data: created, error } = await supabase.from('missions').insert([data]).select().single();
        if (!error && created) {
          await this.logActivity(
            data.owner_id,
            'mission_created',
            `Created mission: ${data.title}`,
            undefined,
            created.id
          );
          return created as Mission;
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error creating mission in Supabase:', e);
      }
    }
    const created = mockStore.createMission(data);
    mockStore.logActivity(
      data.owner_id,
      'mission_created',
      `Created mission: ${data.title}`,
      undefined,
      created.id
    );
    return created;
  },

  async updateMission(id: string, ownerId: string, updates: Partial<Mission>): Promise<Mission | null> {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase = await createClient();
        const { data, error } = await supabase
          .from('missions')
          .update(updates)
          .eq('id', id)
          .eq('owner_id', ownerId)
          .select()
          .single();
        if (!error && data) {
          await this.logActivity(
            ownerId,
            'mission_updated',
            `Updated mission: ${data.title}`,
            undefined,
            id
          );
          return data as Mission;
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error updating mission in Supabase:', e);
      }
    }
    const updated = mockStore.updateMission(id, ownerId, updates);
    if (updated) {
      mockStore.logActivity(
        ownerId,
        'mission_updated',
        `Updated mission: ${updated.title}`,
        undefined,
        id
      );
    }
    return updated;
  },

  // Mission Staff Assignments
  async getMissionAssignments(missionId: string, ownerId: string): Promise<MissionStaff[]> {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase = await createClient();
        const { data, error } = await supabase
          .from('mission_staff')
          .select('*, staff(*)')
          .eq('mission_id', missionId)
          .eq('owner_id', ownerId);
        if (!error && data) return data as MissionStaff[];
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error fetching assignments from Supabase:', e);
      }
    }
    return mockStore.getMissionAssignments(missionId, ownerId);
  },

  async assignStaffToMission(
    ownerId: string,
    missionId: string,
    staffId: string,
    status: MissionStaff['assignment_status'] = 'selected'
  ): Promise<MissionStaff> {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase = await createClient();
        const { data, error } = await supabase
          .from('mission_staff')
          .upsert({
            owner_id: ownerId,
            mission_id: missionId,
            staff_id: staffId,
            assignment_status: status,
            updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
          })
          .select()
          .single();
        if (!error && data) {
          await this.logActivity(
            ownerId,
            'staff_assigned',
            `Assigned staff member to mission`,
            staffId,
            missionId
          );
          return data as MissionStaff;
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error assigning staff in Supabase:', e);
      }
    }
    const res = mockStore.assignStaffToMission(ownerId, missionId, staffId, status);
    mockStore.logActivity(
      ownerId,
      'staff_assigned',
      `Assigned staff member to mission`,
      staffId,
      missionId
    );
    return res;
  },

  async updateAssignmentStatus(
    id: string,
    ownerId: string,
    status: MissionStaff['assignment_status']
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase = await createClient();
        const { error } = await supabase
          .from('mission_staff')
          .update({ assignment_status: status, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
          .eq('id', id)
          .eq('owner_id', ownerId);
        if (!error) return true;
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error updating assignment in Supabase:', e);
      }
    }
    return mockStore.updateAssignmentStatus(id, ownerId, status);
  },

  async removeAssignment(missionId: string, staffId: string, ownerId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
      try {
        const supabase = await createClient();
        const { error } = await supabase
          .from('mission_staff')
          .delete()
          .eq('mission_id', missionId)
          .eq('staff_id', staffId)
          .eq('owner_id', ownerId);
        if (!error) return true;
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Error removing assignment from Supabase:', e);
      }
    }
    return mockStore.removeAssignment(missionId, staffId, ownerId);
  },

          .insert([

              description
